The burden of Dumah. He calleth to me out of Seir, Watchman, what of the night? Watchman, what of the night? The watchman said, The morning cometh, and also the night: if ye will enquire, enquire ye: return, come.

			Isaiah 21:11-12





CHAPTER 1




Prayer Concerning Why You Were Created and Against Voids and Emptiness in Your Life (Prayers from Genesis 1); Prayer for the Holy Spirit & Prayer for the Nations




		 			In the beginning God created the heaven and the earth.

			And the earth was without form, and void; and darkness was upon the face of the deep. And the Spirit of God moved upon the face of the waters.

			And God said, Let there be light: and there was light.

			And God saw the light, that it was good: and God divided the light from the darkness.

			Genesis 1:1-4

		 			Prayer Concerning Why You Were Created

			In the beginning God created the heaven and the earth.

			Genesis 1:1

			1. 	Pray and tell God to show you your purpose and the reason why He created you. You exist for the pleasure of God.

			Thou art worthy, O Lord, to receive glory and honour and power: for thou hast created all things, and for thy pleasure they are and were created.

			Revelation 4:11

			2.	Pray that Glory, Honour and Power will be ascribed to your life.

			These 3 things must come to God because He created you.

			a.	Glory

			b.	Honour

			c.	Power

			If you pray for revelation, or spiritual things, God will bring it to pass. Glory means beauty. Honour is the opposite of shame; it is to give respect to a person who has chosen you. Power is attributed to God as a result of a great change in your life.

			3. 	Pray that your life will be called beautiful. Another word that will be associated with your life is “beautiful”.

			He hath made every thing beautiful in his time: also he hath set the world in their heart, so that no man can find out the work that God maketh from the beginning to the end.

			Ecclesiastes 3:11

			God created you beautiful. Whatever it is that wants to “scratch out” your beauty will be dealt with by God. If you are patient there will be beauty in your life.

			4. 	Pray and declare over your life that you are fearfully, wonderfully and marvellously made.

			I will praise thee; for I am fearfully and wonderfully made: Marvellous are thy works; and that my soul knoweth right well.

			Psalm 139:14

			 These words are being mentioned in relation to your life!

			5. 	Now give thanks for these seven words (Glory, Honour, Power, Beautiful, Fearful, Wonderful and Marvellous) being associated with your life.

			Prayer Against Any Form of Void and Emptiness in Your Life

			And the earth was without form, and void; and darkness was upon the face of the deep. And the Spirit of God moved upon the face of the waters.

			Genesis 1:2

			Tôhû and Bôhû

			Tôhû and Bôhû are Hebrew words which mean without form and void. They also mean emptiness or an undisguisable ruin.

			I beheld the earth, and, lo, it was without form, and void; and the heavens, and they had no light. I beheld the mountains, and, lo, they trembled, and all the hills moved lightly. I beheld, and, lo, there was no man, and all the birds of the heavens were fled. I beheld, and, lo, the fruitful place was a wilderness, and all the cities thereof were broken down at the presence of the LORD , and by his fierce anger.

			Jeremiah 4:23-26

			You may be created beautifully and wonderfully and yet God can get angry with you. (Most people believe it was between Genesis chapters 1 and 2 - during the Creation - that the devil fell.)

			1. 	Pray that all forms of Tôhû and Bôhû are going out of your life.

			For it is the day of the LORD ‘s vengeance, and the year of recompences for the controversy of Zion. And the streams thereof shall be turned into pitch, and the dust thereof into brimstone, and the land thereof shall become burning pitch. It shall not be quenched night nor day; the smoke thereof shall go up for ever: from generation to generation it shall lie waste; none shall pass through it for ever and ever. But the cormorant and the bittern shall possess it; the owl also and the raven shall dwell in it: and he shall stretch out upon it the line of confusion, and the stones of emptiness.

			Isaiah 34:8-11

			2. 	Come before the Lord for restoration and restitution. Pray for restoration from every darkness.

			Chôshek is a Hebrew word meaning darkness. Darkness was part of the main problems in the world. Anywhere you see the word “darkness”, it is associated with judgement or sin.

			 And this is the condemnation, that light is come into the world, and men loved darkness rather than light, because their deeds were evil.

			John 3:19

			3. 	Pray that whatever is associated with these three words (Tôhû, Bôhû and Chôshek ) in your life are restored.

			4. 	Pray for revelation.

			The thief cometh not, but for to steal, and to kill, and to destroy: I am come that they might have life, and that they might have it more abundantly.

			John 10:10

			When the devil has access to a human being he begins to influence the person’s thoughts and ideas. When the devil is successful, the devil in spirit-form becomes a person. So when satan came to Adam and Eve, they suddenly became ashamed.

			5. 	Pray for revelation about every agent leading you to Tôhû and Bôhû. It is banished from your life.

			6. 	Cut off every agent of emptiness in your life. Every agent of Tôhû and Bôhû is cut off.

			Prayer for the Holy Spirit

			Only by pride cometh contention: but with the well advised is wisdom.

			Proverbs 13:10

			You can’t easily change a person’s mind, unless God changes it. It is only the Spirit of God that can change the destruction and the darkness that exists.

			a.	Pray for the Spirit of holiness: It is not easy to be holy - no matter how hard you try on your own, you will keep falling.

			And declared to be the Son of God with power, according to the spirit of holiness, by the resurrection from the dead:

			Romans 1:4

			b.	Pray for the Spirit of power: You cannot stop doing certain things unless you are have power to. Your flesh, heart and mind are all pulling you in different directions and you need the Spirit of power to change.

			c.	Pray for the Spirit of humility: If you become humble, everything about you will change. A humble person thinks differently from a proud person. It is the proud thinking of Lucifer that made him fall. Every evil spirit is connected to pride.

			Prayer for the Nations

			1. Pray for Nigeria, make mention of:

			1. Lagos

			2. Aba

			3. Ibadan

			4. Benin City

			5. Ilorin

			6. Abuja

			7. Jos

			8. Kaduna

			9. Zaria

			10. Kano

			11. Maiduguri

			2. Pray for Congo, make mention of:

			1. Kinshasa

			2. Kananga

			3. Bukavu

			4. Tshikapa

			5. Mbuji- Mayi

			6. Lubumbashi

			7. Kisangani

			3. Pray for Ethiopia, make mention of:

			1. Mek’ele

			2. Gondar

			3. Bahir dar

			4. Dese

			5. Kombolcha

			6. Dire dawa

			7. Debre birhan

			8. Addis ababa

			9. Jimma

			10. Awassa

			11. Adama

			12. Jijiga

				Give God thanks for answered prayers.





CHAPTER 2




Prayer for Light & Prayer for the Blessing of Fruitfulness




		 			And God said, Let there be light: and there was light. And God saw the light, that it was good: and God divided the light from the darkness. And God called the light Day, and the darkness he called Night. And the evening and the morning were the first day... And God created great whales, and every living creature that moveth, which the waters brought forth abundantly, after their kind, and every winged fowl after his kind: and God saw that it was good. And God blessed them, saying, Be fruitful, and multiply, and fill the waters in the seas, and let fowl multiply in the earth.

			Genesis 1:3-5, 21-22



		 			The watchman is meant to do three things: enquire, return and come. To enquire is to seek understanding from God. To return is to come back. When Rick Joyner had a vision, God told him that when he returned to earth, everything would seem vague. To come means to go (deeper) into God. Every watchman who wakes up to pray must learn to enquire, return and come closer to the Lord. To enquire is to seek things from God.

			Pray and enquire from God.

			Prayer for Light

			1.	Pray for the light of God in your Life

			When God said let there be light on the first day, it could not have been the sun because He made the sun on the fourth day. This means there are other types of light, apart from the stars, moonlight or sunlight.

			And God said, Let there be light: and there was light. And God saw the light, that it was good: and God divided the light from the darkness. And God called the light Day, and the darkness he called Night. And the evening and the morning were the first day.

			Genesis 1:3-5

			And God said, Let there be lights in the firmament of the heaven to divide the day from the night; and let them be for signs, and for seasons, and for days, and years: And let them be for lights in the firmament of the heaven to give light upon the earth: and it was so. And God made two great lights; the greater light to rule the day, and the lesser light to rule the night: he made the stars also. And God set them in the firmament of the heaven to give light upon the earth, and to rule over the day and over the night, and to divide the light from the darkness: and God saw that it was good. And the evening and the morning were the fourth day.

			Genesis 1:14-19

			2. 	Pray that whatever represents light should come into your life. Darkness is associated with evil. Darkness and light are not necessarily night and day.

			Ye are all the children of light, and the children of the day: we are not of the night, nor of darkness.

			1 Thessalonians 5:5

			Seven Types of Light

			a.	Pray for the light of knowledge. There is a form of darkness when you don’t know certain things.

			For God, who commanded the light to shine out of darkness, hath shined in our hearts, to give the light of the knowledge of the glory of God in the face of Jesus Christ.

			2 Corinthians 4:6

			b.	 Pray for the light of the Word of God.

			The entrance of thy words giveth light; it giveth understanding unto the simple.

			Psalm 119:130

			Pray for the light of the Word of God for your life.

			When someone is “enlightened”, it means that they know a lot. People sometimes interact with you to see if you are enlightened. When someone knows a lot but does not have the Word of God, they are still in darkness.

			c.	Pray for the light of the Law of God.

			For the commandment is a lamp; and the law is light; and reproofs of instruction are the way of life:

			Proverbs 6:23

			Pray that God would show you seven laws in the Bible or seven laws that have been given to you through your parents.

			A law is a rule that God gives you; it is light for your life. Countries that are lawless are full of darkness. The lives of people who break laws are full of darkness.

			Pray that whatever law there is, will be a light in your life.

			d.	Pray for the light of the gospel.

			In whom the god of this world hath blinded the minds of them which believe not, lest the light of the glorious gospel of Christ, who is the image of God, should shine unto them.

			2 Corinthians 4:4

			Billy Graham preached the gospel. There are many things you can preach about; but the gospel is light.

			e.	Pray for the light of judgement.

			Therefore, have I hewed them by the prophets; I have slain them by the words of my mouth: and thy judgments are as the light that goeth forth.

			Hosea 6:5

			Pray that you will never be angry when you are judged or corrected in anyway.

			Judgement is a decision that is taken about you. People respond to judgement in different ways. When a person perceives he has been mistreated, he also would not like nor agree with the judgement that is passed against him. If you were to visit a prison, you would find that many inmates proclaim their innocence and believe that they do not deserve to be there. However, judgement is necessary. Judgement is a light. When judgement is missing from your life there is a light missing.

			f.	Pray for the light of life.

			The same came for a witness, to bear witness of the Light, that all men through him might believe. He was not that Light, but was sent to bear witness of that Light. That was the true Light, which lighteth every man that cometh into the world.

			John 1:7-9

			Then spake Jesus again unto them, saying, I am the light of the world: he that followeth me shall not walk in darkness, but shall have the light of life.

			John 8:12

			The light of your life is something that guides you to the left, right or straight. Google maps is a bit like the light of life, you don’t know where you are going but it tells you where to go. If you do not receive Jesus you will wonder what to do with your life. The light of your life is Jesus Christ. I have been working for the Lord for many years and I don’t see how it has been a disadvantage to me.

			“Who should you marry?” Jesus will tell you.

			“When should I marry?” Jesus will tell you.

			Pray for the light of life.

			g.	Pray for the light of revelation.

			Arise, shine; for thy light is come, and the glory of the Lord is risen upon thee.

			Isaiah 60:1

			Pray that in the biggest issue of your life, Jesus Christ would be revealed to you as the answer. Whatever you do, the light for that particular issue will come from Jesus.

			You shine when a certain light comes on! I am talking about “thy light” not any light, I mean a revelation for you.

			In June of 1988, when I was in Suhum, a light came to me. That light was, “From today you can teach.” Don’t think that at any point in time, anyone who has a revelation is sure of their revelation. Everyone who has a revelation, even those who say they have seen Jesus are unsure as to whether they really saw Him. You need to have faith in the revelation that God gives you.

			When I heard the voice, “From today you can teach”, I could have begun to question it; but somehow, I believed that it was from God. What revelation has God given to you? It will make you shine in this life. When God told Kenneth Hagin to teach, he looked odd because everyone was an evangelist. Even so, he continued in the teaching ministry.

			Prayer for the Blessing of Fruitfulness

			And God created great whales, and every living creature that moveth, which the waters brought forth abundantly, after their kind, and every winged fowl after his kind: and God saw that it was good. And God blessed them, saying, Be fruitful, and multiply, and fill the waters in the seas, and let fowl multiply in the earth.

			Genesis 1:21-22

			It is a great blessing to be fruitful. Fruitfulness makes you happy. When women give birth, even though they go through a lot of pain and inconveniences, they are happy in the end. When you are not fruitful you are not blessed in a certain way. It is unexplainable as to why a woman would want to give birth again and again. Fruitfulness is a blessing. Being fruitful is what you must desire for your life and not more money.

			1. 	Pray for the blessing of fruitfulness.

			And God said, Let us make man in our image, after our likeness: and let them have dominion over the fish of the sea, and over the fowl of the air, and over the cattle, and over all the earth, and over every creeping thing that creepeth upon the earth. So God created man in his own image, in the image of God created he him; male and female created he them. And God blessed them, and God said unto them, Be fruitful, and multiply, and replenish the earth, and subdue it: and have dominion over the fish of the sea, and over the fowl of the air, and over every living thing that moveth upon the earth.

			Genesis 1:26-28

			I remember many years ago I was in England praying that God would make me fruitful. God will answer your prayer.

			2. 	Pray for God to touch you. If God touches you, everything about you bears fruit.

			The Prophetic Blessings

			I prophesy that whatever you are involved with is going to have the touch of fruitfulness!

			No matter what you are doing, it will be called fruitful and will just grow in your hand!

			Amen.





CHAPTER 3




Prayer for the Holy Spirit; Prayer for the Original Blessings of Fruitfulness & Prayer for the Nations




		 			And God said, Let us make man in our image, after our likeness: and let them have dominion over the fish of the sea, and over the fowl of the air, and over the cattle, and over all the earth, and over every creeping thing that creepeth upon the earth.

			Genesis 1:26

		 			The Holy Spirit has a form. The Holy Spirit looks like a man. When you perceive the Holy Spirit as a person, you can relate with the Holy Spirit and have a better relationship with Him. If you ever have a chance to listen to Benny Hinn talk about the Holy Spirit, you would notice how real the Holy Spirit is to him. You would also realize how he relates to the Holy Spirit as a Person.

			Prayer for the Holy Spirit

			And God said, Let us make man in our image, after our likeness: and let them have dominion over the fish of the sea, and over the fowl of the air, and over the cattle, and over all the earth, and over every creeping thing that creepeth upon the earth.

			Genesis 1:26

			And I will pray the Father, and he shall give you another Comforter, that he may abide with you for ever;

			John 14:16

			1. Pray for the Holy Spirit to come into your life.

			Some people may feel it is derogatory to reduce the Holy Spirit to a man, but that is not the case. When you think of the Holy Spirit as a person it helps you to form a picture in your mind. As you pray for the Holy Spirit you will see a “man” coming to stand by you. When you pray for the Spirit of wisdom, Wisdom will stand by you. In Rick Joyner’s book, “The Final Quest”, he describes Wisdom as a real person.

			Wherever you are today, you need great help, and this great help is coming in the form of the Holy Spirit.

			When the Holy Spirit comes, He comes to help us. When you serve God, the Spirit of humility and the Spirit of holiness are very important. If there is something wrong about your ministry, it usually concerns one of these two things - humility or holiness.

			Lift your hands and pray for the Holy Spirit.

			a.	Pray for the Spirit of Holiness

			b.	Pray for the Spirit of Humility

			c.	Pray for the Spirit of Faith

			d.	Pray for the Spirit of Love

			e.	Pray for the Spirit of Revelation

			Love is a spirit. When you don’t have love, you don’t have the Holy Spirit. We need the Holy Spirit.

			2. 	Pray for the Spirit of faith and love.

			The Spirit of revelation means that something is revealed to you. You must expect things to be revealed to you. You must expect God to reveal His Word, knowledge and wisdom. God’s wisdom is what you must do (about something). When you lack understanding you cannot relate with people. Relationships are reduced because of lack of understanding.

			3. 	Pray for revelation.

			Pray for the Spirit of revelation to come and stand by you.

			I heard Kenneth Hagin say that he prays many times for the Spirit of revelation. Many of the books I write are unique topics; it is a revelation of something.

			4. 	Thank God for the Holy Spirit.

			A man of God once said that the right way of showing gratitude, is to say “Thank you” even before an item given is placed into your hands. When you say “Thank you” it is a sign of acceptance of something.

			Prayer for the Original Blessing of Fruitfulness

			And God blessed them, and God said unto them, Be fruitful, and multiply, and replenish the earth, and subdue it: and have dominion over the fish of the sea, and over the fowl of the air, and over every living thing that moveth upon the earth.

			Genesis 1:28

			Thorns also and thistles shall it bring forth to thee; and thou shalt eat the herb of the field;

			Genesis 3:18

			1. 	Pray for the original blessing of fruitfulness from God. It is not easy to be fruitful.

			2. 	Pray that the thorns and thistles in your life would be burnt.

			Pray against thorns that are coming from your work.

			Perhaps your work is giving you some thorns or something as painful. You can go to a particular workplace and wish you never went there.

			3. 	Pray for twelve kinds of fruits in your life and ministry.

			And he shewed me a pure river of water of life, clear as crystal, proceeding out of the throne of God and of the Lamb. In the midst of the street of it, and on either side of the river, was there the tree of life, which bare twelve manner of fruits, and yielded her fruit every month: and the leaves of the tree were for the healing of the nations.

			Revelation 22:1-2

			God has imparted to you the tree of life that bears twelve kinds of fruits!

			Twelve Kinds of Fruits

			a.	Your souls won

			b.	Your sheep

			c.	Your disciples

			d.	Your followers

			e.	Your sons

			f.	Your daughters

			g.	Your flocks

			h.	Your strangers

			i.	Nations

			j.	Kings

			k.	Priest and ministers

			l.	Forces of the gentiles

			And strangers shall stand and feed your flocks, and the sons of the alien shall be your plowmen and your vinedressers. But ye shall be named the Priests of the LORD: men shall call you the Ministers of our God: ye shall eat the riches of the Gentiles, and in their glory shall ye boast yourselves.

			Isaiah 61:5-6

			Trees are meant to have more fruit. It is only a curse that makes a tree not bear fruits.

			4. 	Pray against any curse that does not make you bear fruits.

			5. 	Pray for good fruits that speak louder than words.

			You are known by your fruits. Good fruits will tell if you are corrupt or not corrupt. When you bear the fruits God wants you to bear, no one will be able to challenge who you are. They may say what they want to say, but a corrupt tree does not bring forth good fruits.

			As you pray for fruits you will be known by your fruits.

			For a good tree bringeth not forth corrupt fruit; neither doth a corrupt tree bring forth good fruit. For every tree is known by his own fruit. For of thorns men do not gather figs, nor of a bramble bush gather they grapes.

			Luke 6:43-44

			In your ministry you need fruits. The curse of barrenness is broken today!

			If I do not the works of my Father, believe me not. But if I do, though ye believe not me, believe the works: that ye may know, and believe, that the Father is in me, and I in him.

			John 10:37-38

			Prayer for the Nations

			Pray for:

			●	Senegal

			●	Guinea

			●	Sierra Leone

			●	Gambia

			●	Liberia

			●	Ivory Coast

			●	Mali

			●	Burkina Faso

			●	Niger

			●	Nigeria

			●	Cameroon

			●	Democratic Republic of the Congo

			●	Angola

			●	Namibia

			●	Botswana

			●	South Africa

			●	Lesotho

			●	Mozambique

			●	Malawi

			●	Zambia

			●	United Republic of Tanzania

			●	Kenya

			●	Ethiopia

			●	South Sudan

			●	Central African Republic

			●	England

			●	France

			●	Italy

			●	Germany

			●	India

			●	Bangladesh

			●	Sri Lanka

			●	Thailand

				Thank God for answered prayers.





CHAPTER 4




Prayer about Things You Must Never Do; Prayer to Become a Good Son & Prayer for the Nations




		 			The burden of Dumah. He calleth to me out of Seir, Watchman, what of the night? Watchman, what of the night? The watchman said, The morning cometh, and also the night: if ye will enquire, enquire ye: return, come.

			Isaiah 21:11-12

1. 	Pray for mercy and forgiveness.

			Pray against any manifestation of pride. Pray for mercy.

			Pray for forgiveness.

			2. 	Pray against any form of pride and lust.

			Many things are done out of pride and lust.

			3. 	Pray concerning the one thing that God has told you to do.

			And the LORD God took the man, and put him into the garden of Eden to dress it and to keep it. And the LORD God commanded the man, saying, Of every tree of the garden thou mayest freely eat:

			Genesis 2:15-16

			As you relate with God, you will begin to see that He gives you instructions on things to do and things not to do. God told Derek Prince to go and preach on the radio and many people got to know him through that. When God tells you to do something, it makes you fruitful. God is constantly speaking to us and instructing us.

			4. 	Pray concerning the one thing that the Holy Spirit has told you not to do.

			There are also some things that God instructs you not to do. Do not think that your desire or love for God can prevent you from falling. God told “perfect” Adam and Eve not to eat of the tree. There is something God has told you not to do.

			5. 	Pray about what God has told you do that will make you fruitful.

			6. 	Pray for grace.

			You may never know what you are capable of doing. People who have had higher aspirations than you have fallen to certain sins. They had more noble goals than you did but they still fell.

			Seven Things to Pray about That You Will Never Do

			a.	Pray that you will never blaspheme against the Lord.

			And he that blasphemeth the name of the LORD, he shall surely be put to death, and all the congregation shall certainly stone him: as well the stranger, as he that is born in the land, when he blasphemeth the name of the LORD, shall be put to death.

			Leviticus 24:16

			b.	Pray that you do not touch holy things.

			And thou shalt set bounds unto the people round about, saying, Take heed to yourselves, that ye go not up into the mount, or touch the border of it: whosoever toucheth the mount shall be surely put to death:

			Exodus 19:12

			c.	Pray that you will never kill anyone accidentally.

			Neither shall he go out of the sanctuary, nor profane the sanctuary of his God; for the crown of the anointing oil of his God is upon him: I am the LORD.

			Leviticus 21:12

			d.	Pray that you will never attack your father.

			Anyone who attacks their father or mother is to be put to death.

			Anyone who curses their father or mother is to be put to death.

			Exodus 21:15, 17 (NIV)

			e.	Pray that you will never not rest.

			Ye shall keep the sabbath therefore,; for it is holy unto you: every one that defileth it shall surely be put to death: for whosoever doeth any work therein, that soul shall be cut off from among his people.

			Exodus 31:14

			f.	Pray that you will never commit the sin of idol worship.

			Again, thou shalt say to the children of Israel, Whosoever he be of the children of Israel, or of the strangers that sojourn in Israel, that giveth any of his seed unto Molech; he shall surely be put to death: the people of the land shall stone him with stones.

			Leviticus 20:2

			g.	Pray that you will never commit adultery.

			And the man that committeth adultery with another man’s wife, even he that committeth adultery with his neighbour’s wife, the adulterer and the adulteress shall surely be put to death. And the man that lieth with his father’s wife hath uncovered his father’s nakedness: both of them shall surely be put to death; their blood shall be upon them.

			Leviticus 20:10-11

			Prayer That You Will Be a Good Son

			And Adam knew Eve his wife; and she conceived, and bare Cain, and said, I have gotten a man from the LORD. And she again bare his brother Abel. And Abel was a keeper of sheep, but Cain was a tiller of the ground. And in process of time it came to pass, that Cain brought of the fruit of the ground an offering unto the LORD. And Abel, he also brought of the firstlings of his flock and of the fat thereof. And the LORD had respect unto Abel and to his offering: but unto Cain and to his offering he had not respect. And Cain was very wroth, and his countenance fell. And the LORD said unto Cain, Why art thou wroth? and why is thy countenance fallen? If thou doest well, shalt thou not be accepted? and if thou doest not well, sin lieth at the door. And unto thee shall be his desire, and thou shalt rule over him.

			Genesis 4:1-7

			You must realize you are not being judged based only on what you say. Your face and what you don’t say is part of your communication. You say a lot through your attitude and your face.

			1. 	Pray that you will be one of the good sons or daughters and not an evil son.

			Not as Cain, who was of that wicked one, and slew his brother. And wherefore slew he him? Because his own works were evil, and his brother’s righteous.

			1 John 3:12

			2. 	Pray to be a good child to anyone who has looked after you or has cared for you even spiritually.

			3. 	Pray against the characteristics of Cain.

			a.	Cain did not know what to do. Pray that you will know what to do.

			Pray and bind the spirit of not knowing.

			Unlike Cain, Abel knew what to do. We have certain characteristics because we are born in sin. People commit sin and say they “didn’t know”. This is the spirit of not knowing what is right. But you have to be careful. You must know what is right.

			And Abel, he also brought of the firstlings of his flock and of the fat thereof. And the LORD had respect unto Abel and to his offering: but unto Cain and to his offering he had not respect. And Cain was very wroth, and his countenance fell.

			Genesis 4:4-5

			And of the children of Issachar, which were men that had understanding of the times, to know what Israel ought to do; the heads of them were two hundred; and all their brethren were at their commandment.

			1 Chronicles 12:32

			You can give an excuse that you did not know something, but you cannot stand behind the excuse of ignorance forever. You must know what is right.

			When Jesus then lifted up his eyes, and saw a great company come unto him, he saith unto Philip, Whence shall we buy bread, that these may eat? And this he said to prove him: for he himself knew what he would do.

			John 6:5-6

			b.	Cain was not sacrificial. Pray that you will be sacrificial and not give poor sacrifices.

			Not as Cain, who was of that wicked one, and slew his brother. And wherefore slew he him? Because his own works were evil, and his brother’s righteous.

			1 John 3:12

			There must be an element of sacrifice in our service to God. A sacrifice is not just in giving money but it can be in many other ways. You do not have to broadcast your sacrifice to everyone.

			The evil spirit of not sacrificing will be taken away as you pray!

			I beseech you therefore, brethren, by the mercies of God, that ye present your bodies a living sacrifice, holy, acceptable unto God, which is your reasonable service.

			Romans 12:1

			c.	Cain was a quarrelsome person. Pray that you will not be quarrelsome.

			Pray to flush out any form of quarrelsomeness from your life.

			When you have a quarrelsome person around, quarrels come up even when there is no issue. The pattern of a quarrelsome person is that there is always something to quarrel about. The very presence of the person is always followed by quarrels.

			Pray that you will not have that spirit.

			d.	Pray that you will not be schismatic. Pray that you will not cause division anywhere you are.

			A man that is an heretick after the first and second admonition reject;

			Titus 3:10

			You will not be associated with bringing division in your family.

			Cain and Abel were divided. A lot of families are divided into “mummy’s supporters” or “daddy’s supporters.” Division is of the devil.

			e.	Pray that you will not be proud. Pray that you will not be a person full of pride.

			Only by pride cometh contention: but with the well advised is wisdom.

			Proverbs 13:10

			You will find quarrels everywhere there is a proud person. Pride brings issues.

			f.	Pray that envy will not be found in you. Pray against envy because envy causes conflict. Cain was envious that God accepted Abel.

			Pray that envy will not be found in you.

				Prayer for the Nations

			a.	Pray for the USA

			b.	Pray for India

			c.	Pray for Nigeria

				Thank God for answered prayers.





CHAPTER 5




Prayer for the Holy Spirit; Prayer for Teaching Gifts & Prayer for the Nations




		 			If ye then, being evil, know how to give good gifts unto your children: how much more shall your heavenly Father give the Holy Spirit to them that ask him?

			Luke 11:13



		 			Give thanks unto God.

			The burden of Dumah. He calleth to me out of Seir, Watchman, what of the night? Watchman, what of the night? The watchman said, The morning cometh, and also the night: if ye will enquire, enquire ye: return, come.

			Isaiah 21:11-12

			Prayer for the Holy Spirit

			If ye then, being evil, know how to give good gifts unto your children: how much more shall your heavenly Father give the Holy Spirit to them that ask him?

			Luke 11:13

			…and declared to be the Son of God with power, according to the spirit of holiness, by the resurrection from the dead:

			Romans 1:4

			As you pray for the Holy Spirit, you will be declared to be God’s child.

			A declaration is very important. Big changes take place in your life when you are declared to be God’s child. It is through the power of God with the Spirit of holiness that you are declared to be God’s child. Holiness means sacredness and moral purity; it also means majesty and to be special. Holiness is something we struggle with. It is difficult to be morally pure and sacred. It takes the Spirit of holiness to be morally pure.

			1.	Pray for the Holy Spirit.

			To be sacred means to be important in a special way. If you look at the Jews, especially on the sabbath day, they distinguish themselves from everyone.

			2.	Pray for the Spirit of Holiness.

			The Spirit of holiness will take you to the next level by affecting two areas of your life.

			The Spirit of holiness will deal with the filthiness of your flesh and spirit. What is in your heart? - Sins like pride, envy and many more that cannot be easily seen.

			Pray for the Spirit of holiness and the Holy Spirit!

			Having therefore, these promises, dearly beloved, let us cleanse ourselves from all filthiness of the flesh and spirit, perfecting holiness in the fear of God.

			2 Corinthians 7:1

			3.	Pray for the Spirit of Humility.

			The Spirit of humility is God’s Spirit. The spirit you possess turns you into its likeness. If you have the Spirit of humility, you will be humble. Praying for the Spirit of humility will make you humble yourself. Humility does not make you feel as though you are being cheated.

			Pray and ask God not to feel you are being cheated.

			The Spirit of humility blocks evil spirits. Some people cannot speak to someone that is younger than them. Having the ability to relate with other races requires humility.

			Pray for the Spirit of humility.

			Who, being in the form of God, thought it not robbery to be equal with God:

			Philippians 2:6

			Now therefore, there is utterly a fault among you, because ye go to law one with another. Why do ye not rather take wrong? why do ye not rather suffer yourselves to be defrauded?

			1 Corinthians 6:7

			1 Corinthians 6:7 describes what happens to Christians who feel that they are being defrauded.

			Pray that this will not be you. Pray for humility.

			Being concerned about your reputation is a sign of pride. As you serve God, you will encounter people who have all kinds of opinions about you. You don’t need to fight against it but rather pray for the ability not to be concerned about the thoughts people have against you.

			But made himself of no reputation, and took upon him the form of a servant, and was made in the likeness of men:

			Philippians 2:7

			4.	Pray for the Spirit to serve.

			A servant is someone who serves, who does errands and runs around to accomplish the will of the person who sent him. A servant is someone who God sends. When you learn to accept low things, you will be accepted for high things.

			The dictionary defines a “servant” as: one who serves, or does services, voluntarily or on compulsion; a person who is employed by another for menial offices, or for other labour, and is subject to his command; a person who labours or exerts himself for the benefit of another, his master or employer; a subordinate helper.

			Pray that you would have a spirit to serve.

			5. 	Pray that you will accept that you are a “man”. When you do not accept you are normal human being, you end up behaving abnormally.

			But made himself of no reputation, and took upon him the form of a servant, and was made in the likeness of men:

			Philippians 2:7

			6. 	Pray that you will become obedient.

			Pray that you will be obedient unto death.

				To be humble means to lower one’s self unto obedience because humility makes you obedient.

			God is sending the Holy Spirit to you and you are becoming obedient.

			You cannot become holy or obedient by your own strength.

			And being found in fashion as a man, he humbled himself, and became obedient unto death, even the death of the cross.

			Philippians 2:8

			Prayer for Teaching Gifts to Come Out of You

			In the midst of the street of it, and on either side of the river, was there the tree of life, which bare twelve manner of fruits, and yielded her fruit every month: and the leaves of the tree were for the healing of the nations.

			Revelation 22:2

			God is going to make you to bear all types of fruits.

			You can bear fruit out of the ministry gift you have. For example, there are many fruits that come from a teaching ministry.

			a.	Pray that you will be a good teacher who transmits understanding.

			I have more understanding than all my teachers: for thy testimonies are my meditation.

			Psalm 119:99

			People explain and understand things differently.

			Pray that the grace of teaching will be on you. Pray that people will understand you as a teacher.

			b.	Pray that you will be a good counsellor.

			And I will restore thy judges as at the first, and thy counsellors as at the beginning: afterward thou shalt be called, The city of righteousness, the faithful city.

			Isaiah 1:26

			Being a counsellor is one of the fruits of your teaching ministry.

			Pray that you will be a good counsellor.

			c.	Pray for the fruit of rebuke in your teaching ministry.

			Sometimes you will not learn anything without a rebuke. Many people do not respond to counselling. Sometimes a person needs to be rebuked in order to be taught.

			Preach the word; be instant in season, out of season; reprove, rebuke, exhort with all longsuffering and doctrine.

			2 Timothy 4:2

			It is better to hear the rebuke of the wise, than for a man to hear the song of fools.

			Ecclesiastes 7:5

			As many as I love, I rebuke and chasten: be zealous therefore, and repent.

			Revelation 3:19

			d.	Pray for the fruit of example setting in your teaching ministry.

			Let no man despise thy youth; but be thou an example of the believers, in word, in conversation, in charity, in spirit, in faith, in purity.

			1 Timothy 4:12

			Through example-setting you can minister lifestyle, words, faith, anointing, purity, love. A shepherd moves and the people follow. Your example-setting ministry is a fruit.

			Reverend Adeboye1 said a person through whom a word comes is very important. Those who teach people actually receive more because they do not only teach, but set examples.

			e.	Pray for the fruit of exhortation in your teaching ministry.

			Preach the word; be instant in season, out of season; reprove, rebuke, exhort with all longsuffering and doctrine.

			2 Timothy 4:2

			To exhort is to stir up. A certain man of God, who is also a teacher, described his own teaching as flat and prosaic; but there are some people who teach and stir you up. That is exhortation.

			f.	Pray for the fruit of teaching as a pastor or a shepherd in your teaching ministry.

			And I will give you pastors according to mine heart, which shall feed you with knowledge and understanding.

			Jeremiah 3:15

			Knowledge and understanding come by teaching. This is the fruit that allows you to keep teaching without getting tired. It is the fruit that doesn’t make people get tired of listening to you.

			g.	Pray for the fruit of reading in your teaching ministry.

			And he came to Nazareth, where he had been brought up: and, as his custom was, he went into the synagogue on the sabbath day, and stood up for to read.

			Luke 4:16

			Reading is a type of ministry. Reading is like preaching. If you listen to audio books it is like listening to preaching.

			h.	Pray for the fruit of writing a song in your teaching ministry.

			Moses therefore, wrote this song the same day, and taught it the children of Israel.

			Deuteronomy 31:22

			Then on that day David delivered first this psalm to thank the LORD into the hand of Asaph and his brethren. Give thanks unto the LORD, call upon his name, Make known his deeds among the people.

			1 Chronicles 16:7-8

			The songs that Tommy Walker2 has written also teach. Writing songs is a form of teaching.

			i.	Pray for the fruit of singing a song in your teaching ministry.

			Your singing ministry is one of the fruits of your teaching ministry. Sometimes you may have to appoint someone to sing a song you have written.

			And he appointed certain of the Levites to minister before the ark of the LORD , and to record, and to thank and praise the LORD God of Israel: Asaph the chief, and next to him Zechariah, Jeiel, and Shemiramoth, and Jehiel, and Mattithiah, and Eliab, and Benaiah, and Obed-edom: and Jeiel with psalteries and with harps; but Asaph made a sound with cymbals;

			1 Chronicles 16:4-5

			Prayer for the Nations

			a.	Pray for the Democratic Republic of the Congo

			b.	Pray for Angola

				Thank God for answered prayers.



			1 General Overseer, Redeemed Christian Church of God

			2 Tommy Walker is a Christian songwriter and gospel artist





CHAPTER 6




Prayer to Walk With God and to Do What Jesus Did; Prayer against Curses & Prayer for the Nations




		 			And the days of Adam after he had begotten Seth were eight hundred years: and he begat sons and daughters: And all the days that Adam lived were nine hundred and thirty years: and he died. And Seth lived an hundred and five years, and begat Enos: ...And Cainan lived seventy years, and begat Mahalaleel: And Cainan lived after he begat Mahalaleel eight hundred and forty years, and begat sons and daughters: And all the days of Cainan were nine hundred and ten years: and he died. And Mahalaleel lived sixty and five years, and begat Jared: And Mahalaleel lived after he begat Jared eight hundred and thirty years, and begat sons and daughters: And all the days of Mahalaleel were eight hundred ninety and five years: and he died. And Jared lived an hundred sixty and two years, and he begat Enoch: And Jared lived after he begat Enoch eight hundred years, and begat sons and daughters: ...And Enoch lived sixty and five years, and begat Methuselah: and Enoch walked with God after he begat Methuselah three hundred years, and begat sons and daughters: and all the days of Enoch were three hundred sixty and five years: And Enoch walked with God: and he was not; for God took him.And Methuselah lived an hundred eighty and seven years, and begat Lamech. And Methuselah lived after he begat Lamech seven hundred eighty and two years, and begat sons and daughters: ...And Lamech lived an hundred eighty and two years, and begat a son: And he called his name Noah, saying, This same shall comfort us concerning our work and toil of our hands, because of the ground which the LORD hath cursed.

			Genesis 5:4-6, 12-19, 21-26, 28-29



		 			Give thanks to God and praise Him.

			Prayer to Walk With God

			1.	Pray that you will walk with God all the days of your life.

			And the days of Adam after he had begotten Seth were eight hundred years: and he begat sons and daughters: And all the days that Adam lived were nine hundred and thirty years: and he died. And Seth lived an hundred and five years, and begat Enos: …And Cainan lived seventy years, and begat Mahalaleel: And Cainan lived after he begat Mahalaleel eight hundred and forty years, and begat sons and daughters: And all the days of Cainan were nine hundred and ten years: and he died. And Mahalaleel lived sixty and five years, and begat Jared: And Mahalaleel lived after he begat Jared eight hundred and thirty years, and begat sons and daughters: And all the days of Mahalaleel were eight hundred ninety and five years: and he died. And Jared lived an hundred sixty and two years, and he begat Enoch: And Jared lived after he begat Enoch eight hundred years, and begat sons and daughters: …And Enoch lived sixty and five years, and begat Methuselah: and Enoch walked with God after he begat Methuselah three hundred years, and begat sons and daughters: and all the days of Enoch were three hundred sixty and five years: And Enoch walked with God: and he was not; for God took him. And Methuselah lived an hundred eighty and seven years, and begat Lamech. And Methuselah lived after he begat Lamech seven hundred eighty and two years, and begat sons and daughters: …And Lamech lived an hundred eighty and two years, and begat a son: And he called his name Noah, saying, This same shall comfort us concerning our work and toil of our hands, because of the ground which the LORD hath cursed.

			Genesis 5:4-6, 12-19, 21-26, 28-29

			The Bible is one of the oldest books with records all the way back to Adam. Enoch walked with God and God took him. A person who walks with God is different from all other men. The grace of Enoch is what we need. You will notice that in your class, amongst your friends and in your family, not many of them walk with God.

			Enoch walked with God after having a child. Not many people walk with God after having a child. Having a child is a milestone in life. There are so many changes that come after having a child; but Enoch, even after he had his child, walked with God.

			2.	Pray and commit yourself to walk with God to the end of your life.

			I believe that walking with God is what makes a difference in life. Will you walk with God or walk alone all your life? If we can get close to God, it will affect your life in a great way.

			And Enoch walked with God after he begat Methuselah three hundred years, and begat sons and daughters:

			Genesis 5:22

			Prayer for 7 Ways of Walking with God

			a.	Pray that you will walk with God before you have children.

			Serving God before you have responsibilities and serving God afterwards are two different things. They are like two different lives. You will notice that younger women are much more cheerful and happier looking than older women.

			b.	Pray that you walk with God even after you have children and responsibilities.

			c.	Pray that you walk with God no matter how your life is.

			It is not common to see someone get to 100 years old; so imagine meeting someone who lived for 400 years. It would make you wonder how their life was. If you live in America your life will be different from someone living in Ghana. Every place is different and everyone is experiencing something different.

			Pray that you will walk with God, no matter the type of life you are experiencing.

			d.	Pray that you will walk with God till the end of your life, as you grow older may you draw closer to God.

			By faith Enoch was translated that he should not see death; and was not found, because God had translated him: for before his translation he had this testimony, that he pleased God.

			Hebrews 11:5

			God translated Enoch. Enoch was not found. When you walk with God, you please God.

			As you grow older you also have a tendency to do evil but it plays out in a horrible way. Both young and old people are struggling to serve God!

			For it came to pass, when Solomon was old, that his wives turned away his heart after other gods: and his heart was not perfect with the LORD his God, as was the heart of David his father.

			1 Kings 11:4

			e.	Pray that you walk with God in truth.

			f.	Pray that you walk with God in righteousness.

			g.	Pray that you walk with God in uprightness of heart.

			And Solomon said, Thou hast shewed unto thy servant David my father great mercy, according as he walked before thee in truth, and in righteousness, and in uprightness of heart with thee; and thou hast kept for him this great kindness, that thou hast given him a son to sit on his throne, as it is this day.

			1 Kings 3:6

			3.	Pray for the grace to walk with God.

			Many of us are walking in deception. Many of us are not living in righteousness. When someone lies to you they begin to go far from you. Telling the truth is part of walking with someone because you draw close to a person as you speak the truth. Walking with God is walking in truth, walking in righteousness and walking in uprightness of heart. God will show you great kindness as you walk with Him.

			Seven Things That Jesus Did That You Must Do

			1. 	Pray for seven things that Jesus did that you must also do to be more like Jesus and walk closely to Him.

			In the beginning was the Word, and the Word was with God, and the Word was God... He came unto his own, and his own received him not. But as many as received him, to them gave he power to become the sons of God, even to them that believe on his name:

			John 1:1, 11-12

			God in His great mercy for us decided to send His Son to come to us and show us how He is. Jesus came full of grace and truth but men rejected Him. Walking with God is walking with Jesus. Once you can accept Jesus and walk according to His will, it is actually walking with God because walking with Jesus is walking with God.

			2.	Pray and write down seven things that Jesus did that you must do.

			Seven Things

			a.	Pray that you will flow with those who accept you.

			Not everyone accepts you, there are many that hate you.

			He came unto his own, and his own received him not.

			John 1:11

			b.	Pray that you will be full of graciousness. Jesus’ glory was that He was full of grace.

			And the Word was made flesh, and dwelt among us, (and we beheld his glory, the glory as of the only begotten of the Father,) full of grace and truth.

			John 1:14

			c.	Pray that you will be full of truth.

			And the Word was made flesh, and dwelt among us, (and we beheld his glory, the glory as of the only begotten of the Father,) full of grace and truth.

			John 1:14

			d.	Pray that you will go into the world.

			He was in the world, and the world was made by him, and the world knew him not. He came unto his own, and his own received him not.

			John 1:10-11

			e.	Pray that you will have something to show the world.

			He saith unto them, Come and see. They came and saw where he dwelt, and abode with him that day: for it was about the tenth hour.

			John 1:39

			f.	Pray that you will be able to tell people to follow you.

			The day following Jesus would go forth into Galilee, and findeth Philip, and saith unto him, Follow me.

			John 1:43

			g.	Pray that you will believe in angels.

			And he saith unto him, Verily, verily, I say unto you, Hereafter ye shall see heaven open, and the angels of God ascending and descending upon the Son of man.

			John 1:51

			4. 	Pray that you will please God.

			By faith Enoch was translated that he should not see death; and was not found, because God had translated him: for before his translation he had this testimony, that he pleased God.

			Hebrews 11:5

			The Bible is a book of miracles and fantastic stories. If you don’t have faith you can not be a Christian. You need to believe in miracles. Enoch pleased God. It is not easy to please humans, how much more God.

			But without faith it is impossible to please him: for he that cometh to God must believe that he is, and that he is a rewarder of them that diligently seek him.

			Hebrews 11:6

			Enoch must have been a real man of faith. He must have believed things. You must believe in God and His words.

			5.	Pray that God will like you.

			Howbeit the LORD God of Israel chose me before all the house of my father to be king over Israel for ever: for he hath chosen Judah to be the ruler; and of the house of Judah, the house of my father; and among the sons of my father he liked me to make me king over all Israel:

			1 Chronicles 28:4

			We are all called God’s bride.

			Pray for God to give you grace to become someone He would like.

			Prayer against Curses

			1. 	Pray against generational curses.

			Whosoever is born of God doth not commit sin; for his seed remaineth in him: and he cannot sin, because he is born of God.

			1 John 3:9

			If we say that we have no sin, we deceive ourselves, and the truth is not in us. If we confess our sins, he is faithful and just to forgive us our sins, and to cleanse us from all unrighteousness.

			1 John 1:8-9

			Who are kept by the power of God through faith unto salvation ready to be revealed in the last time.

			1 Peter 1:5

			Even though we are redeemed from every curse, there are still curses that have to be removed.

			And when Abner was returned to Hebron, Joab took him aside in the gate to speak with him quietly, and smote him there under the fifth rib, that he died, for the blood of Asahel his brother. And afterward when David heard it, he said, I and my kingdom are guiltless before the LORD for ever from the blood of Abner the son of Ner: Let it rest on the head of Joab, and on all his father’s house; and let there not fail from the house of Joab one that hath an issue, or that is a leper, or that leaneth on a staff, or that falleth on the sword, or that lacketh bread.

			2 Samuel 3:27-29

			2. 	Pray that God will intervene against any curse that brings an issue or a sore.

			3.	Pray against generational curses of leprosy.

			Let it rest on the head of Joab, and on all his father’s house; and let there not fail from the house of Joab one that hath an issue, or that is a leper, or that leaneth on a staff, or that falleth on the sword, or that lacketh bread.

			2 Samuel 3:29

			The curse of a leper represents a cancer or an incurable disease.

			You are healed from every incurable disease. Receive total freedom from every demonic attachment in your life.

			4.	Pray against the curse of “one that leaneth on a staff”.

			Let it rest on the head of Joab, and on all his father’s house; and let there not fail from the house of Joab one that hath an issue, or that is a leper, or that leaneth on a staff, or that falleth on the sword, or that lacketh bread.

			2 Samuel 3:29

			One “that leaneth on a staff” symbolises a cripple or someone who is weak.

			The curse is going away. I declare a disconnection from whatever makes you lean on a staff, whatever makes you need a walking stick, whatever causes blindness or whatever makes you need assistance.

			Wherefore, as by one man sin entered into the world, and death by sin; and so death passed upon all men, for that all have sinned:

			Romans 5:12

			One man brought sin and then death followed; and this has now passed down to all men.

			5.	Pray against the devil. Pray for deliverance from generational curses. Plead the blood of Jesus!

			Let it rest on the head of Joab, and on all his father’s house; and let there not fail from the house of Joab one that hath an issue, or that is a leper, or that leaneth on a staff, or that falleth on the sword, or that lacketh bread.

			2 Samuel 3:29

			Prayer for the Nations

			1.	Pray for salvation, pray for mercy and pray for strength for the nations.

			a.	Pray for Burma

			b.	Pray for Vietnam

			c.	Pray for Laos

			d.	Pray for Thailand

			e.	Pray for Cambodia

			f.	Pray for Bangladesh

			g.	Pray for your nation wherever you are.

			I exhort therefore, that, first of all, supplications, prayers, intercessions, and giving of thanks, be made for all men; For kings, and for all that are in authority; that we may lead a quiet and peaceable life in all godliness and honesty.

			1 Timothy 2:1-2

			2.	Pray for all those that are in charge and in authority of your country that you are in.

			The king’s heart is in the hand of the LORD, As the rivers of water: he turneth it whithersoever he will.

			Proverbs 21:1





CHAPTER 7




Prayer for the Church; Prayer for God’s Servants & Prayer for the Nations




		 			Blessed is the man whom thou choosest, and causest to approach unto thee, that he may dwell in thy courts: we shall be satisfied with the goodness of thy house, even of thy holy temple.

			Psalm 65:4

		 			1.	Give thanks to God.

			Blessed is the man whom thou choosest, and causest to approach unto thee, that he may dwell in thy courts: we shall be satisfied with the goodness of thy house, even of thy holy temple.

			Psalm 65:4

			Praying for the Sick

			Kenneth Hagin had visions of Jesus on several occasions. During one of such encounters, the Lord said to him to always pray for the sick because it would result in a different outcome. Sometimes situations and problems look overwhelming, as though nothing good can come out of them. But when we pray it makes a difference!

			2. 	Pray that joy will come into the world, that everywhere you operate from will be called a house of prayer - not a house of strife but a house of prayer.

			These I will bring to my holy mountain and give them joy in my house of prayer. Their burnt offerings and sacrifices will be accepted on my altar; for my house will be called a house of prayer for all nations.”

			Isaiah 56:7 (NIV)

			3.	Pray that your gifts and offering will be accepted.

			Sometimes your offering or gift is not accepted. I have had the experience of receiving a gift which was more of an insult, because gifts have meanings.

			When ye come to appear before me, who hath required this at your hand, to tread my courts? Bring no more vain oblations; incense is an abomination unto me; the new moons and sabbaths, the calling of assemblies, I cannot away with; it is iniquity, even the solemn meeting. ...Wash you, make you clean; put away the evil of your doings from before mine eyes; cease to do evil; Learn to do well; seek judgment, relieve the oppressed, judge the fatherless, plead for the widow. Come now, and let us reason together, saith the LORD: though your sins be as scarlet, they shall be as white as snow; though they be red like crimson, they shall be as wool.

			Isaiah 1:12-13, 16-18

			4. 	Pray and bind every demonic work that seeks to destroy the church and God’s servant.

			(for the weapons of our warfare are not carnal, but mighty through God to the pulling down of strong holds;)

			2 Corinthians 10:4

			5.	Pray and cast down all imagination that exalts itself against the knowledge of God. Whatever you bind on earth will be bound in heaven.

			Verily I say unto you, Whatsoever ye shall bind on earth shall be bound in heaven: and whatsoever ye shall loose on earth shall be loosed in heaven.

			Matthew 18:18

			6.	Pray and bind demonic powers.

			A cobra is a picture of a threatening snake.

			Curse and bind every threatening spirit.

			Declare judgement on every python spirit.

			Pray and curse every deceptive spirit.

			Every cobra, every python deceptive spirit is brought down 	 now!

			(for the weapons of our warfare are not carnal, but mighty through God to the pulling down of strong holds;) Casting down imaginations, and every high thing that exalteth itself against the knowledge of God, and bringing into captivity every thought to the obedience of Christ;

			2 Corinthians 10:4-5

			7. 	Pray for “the gift”.

			Ye also helping together by prayer for us, that for the gift bestowed upon us by the means of many persons thanks may be given by many on our behalf.

			2 Corinthians 1:11

			To write books, have conferences or build a Bible school is all part of the gift bestowed upon you.

			Pray for the gift.

			8. 	Pray that God would open unto you a door of utterance.

			Withal praying also for us, that God would open unto us a door of utterance, to speak the mystery of Christ, for which I am also in bonds:

			Colossians 4:3

			There are many things that happen to ministers because of the mystery of Christ.

			9. Pray for your servant of God.

			Brethren, pray for us.

			1 Thessalonians 5:25

			Finally, brethren, pray for us, that the word of the Lord may have free course, and be glorified, even as it is with you:

			2 Thessalonians 3:1

			Pray for us: for we trust we have a good conscience, in all things willing to live honestly.

			Hebrews 13:18

			10. 	Pray that we will all love God.

			He that hath my commandments, and keepeth them, he it is that loveth me: and he that loveth me shall be loved of my Father, and I will love him, and will manifest myself to him.

			John 14:21

			It is not just by saying, but by actually keeping God’s commandments that show that you love God.

			Pray to love God.

			Pray that you would keep His commandments.

			Pray that you would serve Him.

			Pray for mercy.

			Pray for mercy that God would be kind to you. You don’t want to go to heaven and find out you missed the mark.

			Prayer for the Nations

			1.	Pray for the Nations.

			2. 	Thank God for answered prayers. Thank God that grace will lead you home.

			...and to our beloved Apphia, and Archippus our fellow soldier, and to the church in thy house:

			Philemon 1:2

			You will be a fellow soldier in the battle for Christ!

			Amen.





CHAPTER 8




Prayer Concerning the Mysteries of God & Prayer Concerning the Mysteries of Noah (Prayers from Genesis 6)




		 			And it came to pass, when men began to multiply on the face of the earth, and daughters were born unto them,

			That the sons of God saw the daughters of men that they were fair; and they took them wives of all which they chose.

			And the Lord said, My spirit shall not always strive with man, for that he also is flesh: yet his days shall be an hundred and twenty years.

			There were giants in the earth in those days; and also after that, when the sons of God came in unto the daughters of men, and they bare children to them, the same became mighty men which were of old, men of renown.

			And God saw that the wickedness of man was great in the earth, and that every imagination of the thoughts of his heart was only evil continually.

			And it repented the Lord that he had made man on the earth, and it grieved him at his heart.

			And the Lord said, I will destroy man whom I have created from the face of the earth; both man, and

			beast, and the creeping thing, and the fowls of the air; for it repenteth me that I have made them.

			But Noah found grace in the eyes of the Lord.

			These are the generations of Noah: Noah was a just man and perfect in his generations, and Noah walked with God.

			And Noah begat three sons, Shem, Ham, and Japheth.

			The earth also was corrupt before God, and the earth was filled with violence.

			And God looked upon the earth, and, behold, it was corrupt; for all flesh had corrupted his way upon the earth.

			And God said unto Noah, The end of all flesh is come before me; for the earth is filled with violence through them; and, behold, I will destroy them with the earth.

			Make thee an ark of gopher wood; rooms shalt thou make in the ark, and shalt pitch it within and without with pitch.

			Genesis 6:1-14

		 			Prayer Concerning the Mysteries of God

			And it came to pass, when men began to multiply on the face of the earth, and daughters were born unto them, That the sons of God saw the daughters of men that they were fair; and they took them wives of all which they chose. And the Lord said, My spirit shall not always strive with man, for that he also is flesh: yet his days shall be an hundred and twenty years.

			Genesis 6:1-3

			1.	Pray that you will not shorten your life.

			God changed His mind about how long men would live on the earth when man worsened in his sinful state. Life and the shortening of life is very much connected to sin.

			For the wages of sin is death; but the gift of God is eternal life through Jesus Christ our Lord.

			Romans 6:23

			This is what caused God to give man only 10% of his life expectancy. Everybody after Adam’s sin could not live over 1000 years.

			Then when lust hath conceived, it bringeth forth sin: and sin, when it is finished, bringeth forth death.

			James 1:15

			Death is definitely connected to sin. It is the crossing of certain lines that provoke God.

			2.	Pray that you will never commit a sin that crosses a certain line.

			And God saw that the wickedness of man was great in the earth, and that every imagination of the thoughts of his heart was only evil continually. And it repented the Lord that he had made man on the earth, and it grieved him at his heart.

			Genesis 6:5-6

			When man crossed a certain line, God changed His mind. If you cross a certain line in relation to God, it changes God’s mind because God has a mind which changes.

			3. 	Pray concerning the lusts that are in you.

			Lust conceives sin and sin when it is finished brings forth death. We need to pray about the lusts that we have within us. It could be that there is a major lust to go to America and that desire can lead to your destruction. There may be a lurking desire within you. Your lust could be money. If you read my book, “Those Who Leave You1”, it mentions money as one of the reasons why people leave. There are so many lusts in us, it is scary.

			When God was angry with Adam and Eve, He said to Eve, “Your desire will be for a man.” A “desire” was a punishment because your desire will lead to many things and those things would be the things that would become a punishment for the woman. Sometimes we don’t even know what we desire until an opportunity comes and it manifests itself.

			Pray that God will deliver you from all lusts.

			4.	Pray for forgiveness of sins.

			There are many sins but certain sins should never be committed.

			Whatever sin it is that can change God’s mind about your life and calling, may God show mercy. Pray.

			Pray for the forgiveness of any sin that causes you to cross a certain line. Pray to be delivered from them. Whatever sin is very great in the sight of the Lord, may God forgive us in advance.

			1 (2011) Those Who Leave You; Parchment House, Accra,

			5.	Pray for God not to change His good mind about you.

			May God not change His good mind about you.

			6.	Pray that God will change His mind about any punishment that is destined against you.

				Your prayers can change God’s mind about any punishment you are about to receive. Your prayers are powerful.

			Thus the Lord God showed me, and behold, He was forming a locust-swarm when the spring crop began to sprout. And behold, the spring crop was after the king’s mowing. And it came about, when it had finished eating the vegetation of the land, that I said, “Lord God , please pardon! How can Jacob stand, For he is small?” The Lord changed His mind about this. “It shall not be,” said the Lord . Thus the Lord God showed me, and behold, the Lord God was calling to contend with them by fire, and it consumed the great deep and began to consume the farm land. Then I said, “Lord God , please stop! How can Jacob stand, for he is small?” The Lord changed His mind about this. “This too shall not be,” said the Lord God .

			Amos 7:1-6 NASB

			Prayer Concerning the Mysteries of Noah

			1.	Pray for the Spirit of life to be with you.

			And the Lord said, My spirit shall not always strive with man, for that he also is flesh: yet his days shall be an hundred and twenty years.

			Genesis 6:3

			It is the Holy Spirit who gives life. If God’s Spirit does not want to abide (strive) with us anymore, then it is the end of life! Remember, when man was just clay, God breathed His Spirit into man and he became a living soul and life came into him.

			Let’s pray for the Spirit of life, that the Spirit of God would be upon us.

			If ye then, being evil, know how to give good gifts unto your children: how much more shall your heavenly Father give the Holy Spirit to them that ask him?

			Luke 11:13

			Who also hath made us able ministers of the new testament; not of the letter, but of the spirit: for the letter killeth, but the spirit giveth life.

			2 Corinthians 3:6

			 Ask God for the Holy Spirit! The Holy Spirit who gives life!

			2. 	Pray to find grace in the eyes of the Lord.

			But Noah found grace in the eyes of the Lord.

			Genesis 6:8

			Pray that you would find grace in God’s eyes. You may be subject to evil things but you will be exempted as you find grace in God’s sight!

			3.	Pray that God will speak to you.

			And God said unto Noah, The end of all flesh is come before me; for the earth is filled with violence through them; and, behold, I will destroy them with the earth.

			Genesis 6:13

			When you find grace in the sight of God, God begins to speak with you. That is why sometimes, when I go to pray and I am not able to hear from God I feel like I have not found grace in His eyes.

			Pray for God to speak to you.

			4.	Pray to be close to God so He speaks to you about how He feels.

			God told Noah what He was feeling. When you relate with someone and the person doesn’t tell you what he thinks it means he isn’t close to you. Telling someone how you feel is different from giving instructions. When someone tells you how they feel the person is closer to you.

			I Knew His Mind

			I was once talking to somebody some years ago when the person asked me what I thought about something. But before I could answer, he gave me a response. It wasn’t an instruction to do anything but it was concerning another individual; and immediately I knew his mind on the issue. This is what makes you close to somebody.

			5.	Pray that you will do whatever instruction God gives you.

			Make thee an ark of gopher wood; rooms shalt thou make in the ark, and shalt pitch it within and without with pitch.

			Genesis 6:14

			6.	Pray that you become one of the agents of salvation. God is a God of salvation.

			The Lord liveth; and blessed be my rock; and let the God of my salvation be exalted.

			Psalm 18:46

			He shall receive the blessing from the Lord, and righteousness from the God of his salvation.

			Psalm 24:5

			Lead me in thy truth, and teach me: for thou art the God of my salvation; on thee do I wait all the day.

			Psalm 25:5

			Right from the beginning, God has been trying to save. God involved Noah in salvation. If you find grace in God’s eyes, He will involve you in salvation.

			Even when God was angry with Israel and decided to destroy them, He saved the young ones. God doesn’t destroy what He builds. God is always trying to save the very thing that He is judging. When God wants to save you, He will give you someone who will be a blessing to you. The work of the devil is to destroy. He wants to destroy the ark that is being built and he wants to destroy who is being sent to you.

			Plucked from the Fire

			John Wesley was once in a house that caught fire. When his family ran out of the house, John’s father realised that he was still in the building. John’s father ran back into the house in an attempt to save John and he did! Many years later, when John Wesley was very sick and thought he was going to die, he wrote what should be written on his epitaph, “A brand plucked out of the fire”, in remembrance of what had happened to him in his childhood.

			Pray that you will be an agent of salvation through soul winning and church planting.

			7.	Pray that your family will be used to build an ark.

			Many will be lost, but some people will be saved! God is the One who saves people. We are either joining hands with God or joining in with the devil. God is going to use you and your whole family to build an ark. Noah, his wife, and his children - Shem, Ham, Japheth were used to build an ark.

			Pray that your family will be an ark building family and not ark destroyers.

			8.	Bind the adversary that opposes the building of the ark.

			Bind the adversary that opposes any church building.

			The thief cometh not, but for to steal, and to kill, and to destroy: I am come that they might have life, and that they might have it more abundantly.

			John 10:10

			Verily I say unto you, Whatsoever ye shall bind on earth shall be bound in heaven: and whatsoever ye shall loose on earth shall be loosed in heaven.

			Matthew 18:18

			Anyone trying to crack the side of the ark to make it have a hole and sink is bound today!

			Lift your hands and declare judgement on all ark breakers and all ark attackers

			We bind the snake and the crocodile spirits that jump out of the water to destroy!

			We bind the hippopotamus spirit

			We bind every adversary spirit, every wicked and accusing spirit!

			We cast them down in the name of Jesus!

			Again I say unto you, That if two of you shall agree on earth as touching anything that they shall ask, it shall be done for them of my Father which is in heaven.

			Matthew 18:19

			We agree together that the work of Satan against ark building is bound in Jesus’ Name!

			Amen.





CHAPTER 9




Prayer for the Spirit of Revelation; Prayer to be an Ark Builder & Prayer for the Nations




		 			I counsel thee to buy of me gold tried in the fire, that thou mayest be rich; and white raiment, that thou mayest be clothed, and that the shame of thy nakedness do not appear; and anoint thine eyes with eyesalve, that thou mayest see.

			Revelation 3:18

		 			Prayer for the Spirit of Revelation

			I counsel thee to buy of me gold tried in the fire, that thou mayest be rich; and white raiment, that thou mayest be clothed, and that the shame of thy nakedness do not appear; and anoint thine eyes with eyesalve, that thou mayest see.

			Revelation 3:18

			The reality of Christianity is that we have to deal with both good and evil in their mature forms. We must be strong Christians to survive.

			Pray for the Spirit of revelation to be upon you. Pray that the Spirit who opens eyes may be upon you. Pray that the Spirit of dreams and visions should come upon you. Pray for a bright flash of understanding, wisdom and counsel.

			It is revelation like a brilliant flash that makes the difference.

			Get some oil and anoint your eyes. God is going to give you a fresh anointing for revelation. Revelation means to see - just like when the scientist made a discovery and shouted “Eureka”.

			Place the anointing oil on your eyes.

			Thank God for the anointing.

			Father thank You that You are anointing every eye. Take away whatever represents blindness in our lives. Let us experience new sight, be it visions, knowledge or dimensions of wisdom. Thank You, Lord! Reveal unto us what keeps us in darkness and keeps us limited.

			Lift your hands and pray for the Spirit of revelation.

			1.	Pray for the Spirit of revelation of weakness and foolishness.

			I want to give you a revelation. Do you want this revelation? Keep this in your heart as we pray. God will give you understanding.

			There is a “foolishness” that you practice that will overcome the devil and there is a “weakness” that you practice that will overcome the devil. God recommends His weak and foolish things to you because you can use it to overcome the devil. The Cross is the best example: the foolishness and weakness of God is the Cross.

			But God hath chosen the foolish things of the world to confound the wise; and God hath chosen the weak things of the world to confound the things which are mighty;

			1 Corinthians 1:27

			There will be a time in your life when you may have to walk like a fool. There will be a time when you may have to walk as though you are weak, but it will actually be stronger than the enemy. That is why sacrifice is so powerful. Humility, which appears weak, destroys Satan. Wives who receive this revelation are the happiest wives.

			Pray for God to reveal the “weak and foolish” things you need to embrace throughout your life and ministry.

			2.	Pray for the Spirit of revelation to see that a man is a gift.

			But withal prepare me also a lodging: for I trust that through your prayers I shall be given unto you.

			Philemon 1:22

			Through your prayers a particular person will be given into your life. Paul knew that the “gift” was a man.

			Wherefore he saith, When he ascended up on high, he led captivity captive, and gave gifts unto men. ...And he gave some, apostles; and some, prophets; and some, evangelists; and some, pastors and teachers;

			Ephesians 4:8, 11

			Apostles, prophets, evangelists, pastors and teachers are men that God has given as a gift. Think about the good people that God has given you in your life, they are gifts. Kenneth Hagin is a gift to me.

			God Gave Me Another Gift

			One morning I was praying and I saw my wife praying. She was listening to Derek Prince and I decided to also listen to him; and as I listened, I realised that God had given me another gift.

			Satan is always trying to throw a smoke screen so that certain gifts will be far away from you.

			Pray for God to reveal to you any man who is a gift in your life.

			3.	Pray for God to reveal to you all that you have to do.

			And Noah did according unto all that the Lord commanded him. And Noah was six hundred years old when the flood of waters was upon the earth.

			Genesis 7:5-6

			Noah had six hundred years of doing the will of God. Noah did all that the Lord asked him to do.

			Pray for God to reveal to you all that you have to do. You don’t want to get to heaven and find out there was a part you didn’t do. Pray that God will reveal to you all that you have to do. Lift your hands and pray!

			4.	Pray for God to reveal to you special skills that you need.

			Pray that special skills needed to obey God will be revealed to you.

			And Noah went in, and his sons, and his wife, and his sons’ wives with him, into the ark, because of the waters of the flood. Of clean beasts, and of beasts that are not clean, and of fowls, and of every thing that creepeth upon the earth, There went in two and two unto Noah into the ark, the male and the female, as God had commanded Noah.

			Genesis 7:7-9

			As you serve God, there are special skills you need to know and have, which will make a big difference in your life and ministry.

			Like Noah, it could be animal-catching skills or even computer skills. Noah was able to know every animal, some were female and some were male. The skill you may need may be to learn a language. It is a skill to pray.

			Lift your hands and thank God for special skills.

			5.	Pray for the Spirit of revelation on survival.

			And the rain was upon the earth forty days and forty nights. And the waters prevailed upon the earth an hundred and fifty days.

			Genesis 7:12, 24

			The time of Noah can be likened to a pandemic that affected the whole earth.

			God is giving you the right timing for wherever you have the wrong timing. Whatever crisis you don’t know and understand, God is revealing to you how to survive.

			Father thank You for revealing to Your children the grace for surviving; the grace to survive marital and financial crisis!

			Lord thank You for mercy and grace to escape.

			Pray for yourself and your household, for the revelation of survival. Lord, reveal to me how to survive every storm.

			Lift your hands and thank God that whatever the enemy throws at you, you are surviving by His mighty grace.

			Prayer for Yourself and Your Family to be Ark Builders

			We are going to pray on how you and your family can be ark builders. When you leave a church, your children become confused because they have friends in the church.
