Children love to be like other children, they don’t like to be the odd ones out. Your children will grow to be rebellious when they see you leave something you were once committed to.

			Noah and all his children were in the ark. You and all your children will remain in the ark as you pray. None of your children will be confused. Many times, children who experience broken homes are not normal.

			Pray that you and your family will be ark builders.

			1. 	Pray against the curse of Ham.

			Pray for God to deliver you from mocking your father. Even if your father is in prison, never mock at your father.

			And the sons of Noah, that went forth of the ark, were Shem, and Ham, and Japheth: and Ham is the father of Canaan. These are the three sons of Noah: and of them was the whole earth overspread. And Noah began to be an husbandman, and he planted a vineyard: And he drank of the wine, and was drunken; and he was uncovered within his tent. And Ham, the father of Canaan, saw the nakedness of his father, and told his two brethren without. And Shem and Japheth took a garment, and laid it upon both their shoulders, and went backward, and covered the nakedness of their father; and their faces were backward, and they saw not their father’s nakedness. And Noah awoke from his wine, and knew what his younger son had done unto him. And he said, Cursed be Canaan; a servant of servants shall he be unto his brethren.

			Genesis 9:18-25

			Everybody in the world comes from either Shem, Ham or Japheth. It is easy for us to do things that can provoke a curse. Many betrayers don’t realize that the effect of treachery or being like Absalom impacts their children in a devastating way. Some of us are children of rebellious people and unknowingly, the issues in our lives are as a result of it.

			2. 	Pray to God that you would not be a mocker.

			If you have been a mocker of your father, ask God to have mercy on you.

			A Bad Example

			One day I was preaching and was going to give an illustration when the Holy Spirit told me “Don’t use your father as a bad example.” The Holy Spirit saved me from being a mocker of my father. We can easily be like Ham.

			3.	Pray that you will be a child that covers rather than a child that exposes and embarrasses.

			 “My father doesn’t pay my school fees, someone else does. He is a useless man”. You can’t go outside and tell people bad things about your father!

			Pray to be a child that covers rather than a child that embarrasses. Pray for the grace to be a covering for your father.

			4.	Pray to never forget what your father has done for you.

			But Noah found grace in the eyes of the Lord.

			Genesis 6:8

			God didn’t speak to Shem, Ham or Japeth. Noah was the one that found grace in the eyes of the Lord. You may not know that it is your father that has found grace in God’s eyes and you are enjoying that grace. If Noah had not built an ark his children would have drowned.

			...ye all are partakers of my grace.

			Philippians 1:7

			5.	Pray that you never forget how God sees your father.

			For he shall be great in the sight of the Lord, ...

			Luke 1:15

			Your father can be great in the eyes of the Lord but maybe not in your eyes. Noah found grace with God and God chose him to build an ark. When you are a child in the house, sometimes you don’t see how great your father is. But God sees your father as great, especially in relation to you. That is why we have to honour our fathers.

			Pray for God to help you to see how great your father is - be it your biological or spiritual father.

			Honour thy father and mother; (which is the first commandment with promise;) That it may be well with thee, and thou mayest live long on the earth.

			Ephesians 6:2-3

			6.	Pray that you will never repay good with evil.

			Whoso rewardeth evil for good, evil shall not depart from his house.

			Proverbs 17:13

			7. 	Pray for forgiveness for being a son who repays good with evil.

			8.	Pray that you will never make a move against your father

			Peter betrayed Jesus but Judas organised the arrest of Jesus. That is the difference between Peter and Judas. That is what Absalom did to his father (a betrayal). Ham organised others to come and see his father’s nakedness.

			We are praying that we will never be used or rise up against our fathers.

			9.	Pray to be a blessed child and not a cursed child.

			Pray that you will never be categorised as a cursed son.

			Refuse anything that will make you a cursed son. God is having mercy on us.

			God is a kind God. If all children are meant to be cursed for their rebellion, we wouldn’t be here.

			Pray to be a blessed child and not a cursed child.

				Prayer for the Nations

			1.	Pray for Congo

			2.	Pray for Ethiopia

			3.	Pray for Madagascar

			4.	Pray for the Caribbean Island

			5.	Pray for Ghana

			6.	Pray for Nigeria

			7.	Pray for Ivory Coast

			8.	Pray for the Philippines





CHAPTER 10




Prayer for Strength; Prayer for the Blessings of Abraham & Prayer for the Nations




		 			We then that are strong ought to bear the infirmities of the weak, and not to please ourselves.

			Romans 15:1

			Now the Lord had said unto Abram, Get thee out of thy country, and from thy kindred, and from thy father’s house, unto a land that I will shew thee:

			And I will make of thee a great nation, and I will bless thee, and make thy name great; and thou shalt be a blessing:

			And I will bless them that bless thee, and curse him that curseth thee: and in thee shall all families of the earth be blessed.

			Genesis 12:1-3



		 			Prayer for Strength

			1.	Thank God for strength.

			We then that are strong ought to bear the infirmities of the weak, and not to please ourselves.

			Romans 15:1

			Your strength is shown when you help the weak, not when you discard or reject them. God doesn’t throw away weak things and neither should we. Strong countries look after the weak, the crippled, blind and deaf.

			Lift up your hands and thank God that you are one of the strong helping the weak. Every weak thing in your life will be strengthened.

			A bruised reed shall he not break, and smoking flax shall he not quench, till he send forth judgment unto victory.

			Matthew 12:20

			“Kill All the Mentally Ill”

			When Hitler came on the scene, one of the first stances that he took was that the mentally ill were a nuisance to society. Hitler later ordered and signed to have them all killed. This is an example of the strong not bearing the infirmities of the weak. It takes a lot to put up with people’s infirmities.

			What are the types of weaknesses that we have to put up with? Let us start with sin. When someone sins or gets into trouble, your strength is shown by your ability to accommodate the person rather than throw the person away.

			God is making us strong shepherds. People have problems and they are all part of your flock.

			The Strong Pastor

			I once visited a church filled with different types of people. I knew the background of some of these people and I knew they could not be Christians. However, the pastor had all these people in his church, pastors them, and is always preaching to them. It shows you the strength of the shepherd, that he doesn’t only accommodate perfect people.

			Brethren, if a man be overtaken in a fault, ye which are spiritual, restore such an one in the spirit of meekness; considering thyself, lest thou also be tempted.

			Galatians 6:1

			Spiritual people restore those who have faults. Strong armies have hospitals as part of the army to restore the wounded. Doing the work of God does not only include winning new souls but it is also fighting to look after the infirmities of those who are struggling.

			2.	Pray for God to strengthen you.

			3.	Pray for strength to look after those who are in sin.

			4.	Pray for strength to look after those who are messed up.

			5.	Pray for strength to look after those who are sick.

			6.	Pray for strength to look after those who have mental problems.

			7.	Pray for strength to look after those who are spiritually down.

			8.	Pray for strength to look after those who are handicapped.

			9.	Pray for strength to look after those that nobody likes.

			When I meet someone whom no one wants to marry, I always feel sad and want to help the person to get married.

			10.	Pray for strength to become what God wants you to become.

			Prayer for the Blessings of Abraham

			Christ hath redeemed us from the curse of the law, being made a curse for us: for it is written, Cursed is every one that hangeth on a tree: That the blessing of Abraham might come on the Gentiles through Jesus Christ; that we might receive the promise of the Spirit through faith.

			Galatians 3:13-14

			We see the blessings of Abraham in Genesis chapter 12 where the story of Abraham begins.

			1.	Pray for the blessing of separation from sin and wickedness in your family.

			Now the Lord had said unto Abram, Get thee out of thy country, and from thy kindred, and from thy father’s house, unto a land that I will shew thee:

			Genesis 12:1

			I believe that every family has some wickedness in it; so one of the first blessings of Abraham is to be separated from your family. You will be shocked to know that murderers, thieves and wicked people were all present in your family line. Anyone who comes from Ghana, and has family living near the castles, the slave trading posts, must have a connection to slave trade. I believe that God first of all separates us from family (and makes us different), and that is why you may hear some people say things like, “A wicked man like your father cannot give birth to an angelic child like you!”

			2.	Pray for Abraham’s blessing to be separated from the wickedness of your household.

			Wherefore, as by one man sin entered into the world, and death by sin; and so death passed upon all men, for that all have sinned:

			Romans 5:12

			One of the great blessings is to first come away and separate yourself from your father’s house and your country.

			3.	There are also sins of countries. Pray that you will be separated from the sins and wickedness of your country.

			You may never know what your country has been involved in. God wants to separate you from the bad thing that is in your country, so that it is never associated with you. You may not know the significance of this prayer till the day, when you are trying to get married, it is asked “Where is he from?” The answer to the question “Where is he from?” can immediately cause the answer to your marriage proposal to be “No”. Many times, when you go out to start churches as a missionary and you say that you are from a particular country, there are such negative reactions.

			4.	Pray for Abraham’s blessing to be separated from the wickedness of your country.

			5.	Pray that God will show you the land where you must live.

			Now the Lord had said unto Abram, Get thee out of thy country, and from thy kindred, and from thy father’s house, unto a land that I will shew thee:

			Genesis 12:1

			6.	Pray for God to show you the particular land, area or country that you should be in. This is the land where you will do well.

			When you don’t pray this type of prayer you can never be a missionary. Abraham’s blessing is, “Leave your country and I will show you were to go.” There are places with people that you don’t know and God says, “I will show you”. The world is full of evil and God says He will separate us. When you are not open to God’s separation, you will be like everyone else in the world.

			7.	Pray that God will make your family a great nation.

			And I will make of thee a great nation, and I will bless thee, and make thy name great; and thou shalt be a blessing:

			Genesis 12:2

			When you see someone who dies and leaves behind children, grandchildren and great grandchildren, it is a great nation.

			You will give birth to great children! Your family and your church family are becoming a great nation!

			8.	Pray for a blessing.

			And I will make of thee a great nation, and I will bless thee, and make thy name great; and thou shalt be a blessing:

			Genesis 12:2

			“I will bless thee.” These are short words; but they have ramifications and implications beyond your imagination.

			9.	Pray for the blessing of a great name.

			And I will make of thee a great nation, and I will bless thee, and make thy name great; and thou shalt be a blessing:

			Genesis 12:2

			Whatever this verse means, let it be so. When your name is mentioned and no one knows who you are, it is quite different from a name that is mentioned and known.

			Lift your hands! People will name their children after you, they will want to be associated with your name.

			10.	Pray to be a blessing.

			And I will make of thee a great nation, and I will bless thee, and make thy name great; and thou shalt be a blessing:

			Genesis 12:2

			When you are a blessing to somebody, that is when you are really strong and blessed. If you have everything in the world and everyone around you does not have anything, how does it help? When you are a selfish person, you are someone who is never happy. The real blessing is when you are able to be a blessing to others.

			Many years ago, I felt the Lord telling me to be a blessing to other churches and that was what gave birth to the Iron Sharpeneth Iron Conference (now Give Thyself Wholly Conference). The Healing Jesus [Evangelistic] Campaigns and the books I have written are a blessing to other churches.

			11.	Pray for the blessing of divine protection.

			And I will bless them that bless thee, and curse him that curseth thee: and in thee shall all families of the earth be blessed.

			Genesis 12:3

			The devil and his agents are always looking to attack a good thing but there is a protection God gives.

			Lift your hands and pray for this great protection. Pray for divine protection.

			Prayer for the Nations

			Even them will I bring to my holy mountain, and make them joyful in my house of prayer: their burnt offerings and their sacrifices shall be accepted upon mine altar; for mine house shall be called an house of prayer for all people.

			Isaiah 56:7

			Pray that labourers will be sent and that churches will be built. No nation shall be too strong.

			1.	Pray for Vietnam

			2.	Pray for China

			3.	Pray for Russia

			4.	Pray for South Korea

			5.	Pray for North Korea

			6.	Pray for Japan

			7.	Pray for Indonesia

			8.	Pray for the Philippines

			9.	Pray for Malaysia

			10.	Pray for Singapore

			11.	Pray for Cambodia

			12.	Pray for Thailand

			13.	Pray for Myanmar (Burma)

			14.	Pray for Bangladesh

			15.	Pray for India

				Thank God for the sending out of missionaries, thank God that it shall not cease.





CHAPTER 11




Prayer for Light & Prayer to Fulfil Your Ministry




		 			All things were made by him; and without him was not any thing made that was made. In him was life; and the life was the light of men.

			John 1:3-4

			And if ye shall despise my statutes, or if your soul abhor my judgments, so that ye will not do all my commandments, but that ye break my covenant: “I also will do this unto you; I will even appoint over you terror, consumption, and the burning ague, that shall consume the eyes, and cause sorrow of heart: and ye shall sow your seed in vain, for your enemies shall eat it.

			Leviticus 26:15 -16



		 			Why did God send punishments? Because the people did not obey or respect His laws.

			Pray for an answer to any questions on your mind. Pray for God to send His answer.

			Prayer for Seven Kinds of Light

			1. 	Pray for the entrance of God’s Word that brings light.

			The entrance of thy words giveth light; it giveth understanding unto the simple.

			Psalm 119:130

			2. 	Pray for the privilege of receiving a commandment from God that brings light.

			For the commandment is a lamp; and the law is light; and reproofs of instruction are the way of life.

			Proverbs 6:23

			3. 	Pray for judgment that brings light. If we do not judge ourselves, we will be judged.

			Therefore, have I hewed them by the prophets; I have slain them by the words of my mouth: and thy judgments are as the light that goeth forth.

			Hosea 6:5

			4. 	Pray for the light of the gospel that brings light.

			In whom the god of this world hath blinded the minds of them which believe not, lest the light of the glorious gospel of Christ, who is the image of God, should shine unto them.

			2 Corinthians 4:4

			5. 	Pray for the light of Jesus that brings life.

			He was not that Light, but was sent to bear witness of that Light. That was the true Light, which lighteth every man that cometh into the world.

			John 1:8-9

			Jesus was the TRUE light that lights everyone.

			6. 	Pray for the light that comes from following Jesus.

			All things were made by him; and without him was not any thing made that was made. In him was life; and the life was the light of men.

			John 1:3-4

			Jesus’ life is a direction for us. Anything that Jesus did is a direction for you. His life is a LIGHT.

			7. 	Pray for the light that comes through God’s servants.

			There is a man in thy kingdom, in whom is the spirit of the holy gods; and in the days of thy father light and understanding and wisdom, like the wisdom of the gods, was found in him; whom the king Nebuchadnezzar thy father, the king, I say, thy father, made master of the magicians, astrologers, Chaldeans, and soothsayers;

			Daniel 5:11

			Prayer to Fulfil Your Ministry

			1.	Pray that your ministry will be fulfilled straight away.

			And straightway he called them: and they left their father Zebedee in the ship with the hired servants, and went after him. And they went into Capernaum; and straightway on the Sabbath day he entered into the synagogue, and taught.

			Mark 1:20-21

			2.	Pray for the clarity of your ministry.

			Pray that God will let you know what you are supposed to do in order to finish your ministry.

			3.	Pray for your apostolic ministry to be fulfilled.

			Truly the signs of an apostle were wrought among you in all patience, in signs, and wonders, and mighty deeds.

			2 Corinthians 12:12

			Four Signs of Your Apostolic Ministry

			1. 	Patience: This means constancy, which means to be the same. You need constancy because constancy brings hardness.

			2. 	Signs: There must be supernatural signs that reveal your apostleship. One of these signs is a financial sign.

			3. 	Mighty deeds: Mighty deeds mean great accomplishments.

			4. 	Miracles: This means an omen.

			Communion Prayer

			The Bread

			Father, thank You for the Body of Jesus Christ.

			There is no one greater, no one with more light, revelation and direction than You Lord Jesus

			As we come before the Holy Communion, which is the Holy Interaction, we thank You for such a blessing

			Fill us with Your greatness as we partake of the body of Jesus Christ

			The body of Jesus Christ. (Eat It)

			The Blood

			May you be sanctified, cleansed of pollutions, of mistakes and errors

			May you be cleansed of things that connect you to the devil, things that link you to satan’s world

			 When I see the blood I will pass over

			May anything that has targeted you pass over because of the blood, which takes away the sins of the world. (Drink It)

			The Prophetic Blessings

			May the Lord remember you among the many people that are in the world

			May you be remembered

			As you are remembered, may you be lifted out of a dark hole

			The hole of death

			The hole of despair

			May the Lord be your Shepherd

			May He appear at the mouth of the hole

			May He pull you out of the miry clay

			May He wash you!

			May you rest in His loving arms!

			May He place you upon a rock to save!

			The Lord bless you, remember you and pick you from that hole and bless you forever

			I see the Lord pulling you out!

			May you be pulled out!

			May your despair end now by a blessing!

			The blessing of the shepherd comes your way now!

			The Good Shepherd stretches out His hand to you in love

			Receive Him and receive this great blessing in your life!

			Amen.





CHAPTER 12




Prayer for the Holy Spirit; Prayer for the Blessings of Zebedee & Prayer to Bind Demons




		 			If ye then, being evil, know how to give good gifts unto your children: how much more shall your heavenly Father give the Holy Spirit to them that ask him?

			Luke 11:13

		 			Prayer to be Filled with the Holy Spirit

			1.	Pray for the Spirit of Humility.

			2.	Pray for the Spirit of Holiness.

			3.	Pray for the Spirit of Faith.

			We having the same spirit of faith, according as it is written, I believed, and therefore, have I spoken; we also believe, and therefore, speak;

			2 Corinthians 4:13

			4.	Pray for the Spirit of Wisdom and Revelation.

			5.	Pray for the Spirit of Love.

			Love is patient, love is kind and is not jealous; love does not brag and is not arrogant, does not act unbecomingly; it does not seek its own, is not provoked, does not take into account a wrong suffered, does not rejoice in unrighteousness, but rejoices with the truth; bears all things, believes all things, hopes all things, endures all things.

			1 Corinthians 13:4-7 (NASB)

			Desiring to be teachers of the law; understanding neither what they say, nor whereof they affirm.

			1 Timothy 1:7

			Prayer for the Blessings of Zebedee

			And when he had gone a little farther thence, he saw James the son of Zebedee, and John his brother, who also were in the ship mending their nets. And straightway he called them: and they left their father Zebedee in the ship with the hired servants, and went after him.

			Mark 1:19-20

			Seven Aspects of the Zebedee Family Anointing

			1.	Pray to be a wealthy family.

			2.	Pray to be a family that does not resist the ministry.

			3.	Pray to be a family that esteems ministry greater than secular work.

			4.	Pray to be a family where the father and mother support the work of God.

			And many women were there beholding afar off, which followed Jesus from Galilee, ministering unto him: Among which was Mary Magdalene, and Mary the mother of James and Joses, and the mother of Zebedee’s children.

			Matthew 27:55-56

			Within every family, if the father and the mother are not united, the children will also be divided.

			5.	Pray to be a family that has holy ambitions for your children in the ministry.

			Then came to him the mother of Zebedee’s children with her sons, worshipping him, and desiring a certain thing of him. And he said unto her, What wilt thou? She saith unto him, Grant that these my two sons may sit, the one on thy right hand, and the other on the left, in thy kingdom.

			Matthew 20:20-21

			6.	Pray to be a family that supports Jesus by traveling with Him.

			Lift your hands and pray that you will be someone who supports Jesus by traveling with Him.

			And many women were there beholding afar off, which followed Jesus from Galilee, ministering unto him: Among which was Mary Magdalene, and Mary the mother of James and Joses, and the mother of Zebedee’s children.

			Matthew 27:55-56

			7. Pray to be a family that supports Jesus in His suffering and even after His death.

			And when the sabbath was past, Mary Magdalene, and Mary the mother of James, and Salome, had bought sweet spices, that they might come and anoint him.

			Mark 16:1

			Everybody wants to be winner, but the people who are with you after you have lost are your real friends.

			Prayer to Bind Demons

			1.	Pray concerning the rooms in your house, the rooms of your mind, your emotions, your prayer life and your devotion to God.

			2.	Pray concerning the rooms of your decisions, your relationships, your health, your destiny, your ministry and your calling.

			3.	Pray and bind any demon that will try and occupy any of these rooms.

			4.	Pray and curse every satanic attack on any of these rooms.

			5.	Pray and block their access, bind demons that want to divert and prevent your destiny.

			Bind every power that is resisting your ministry and calling.

			6.	Pray for the spirit of transparency, the spirit of truth, forgiveness and patience.

			Pray for these spirits.

			7. 	Pray and break every demon that causes lack of truth, patience, forgiveness and transparency.

			8.	Pray to bind the spirit of duplicity, hypocrisy and duality.

			Pray and bind this darkness in your life. Bind the presence of these demons.

			9.	Pray to bind the spirit of disappointment, despair, dysfunction and discouragement.

			Pray for the victory over these demons. The demons of disappointment, despair, dysfunction and disappointment. We bind the power of hopelessness!

			Communion Prayer

			The Bread

			Father we come bowing before Your Holy Bread (the body of Jesus Christ)

			Let the greatest blessing be upon Your children as we take Your Body

			Let every disappointment in this life bow

			May despair, hopelessness and discouragement bow

			Let all dysfunctional things give in and give up!

			Let the deteriorations of our life bow!

			The body of Jesus Christ. (Eat It)

			The Blood

			Whatever represents evidence against you

			Whatever represents an evil witness against you

			Whatever represents your sins, your mistakes, transgressions, your iniquities

			Because of the blood receive forgiveness and mercy!

			Just as the Egyptians were washed away by the Red Sea, through this blood, whatever stands against you are also washed away by the blood

			Receive the blood of Jesus

			Thank You Father, that You really are in heaven

			We entrust ourselves to You, Lord

			The blood of Jesus speaks for you today! (Drink It)

			The Prophetic Blessings

			Receive power in your life right now!

			Receive the manifestation of power!

			Whatever is missing, find it today

			Whatever is gone out of your hand comes back

			Whatever flees away, returns to you

			May this blessing accompany you today and everyday

			Return to the Lord and you shall receive!

			Seek Him and you shall find Him!

			Draw near to Him and He shall draw near to you!

			For this is the greatest blessing of God: His presence in your life

			Let it never depart from you

			Your sins, your transgressions and your iniquities are washed away by the blood of Jesus

			Be blessed! I bless you with the blessing of the Lord

			May you enjoy His blessing in your life today and forevermore

			Amen.





CHAPTER 13




Prayer for the Ministry of Jesus; Prayer for the Divinely Appointed People for Your Life; Prayer to Bind the Enemy & Prayer for the Tsalach Anointing




		 			And he was there in the wilderness forty days, tempted of Satan; and was with the wild beasts; and the angels ministered unto him.

			Now after that John was put in prison, Jesus came into Galilee, preaching the gospel of the kingdom of God,

			And saying, The time is fulfilled, and the kingdom of God is at hand: repent ye, and believe the gospel.

			Now as he walked by the sea of Galilee, he saw Simon and Andrew his brother casting a net into the sea: for they were fishers.

			Mark 1:13-16

		 			Prayer for the Ministry of Jesus

			There is therefore, now no condemnation to them which are in Christ Jesus, who walk not after the flesh, but after the Spirit. ...For to be carnally minded is death; but to be spiritually minded is life and peace.

			Romans 8:1, 6

			John’s death was a trigger for Jesus’s ministry to begin. In every event you must see the supernatural side. What event is triggering something supernatural for you? What spiritual triggers are happening in your life?

			Lord, help us to be spiritually minded because spiritual-mindedness is life and peace.

			1.	Pray for the Spirit of revelation, for spiritual understanding about what is happening.

			Help me to be spiritually minded Lord!

			a)	Father, give me the Spirit of revelation to understand the events that are happening around me.

			b)	Father, give me the Spirit of revelation to understand the events happening in my life.

			c)	Father, give me the Spirit of revelation to understand the events happening in the world now.

			2. 	Pray about the time to fulfil these four things. Do we believe that Jesus is the centre and the main thing? When you don’t know what to do, look at Jesus.

			And saying, The time is fulfilled, and the kingdom of God is at hand: repent ye, and believe the gospel.

			Mark 1:15

			a)		The time is fulfilled.

			b)		The kingdom of God is at hand.

			c)		Repent or change - The things I used to do, I do them 		no more.

			d)		Believe the gospel.

			Prayer That You Will Meet All Your Divinely Appointed People

			We will not accomplish what we are supposed to accomplish without certain people. These people are connected to our destinies. They are destiny changers.

			Pray that whoever God has chosen for your destiny would be found.

				Father, guide me to meet my divinely appointed people.

			Now as he walked by the sea of Galilee, he saw Simon and Andrew his brother casting a net into the sea: for they were fishers.

			Mark 1:16

			The people we meet are very critical to our lives and ministry. There are certain people you meet in your life who are divinely appointed people. God wants you to meet these people the way Jesus met Simon Peter and his brother, Andrew.

			7 Divinely Appointed People in Your Life

			i.		Your divinely appointed friends in your life

			ii.		Your divinely appointed pastors in your life

			iii.		Your divinely appointed prophets in your life

			iv.		Your divinely appointed wife or husband in your life

			v.		Your divinely appointed disciples and followers in your 		life

			vi.		Your divinely appointed helpers in your life

			vii.	Your divinely appointed pillars of your life - these are 		solid pillars for your life

			Who is in your life right now?

			3. 	 Pray concerning your Simon and Andrew. Andrew is the “manly” person in your life.

			Pray that you will meet your “Andrew(s)”.

			One of his disciples, Andrew, Simon Peter’s brother, saith unto him, There is a lad here, which hath five barley loaves, and two small fishes: but what are they among so many?

			John 6:8-9

			a.	Andrew is a strong man.

			b.	Andrew is a man of discipline.

			c.	Andrew is a practical man.

			d.	Andrew is a problem solver.

			e.	Andrew introduces people to Jesus.

			He first findeth his own brother Simon, and saith unto him, We have found the Messias, which is, being interpreted, the Christ. And he brought him to Jesus. And when Jesus beheld him, he said, Thou art Simon the son of Jona: thou shalt be called Cephas, which is by interpretation, A stone.

			John 1:41-42

			4.	Pray for someone who will bring you closer to Jesus.

			Pray that you will meet your Andrew, who will bring you closer to Jesus.

			And there were certain Greeks among them that came up to worship at the feast: The same came therefore, to Philip, which was of Bethsaida of Galilee, and desired him, saying, Sir, we would see Jesus. Philip cometh and telleth Andrew: and again Andrew and Philip tell Jesus.

			John 12:20-22

			5.	Pray for 7 things that will make people choose you (the way Peter was chosen).

			i.	Pray for the anointing of Peter.

			ii.	Pray that you will be someone who is good company.

			iii.	Pray that you will be someone who can be disciplined or rebuked without apology.

			iv.	Pray that you will become like Peter, someone who is ready to try new things.

			But straightway Jesus spake unto them, saying, Be of good cheer; it is I; be not afraid. And Peter answered him and said, Lord, if it be thou, bid me come unto thee on the water.

			Matthew 14:27-28

			v.	Pray that you will be someone who flows and is obedient.

			Notwithstanding, lest we should offend them, go thou to the sea, and cast an hook, and take up the fish that first cometh up; and when thou hast opened his mouth, thou shalt find a piece of money: that take, and give unto them for me and thee.

			Matthew 17:27

			vi.	Pray that you will be someone who sees things in the right way.

			When Jesus came into the coasts of Caesarea Philippi, he asked his disciples, saying, Whom do men say that I the Son of man am?

CHAPTER 14




Prayer to Become a Beloved Son & Prayer against Temptation




		 			And there came a voice from heaven, saying, Thou art my beloved Son, in whom I am well pleased.

			And immediately the spirit driveth him into the wilderness.

			And he was there in the wilderness forty days, tempted of Satan; and was with the wild beasts; and the angels ministered unto him.

			Mark 1:11-13



		 			Prayer to Become a Beloved Son

			1.	Pray for grace and mercy to become a true, beloved son.

			Jesus is the way to become a beloved son - look unto Jesus.

			1.	You will find the WAY from Jesus

			2.	You will find the TRUTH from Jesus

			3.	You will find the LIGHT from Jesus

			And there came a voice from heaven, saying, Thou art my beloved Son, in whom I am well pleased.

			Mark 1:11

			Seven Steps to Becoming a Beloved Son

			i.	Pray that you will be humbled so that you will be a beloved son.

			ii.	Pray that you will accept not to be known so that you will be a beloved son.

			iii.	Pray that you will be submitted to God.

			iv.	Pray that you will be into baptism; ie going deep into things. None of the minor and major Prophets was into baptism but Jesus was into baptism.

			v.	Pray that you will be respectful to your man of God so that you will be a beloved son.

				Respect the person that God has put in front of you.

				Respect depends on your culture because you may be “culturally” respectful but not genuinely respectful. Therefore, pray to become genuinely respectful.

			vi.	Pray that you will be a missionary so that you will be a beloved son.

			vii. Pray that you will be led by the Spirit so that you can be a 	 beloved son.

				There is one point in the points above that is especially for you. Identify it and pray about it.

			2. 	Pray for the Spirit of revelation to become a beloved son.

			We need the Spirit of revelation to reveal things to us. We need the Spirit of revelation to become beloved sons. Once you have been a traitor, it is not easy to be accepted.

			There is a principle in law - “you ought to have known” - that can be used to charge you for crime. “You ought to have known” all these points that make you beloved son. Therefore, pray for the Spirit of revelation so that when any of these points are happening, you will notice them.

			Deploying Matthew 18:19 in Prayer

			When you are praying, there are times that you have to deploy Matthew 18:19:

			Again I say unto you, That if two of you shall agree on earth as touching any thing that they shall ask, it shall be done for them of my Father which is in heaven.

			Matthew 18:19

			3. 	Pray and agree with somebody so that you will become a beloved son.

			Lord help me to become a beloved son.

			Prayer against the Spirit of Temptation

			 The devil is the one behind temptations. The devil will test us against everything God wants to do with our lives.

			Then was Jesus led up of the Spirit into the wilderness to be tempted of the devil.

			Matthew 4:1

			1.	Pray and bind the spirit of temptation that tests you to see if you can do what you say you can do.

			2.	Pray for immediate demolition of satanic structures and obstacles to test your calling and your life.

				I demand immediate demolition of satanic structures and obstacles. I bind anything designed to test my strength and commitment.

			3.	Pray and bind the spirit of temptation that wants to prove to you that you are of a low quality.

				Satan is all out to declare you as not good enough to do what you have said you want to do. Therefore, pray against this trial to test you to see if you are good enough to do what you want to do for God. Bind this spirit of temptation.

			4.	Pray and bind the spirit of temptation that makes you think in a bad way.

			And as he said these things unto them, the scribes and the Pharisees began to urge him vehemently, and to provoke him to speak of many things: Laying wait for him, and seeking to catch something out of his mouth, that they might accuse him.

			Luke 11:53-54

			5.	Pray against any power to make you think in bad way.

			6.	Pray and bind the spirit of temptation that tries you to see how you will behave yourself.

			7.	Pray and bind the demon that follows you to say “Aha”.

			Also, thou son of man, prophesy unto the mountains of Israel, and say, Ye mountains of Israel, hear the word of the Lord: Thus saith the Lord God; Because the enemy hath said against you, Aha, even the ancient high places are ours in possession:

			Ezekiel 36:1-2

			And it came to pass in the eleventh year, in the first day of the month, that the word of the Lord came unto me, saying, Son of man, because that Tyrus hath said against Jerusalem, Aha, she is broken that was the gates of the people: she is turned unto me: I shall be replenished, now she is laid waste:

			Ezekiel 26:1-2

			8.	Pray and bind the spirit of temptation that puts a test to your feelings (hatred, anger, lust, wickedness, etc.)

				I clear the atmosphere around me for anything that causes hatred, lust, anger wickedness in the name of Jesus!

			9.	Pray and bind the spirit of temptation that puts to test your judgment. Bind the demon that maliciously mocks you AFTER you have taken your decision.

				I declare my feet shall not enter into evil decisions!

				I declare that the right decisions would be taken!

				I bind the spirit that tries to influence me to take the wrong option or decision!

			10.	Pray and bind the spirit of craftiness. Craftiness means to be skillful in deceiving. You will never know that you are being manipulated. Financially, mortgages are the schemes of banks that deceive many people and it is very crafty.

				I release myself from deceptions and manipulations!

				I cleanse my heart, my mind from demonic craftiness in the name of Jesus.

				I renounce all satanic craftiness!

				I retreat from it!

				I escape from all demonic entanglement!

				I escape from every financial pit into which I have fallen!

			11.	Pray and bind the spirit of manipulation.

			12.	Pray and bind the spirit of infiltration and encroachment.

				Today marks the end of every satanic infiltration and encroachment!

				We bind satanic infiltrations on our lands.

				We bind satanic encroachment on our lands and we sack all infiltrations and encroachments on our land.

			Communion Prayer

			The Bread

			We partake of the life of Jesus

			This life was manifested to us in flesh

			Lord, as we receive this life, let the good decisions of this life, the blessedness of this life of Jesus, be part of our lives

			We honour Your body, and we honour Your name

			The body of Jesus Christ. (Eat It)

			The Blood

			The blood that speaks better things is the same blood that covers your sins.

			It washes away your grievous mistakes

			Receive of the blood, forgiveness, cleansing, sanctification washing, and regeneration

			The blood of Jesus Christ. (Drink It)

			The Prophetic Blessings

			Marvelous things are taking place in your life!

			May a marvelous blessing approach you with bright lights!

			Receive the marvelous help from God!

			Receive a marvelous rescue of the Lord!

			Whatever is a tormenting and plaguing issue of concern in

			your life be healed of it now in the name of Jesus!

			Receive help from God!

			The Lord bless thee

			The Lord bless all that are my children

			The Lord keep thee

			The Lord make His face shine

			The Lord be pleased with you, and be gracious unto you

			The Lord lift up His countenance, reverse His anger, and change His mind that was negative about you

			The Lord give you peace on every tormenting turmoil in your life

			The Lord give you peace and help you

			Bless them, help them, and remember them

			Amen.

Prayer to Believe in the Lord and His Prophets & Prayer to Stop the Devil in His Tracks




		 			 And they rose early in the morning, and went forth into the wilderness of Tekoa: and as they went forth, Jehoshaphat stood and said, Hear me, O Judah, and ye inhabitants of Jerusalem; Believe in the Lord your God, so shall ye be established; believe his prophets, so shall ye prosper.

			And they rose early in the morning, and went forth into the wilderness of Tekoa: and as they went forth, Jehoshaphat stood and said, Hear me, O Judah, and ye inhabitants of Jerusalem; Believe in the Lord your God, so shall ye be established; believe his prophets, so shall ye prosper.

			There was a little city, and few men within it; and there came a great king against it, and besieged it, and built great bulwarks against it: Now there was found in it a poor wise man, and he by his wisdom delivered the city; yet no man remembered that same poor man.

CHAPTER 16




Praying with Jesus - Prayer for the Spirit to Come Upon You Like a Dove & Prayer to Bind the Devils that are Following You




And it came to pass in those days, that Jesus came from Nazareth of Galilee, and was baptized of John in Jordan. And straightway coming up out of the water, he saw the heavens opened, and the Spirit like a dove descending upon him:

			Mark 1:9-10

		 			Prayer for the Holy Spirit to Come Upon You Like a Dove

			1. 	Pray for the Holy Spirit like a dove, to descend upon you. A person takes on the characteristic of the spirit that influences him.

			And he was casting out a devil, and it was dumb. And it came to pass, when the devil was gone out, the dumb spake; and the people wondered.

			Luke 11:14

			If we pray for the Spirit of holiness, we will become holy. It is a bad spirit in us that would makes us rebellious towards authority. It is a bad spirit that would make you see a good person as a hard man.

			Then he which had received the one talent came and said, Lord, I knew thee that thou art an hard man, reaping where thou hast not sown, and gathering where thou hast not strawed:

			Matthew 25:24

			How is it that two servants in the story in Matthew 25 didn’t see their master as a hard man? It is simply because they did not have a rebellious spirit.

			Father give me the Holy Spirit. Let Him come upon my life. Let Him come upon my ministry. Help us to understand why we need the Holy Spirit. Father, help me.

			2. Pray for the Spirit of holiness.

			And declared to be the Son of God with power, according to the spirit of holiness, by the resurrection from the dead:

			Romans 1:4

			What do you want to declare? Declare to be holy today.

			Pray for the Spirit of holiness, to make us holy.

			What Can Be on Us that Will Not Make the Dove Come Upon Us?

			And thou shalt speak unto the children of Israel, saying, This shall be an holy anointing oil unto me throughout your generations. Upon man’s flesh shall it not be poured, neither shall ye make any other like it, after the composition of it: it is holy, and it shall be holy unto you.

			Exodus 30:31-32

			The anointing cannot be poured on the “flesh”. The anointing does not like certain things. Galatians 5:19-21 lists things which prevent the anointing from landing on us.

			Now the works of the flesh are manifest, which are these; Adultery, fornication, uncleanness, lasciviousness, Idolatry, witchcraft, hatred, variance, emulations, wrath, strife, seditions, heresies, Envyings, murders, drunkenness, revellings, and such like: of the which I tell you before, as I have also told you in time past, that they which do such things shall not inherit the kingdom of God.

			Galatians 5:19-21

			Prayer Concerning Seventeen Things that Frighten the Dove (The Holy Spirit) from Your Life

			1. 	Adultery

			Every desire for somebody’s husband or wife must die in you.

			Pray that you will overcome ADULTERY in your life because it frightens the Dove.

			2. 	Fornication

			Pray that you will overcome FORNICATION in your life because it frightens the Dove.

			3. 	Uncleanness

			Pray that you will overcome UNCLEANNESS in your life because it frightens the Dove.

			4. 	Lasciviousness

			Pray that you will overcome LASCIVIOUSNESS in your life because it frightens the Dove. Pray for all these things to go out of your life.

			5. 	Idolatry

			Note that anything can become too precious to you and it can become an idol in your life.

			Pray that you will overcome IDOLATRY in your life because it frightens the Dove.

			6. 	Witchcraft

			Pray that you will overcome WITCHCRAFT in your life because it frightens the Dove.

			7. 	Hatred

			Pray that you will overcome HATRED in your life because it frightens the Dove.

			8. 	Variance

			Pray that you will overcome VARIANCE in your life because it frightens the Dove. Variance is quarrelsomeness.

			9. 	Emulations

			Pray that you will overcome EMULATIONS in your life because it frightens the Dove.

			Emulation is contentious rivalry, factions, groups, etc. These things do not make the church grow. You also do not do well in life because you don’t know where your divine helper will come from.

			10. 	Wrath

			Pray that you will overcome WRATH in your life because it frightens the Dove.

			11. Strife

			Pray that you will overcome STRIFE in your life because it frightens the Dove.

			12. Seditions

			Pray that you will overcome SEDITIONS in your life because it frightens the Dove.

			13. Heresies

			Pray that you will overcome HERESIES in your life because it frightens the Dove.

			14. Envyings

			Pray that you will overcome ENVYINGS in your life because it frightens the Dove.

			15. Murders

			Pray that you will overcome MURDERS in your life because it frightens the Dove.

			16. Drunkenness

			Pray that you will overcome DRUNKENNESS in your life because it frightens the Dove.

			17. 	Revellings

			Pray that you will overcome REVELLINGS in your life that frighten the Dove.

			Today marks the end of anything that drives away the anointing!

			Prayer to Bind the Devils that are Following You

			Name and bind the devils that are following you.

			Declare judgment upon them and cast them out. It may be procrastination, it may be poverty, it may be saying and confessing negative things. It may be near-success syndrome, it may be laziness, it may be the spirit of whoredoms, or it may be the spirit of divorce.

			They will not frame their doings to turn unto their God: for the spirit of whoredoms is in the midst of them, and they have not known the Lord.

			Hosea 5:4

			Neither give place to the devil.

			Ephesians 4:27

			 				Pray and break every demonic attachment to your life.

				Pray and block all access points to your life and your home.

				Pray and renounce all permissions that you have granted the enemy in your life. Renounce all those permissions.



				Pray and bind demons of secrecy and deception that make you a pretender.

				Pray and bind the spirit of hypocrisy. Hypocrite means actor.

				Pray and bind the spirit of poverty.



			Continuous struggle for financial resource or financial hardship - I release it from my life!

			Communion Prayer

			The Bread

			Father we lift up before You the holy bread that symbolizes the death of Christ

			Lord, if You had not come to die for us, we would have no way to explain ourselves and our sins

			Our sins and mistakes stand against us

			They are evidence against us; but through the eternal sacrifice of the Cross, payment has been made for the things we have done and those that we will do

			The manifestation of healing and deliverance comes to us now!

			The cup of blessing comes with this great blessing - all evidence against you is wiped out

			Whatever your need, that you have not prayed for, may it be given to you today!

CHAPTER 17





Prayer for Guidance; Prayer for the Ability to be Impressed; Prayer to Bind Devils & Prayer for the Shield of Faith




And there went out unto him all the land of Judaea, and they of Jerusalem, and were all baptized of him in the river of Jordan, confessing their sins.

			Mark 1:5

		 			 		 			Prayer that God Will Guide You on Whatever Journey You are Supposed to Make

			Father, guide me like You guided Jesus to where the anointing was.

			Seven Things You Need for the Journey to Baptism

			And it came to pass in those days, that Jesus came from Nazareth of Galilee, and was baptized of John in Jordan.

			Mark 1:9

			1. 	Pray to have SPIRITUAL RECOGNITION of men. Don’t wait to lose a person before you recognize who he is.

			Ten People You Must be able to Recognize

			a.		You must be able to recognize an anointed person.

			b.		You must be able to recognize a wise person.

			c.		You must recognize a prophet.

			d.		You must be able to recognize a saviour.

			e.		You must be able to recognize a destroyer.

			f.		You must be able to recognize a thief.

			Receive us; we have wronged no man, we have corrupted no man, we have defrauded no man.

			2 Corinthians 7:2

			g.		You must be able to recognize a liar.

			A lying tongue hateth those that are afflicted by it; and a flattering mouth worketh ruin.

			Proverbs 26:28

			h.		You must be able to recognize an accuser.

			i.		You must be able to recognize a traitor.

			j.		You must be able to recognize a lover.

			2.	Pray to have SPIRITUAL HUMILITY on the journey to baptism.

			And it came to pass in those days, that Jesus came from Nazareth of Galilee, and was baptized of John in Jordan.

CHAPTER 18




Prayer to Give Thanks; Prayer Concerning Sins; & Prayer Concerning our Lord Jesus Christ’s Ministry - Signs and Types of Baptism




The voice of one crying in the wilderness, Prepare ye the way of the Lord, make his paths straight. John did baptize in the wilderness, and preach the baptism of repentance for the remission of sins.

			And there went out unto him all the land of Judaea, and they of Jerusalem, and were all baptized of him in the river of Jordan, confessing their sins. And John was clothed with camel’s hair, and with a girdle of a skin about his loins; and he did eat locusts and wild honey;

			And preached, saying, There cometh one mightier than I after me, the latchet of whose shoes I am not worthy to stoop down and unloose. I indeed have baptized you with water: but he shall baptize you with the Holy Ghost.

			Mark 1:3-8

		 			 		 			Prayer to Give Thanks

			Give thanks to God before you start praying about the ministry of our Lord Jesus Christ. Give thanks for His mighty acts.

			Thank You Lord for Your mighty acts.

			Prayer Concerning Your Sins

			Many times did he deliver them; but they provoked him with their counsel, and were brought low for their iniquity.

			Psalm 106:43

			This is the way to be in ministry. The way is to pray concerning your sins.

			1.	Pray about your sins, confess your sins.

			2.	Pray about your iniquities, confess your iniquities. What three iniquities can you identify in your life?

			3.	Pray about your transgressions, confess your transgressions.

			4.	Pray and confess your sins and debts.

			5.	Pray about your sins so that you will not be brought low according to Psalm 106:43.

			Identify ten tendencies in your life that must end. It may be a tendency to steal, a tendency towards sexual perversions or a tendency towards lying.

			Psalm 46:43 says they were brought low for their iniquities (tendencies). “Tendency” means you have not done it yet, but it is in you. Our tendencies can end our lives.

			Pray specifically about three iniquities in your life and ask God to forgive you.

			Purge me of my iniquities so that I will not be brought low. Deliver me a Lord, forgive me Lord and have mercy on me Lord!

			Prayer Concerning Our Lord Jesus Christ’s Ministry

			The voice of one crying in the wilderness, Prepare ye the way of the Lord, make his paths straight. John did baptize in the wilderness, and preach the baptism of repentance for the remission of sins.

			And there went out unto him all the land of Judaea, and they of Jerusalem, and were all baptized of him in the river of Jordan, confessing their sins. And John was clothed with camel’s hair, and with a girdle of a skin about his loins; and he did eat locusts and wild honey;

			And preached, saying, There cometh one mightier than I after me, the latchet of whose shoes I am not worthy to stoop down and unloose. I indeed have baptized you with water: but he shall baptize you with the Holy Ghost.

			Mark 1:3-8

			Six Signs that Show that You Believe in Baptism

			Baptism means you are entering into something wholly and totally.

			Meditate upon these things; give thyself wholly to them; that thy profiting may appear to all.

			1 Timothy 4:15

			It means:

			a.	To do something that is evident to all.

			b.	To do something that makes you go deeper.

			c.	To do something that you have to die for.

			d.	To do something that shows on the outward.

			e.	To do something that is humbling.

			f.	To do something that requires you to trust others.

			Six Types of Baptisms

			1. 	Baptism of repentance from sins

			This means to make a U-turn. You move away completely from sin, this is WHOLE repentance.

			Seven Signs That Show I Believe in the Baptism of Repentance

			a.	I have entered into righteousness wholly.

			b.	I do righteousness that is evident to all.

			c.	I go deeper into righteousness.

			d.	Something dies in me.

			e.	I do righteousness that shows on the outside.

			f.	I am prepared to do something that is humbling.

			g.	I do something that shows that I trust others.

			2.	Baptism into Jesus Christ

			Seven Signs That Show I Believe in Baptism into Christ

			a.	I have entered into Christ wholly.

			b.	My service to Christ is evident to all.

			c.	I go deeper into Christ.

			d.	Something dies in me for Christ to be in me.

			e.	The Christ in me shows on the outside.

			f.	I am prepared to do something that is humbling to be in Christ.

			g.	I do something that shows that I trust others in Christ.

			3.	Baptism into Ministry

			Seven Signs That Show I Believe in Baptism into Ministry

			But Jesus answered and said, Ye know not what ye ask. Are ye able to drink of the cup that I shall drink of, and to be baptized with the baptism that I am baptized with? They say unto him, We are able. And he saith unto them, Ye shall drink indeed of my cup, and be baptized with the baptism that I am baptized with: but to sit on my right hand, and on my left, is not mine to give, but it shall be given to them for whom it is prepared of my Father.

			Matthew 20:22-23

			a.	I have entered into the ministry wholly.

			b. 	My Ministry is evident to all.

			c. 	I go deeper into the ministry.

			d. 	Something dies in me for me to be in ministry.

			e. 	My baptism into the ministry shows on the outside.

			f. 	I am prepared to do something that is humbling to be in ministry.

			g.	I do things that show that I trust others in the ministry.

			4. 	Baptism into a man of God

			And were all baptized unto Moses in the cloud and in the sea;

			1 Corinthians 10:2

			Seven Signs that Show I believe in Baptism into a Man of God

			a.	I have entered into my man of God wholly.

			b.	My service to my man of God is evident to all.

			c.	I go deeper into my man of God.

			d.	Something dies in me for me to be baptized into the man of God.

			e.	My baptism into my man of God shows on the outside.

			f.	I am prepared to do something that is humbling.

			g.	I do things that show that I trust my man of God.

			5. 	Baptism into the Holy Ghost

			I indeed have baptized you with water: but he shall baptize you with the Holy Ghost.

			Mark 1:8

			Seven Signs That Show I Believe in Baptism into the Holy Ghost

			a.	I have entered into the Holy Ghost wholly.

			b.	My service in the Holy Ghost is evident to all.

			c.	I go deeper into the Holy Ghost.

			d.	Something dies in me for the Holy Ghost to be in me.

			e.	The Holy Spirit in me shows on the outside.

			f.	I am prepared to do something that is humbling to be baptized in the Holy Ghost.

			g.	I do things that shows that I trust others in the Holy 	Ghost.

			6. 	Baptism into the Body of Christ

			For by one Spirit are we all baptized into one body, whether we be Jews or Gentiles, whether we be bond or free; and have been all made to drink into one Spirit.

			1 Corinthians 12:13

			For he that eateth and drinketh unworthily, eateth and drinketh damnation to himself, not discerning the Lord’s body.

			1 Corinthians 11:29

			Seven Signs That Show I Believe in Baptism into the Body

			a.	I have entered into the Body of Christ wholly.

			b.	My service to the Body of Christ is evident to all.

			c.	I go deeper into the Body of Christ.

			d.	Something dies in me for the Body of Christ to be in me.

			e.	The Body of Christ in me shows on the outside.

			f.	I am prepared to do something that is humbling to be baptized into the Body of Christ.

			g.	I do things that show that I trust the Body of Christ.

			Communion Prayer

			The Bread

			Father we come before Your throne

			We present this bread

			We say this is the body of Jesus Christ

			Thank you for what You’ve done for us

			Thank You for Your mighty power

			Let it be reversed, neutralized and sanctified by the blood

			The blood of Jesus. (Drink It)

			May the Lord keep you and bless you

			May you be protected because of the anointing!

			The Lord keep thee

			No bad news shall befall you!

			May the Lord make His face shine upon you and be gracious unto you.

			Yea, they despised the pleasant land, they believed not his word: But murmured in their tents, and hearkened not unto the voice of the Lord. 			Pray also against the presence of satan as a slow, low-grade continuous, accuser in your life.

			After these things did king Ahasuerus promote Haman the son of Hammedatha the Agagite, and advanced him, and set his seat above all the princes that were with him. 			Every mystery illness, every frightening and fearful thought, every depression and anxiety, every sleeplessness and every curse that has come to your house, is dispelled because of the blood of Jesus. (Drink It)

			The Lord shall guide you continually

			The Lord, be gracious unto you

			May He smile on you and give you peace

CHAPTER 20




Prayer for the Spirit of Truth & Prayer for Pleasant Lands




		 			And he said unto them, I beheld Satan as lightning fall from heaven.

			Luke 10:18

		 			Prayer for the Spirit of Truth

			Truth is very difficult to accept. Pray that truth will be part of you.

			Even the Spirit of truth; whom the world cannot receive, because it seeth him not, neither knoweth him: but ye know him; for he dwelleth with you, and shall be in you.

			John 14:17

			The Greatest Secret About Our Liberty

			And ye shall know the truth, and the truth shall make you free.

			John 8:32

			God is not a man, that he should lie; neither the son of man, that he should repent: hath he said, and shall he not do it? or hath he spoken, and shall he not make it good?

			Numbers 23:19

			If God is not a man that He should lie, then the main characteristic of men is to lie. Knowing the truth and telling yourself the truth will help you to advance tremendously. God sets us free by we telling ourselves the truth.

			So, what is the truth about you and I?

			Father, give me the Spirit of truth.

			Eighteen Truths About Yourself

			This know also, that in the last days perilous times shall come. For men shall be lovers of their own selves, covetous, boasters, proud, blasphemers, disobedient to parents, unthankful, unholy,

			2 Timothy 3:1-2

			1.	Pray about the truth of being a lover of yourself.

			2.	Pray about the truth of being covetous.

			3.	Pray about the truth of being a boaster.

			4.	Pray about the truth of being proud.

			5.	Pray about the truth of being a blasphemer.

			6.	Pray about the truth of being disobedient to your parents.

			7.	Pray about the truth of being unthankful.

			8.	Pray about the truth of being unholy.

			Thou lovest righteousness, and hatest wickedness: therefore, God, thy God, hath anointed thee with the oil of gladness above thy fellows.

			Psalm 45:7

			Without natural affection, trucebreakers, false accusers, incontinent, fierce, despisers of those that are good, Traitors, heady, highminded, lovers of pleasures more than lovers of God; Having a form of godliness, but denying the power thereof: from such turn away.

			2 Timothy 3:3-5

			9.	Pray about the truth of being without natural affection.

			10.	 Pray about the truth of being a truce breaker.

			11.	 Pray about the truth of being a false accuser.

			12.	 Pray about the truth of being incontinent, this means 	without self-control.

			13.	 Pray about the truth of being fierce (that is, using brutal 	violence).

			14.	 Pray about the truth of being despisers of those that are 	good.

			15.	 Pray about the truth of being traitors.

			16.	 Pray about the truth of being heady.

			17.	 Pray about the truth of being high-minded.

			18.	 Pray about the truth of being lovers of pleasure more 	than lovers of God.

			The Truth Must Be Told to Four People

			1.	You must tell the truth to God.

			2.	You must tell the truth to those you love.

			3.	You must tell the truth to those you want to have a good relationship with.

			4.	You must tell the truth to yourself.

			But speaking the truth in love, may grow up into him in all things, which is the head, even Christ:

			Ephesians 4:15

			Pray that you will tell these four people the truth all the time.

			When we start speaking the truth, we start to grow in every aspect of our lives. Truth takes our relationships up and lack of truth takes our relationships down.

			Father, take me up by the Spirit of truth.

			How Does the Truth Help Us?

			And they thirsted not when he led them through the deserts: he caused the waters to flow out of the rock for them: he clave the rock also, and the waters gushed out.

			Isaiah 48:21

			God’s power leads us through a desert.

			And the Word was made flesh, and dwelt among us, (and we beheld his glory, the glory as of the only begotten of the Father,) full of grace and truth...For the law was given by Moses, but grace and truth came by Jesus Christ.

			John 1:14, 17

			Once we accept truth, the grace of God will kick in to help us.

			And it came to pass, when the Lord would take up Elijah into heaven by a whirlwind, that Elijah went with Elisha from Gilgal. And Elijah said unto Elisha, Tarry here, I pray thee; for the Lord hath sent me to Beth–el. And Elisha said unto him, As the Lord liveth, and as thy soul liveth, I will not leave thee. So they went down to Beth–el.

CHAPTER 21




Prayer to Remove Any Idols and gods from Your Life & Prayer Against Curses




		 			Praise ye the Lord. O give thanks unto the Lord; for he is good: for his mercy endureth for ever.

			Blessed are they that keep judgment, and he that doeth righteousness at all times...

			Our fathers understood not thy wonders in Egypt; they remembered not the multitude of thy mercies; but provoked him at the sea, even at the Red sea.

			But lusted exceedingly in the wilderness, and tempted God in the desert...

		 			Who can utter the mighty acts of the Lord? who can shew forth all his praise?

			Those Who Forget

			People who have the least are normally ungrateful. Somebody once told me, “Orphans are usually ungrateful.” This is because people do not remember the great things other people have done for them.

			3. 	Pray for the Spirit of remembrance. The Holy Spirit is the Spirit of remembrance. He is the one who can help us to remember the great things that God, through people, has done for us.

			But the Comforter, which is the Holy Ghost, whom the Father will send in my name, he shall teach you all things, and bring all things to your remembrance, whatsoever I have said unto you.

			John 14:26

			Pray for the spirit of remembrance.

			Father, deliver me from forgetfulness.

			Prayer to Remove any Form of Idol from Your Life

			They made a calf in Horeb, and worshipped the molten image.

			Psalm 106:19

			Every type of idol in our lives must go away. This is a major provocation to God.

			And Moses returned unto the Lord, and said, Oh, this people have sinned a great sin, and have made them gods of gold.

			Exodus 32:31

			Many of us have made money into gods of gold.

			They provoked him to jealousy with strange gods, with abominations provoked they him to anger. They sacrificed unto devils, not to God; to gods whom they knew not, to new gods that came newly up, whom your fathers feared not.

			Deuteronomy 32:16-17

			Deuteronomy 32:16-17 shows us that gods are devils. The people incensed God greatly because they had actually chosen devils.

			And the king said unto her, Be not afraid: for what sawest thou? And the woman said unto Saul, I saw gods ascending out of the earth.

			1 Samuel 28:13

			Seeing “gods ascending out of the earth” means that these are living beings on the earth.

			And ye shall overthrow their altars, and break their pillars, and burn their groves with fire; and ye shall hew down the graven images of their gods, and destroy the names of them out of that place.

			Deuteronomy 12:3

			The images are the ‘images of their gods”.

			All these verses show that God does not like idols at all, because they are devils.

			1.	Pray that whatever connection there is between you and an idol is going away right now.

			This is the end of idols in our lives. PRAY!

			The List of gods to Pray Against

			a. 	The gods of gold

			They drank wine, and praised the gods of gold, and of silver, of brass, of iron, of wood, and of stone.

			Daniel 5:4

			b. 	The gods of silver

			They drank wine, and praised the gods of gold, and of silver, of brass, of iron, of wood, and of stone.

			Daniel 5:4

			They drank wine, and praised the gods of gold, and of silver, of brass, of iron, of wood, and of stone.

			Daniel 5:4

			They drank wine, and praised the gods of gold, and of silver, of brass, of iron, of wood, and of stone.

			Daniel 5:4

			They drank wine, and praised the gods of gold, and of silver, of brass, of iron, of wood, and of stone.

			Daniel 5:4

			Deuteronomy 4:28

			And the Lord shall scatter you among the nations, and ye shall be left few in number among the heathen, whither the Lord shall lead you. And there ye shall serve gods, the work of men’s hands, wood and stone, which neither see, nor hear, nor eat, nor smell.

			Deuteronomy 4:27-28

			And the Lord said unto Moses, See, I have made thee a god to Pharaoh: and Aaron thy brother shall be thy prophet.

			Exodus 7:1

			A man can become a god to you. God is against all other gods because they are devils.

			They sacrificed unto devils, not to God; to gods whom they knew not, to new gods that came newly up, whom your fathers feared not.

			Deuteronomy 32:17

			Which of us does not have money which can become a god? Which of us does not have a pastor who can become a god? Which of us does not have a man or a woman who can become a god?

			Rise to your feet and begin to pray to cleanse yourself of any god in your life. PRAY!

			Ask yourself, “What can I not give up?” That “thing” is your god. Pray and renounce it or renounce them.

			Pray and disconnect yourself. We shall not serve any of these gods!

			Characteristics of a god

			 				What makes you wake up early?

				What makes you travel?

				What makes you work hard?

				What makes you sacrifice?

				What makes you give?

				What makes you loyal?

				What makes you praise?



			And ye shall overthrow their altars, and break their pillars, and burn their groves with fire; and ye shall hew down the graven images of their gods, and destroy the names of them out of that place.

			Deuteronomy 12:3

			2.	Pray and remove any god from your hidden closet. If it is a name, change your name. If it is a necklace, throw it away.

			Every image, every artifact, pray and destroy it now!

			Idols bring curses

			And Laban went to shear his sheep: and Rachel had stolen the images that were her father’s.

			Genesis 31:19

			Now Rachel had taken the images, and put them in the camel’s furniture, and sat upon them. And Laban searched all the tent, but found them not.

			Genesis 31:34

			Rachel was sitting on gods. What secrets are you sitting on? Sometimes we don’t understand the mysteries behind certain things.

			Whatever represents a god that is bringing a curse, we end it today.

			The Curse for Sitting on a Stolen god

			And Rachel died, and was buried in the way to Ephrath, which is Bethlehem.

			Genesis 35:19

			The curse for sitting on a stolen god is death - be it ministerial death, career death, financial death, business death or marital death!

			Whatever secret there is in our lives that brings a curse and can lead to death, has ended now! This is the end of images! This marks the end of secrets! Pray!

			Prayer against Seven Curses

			1. 	Generational or family curses.

			 Pray and break every generational curse.

			Whatever family curse that has been passed down to us, we break out of it now!

			Thou shalt not bow down thyself to them, nor serve them: for I the Lord thy God am a jealous God, visiting the iniquity of the fathers upon the children unto the third and fourth generation of them that hate me;

			Exodus 20:5

			I break any negative pattern associated with my family!

			Pray and Confess Your Sins of Idol Worshiping

			If they shall confess their iniquity, and the iniquity of their fathers, with their trespass which they trespassed against me, and that also they have walked contrary unto me; Then will I remember my covenant with Jacob, and also my covenant with Isaac, and also my covenant with Abraham will I remember; and I will remember the land.

			Leviticus 26:40, 42

			Pray and confess your idols and your father’s idols and secrets.

			2. 	The curse in the city.

			Cursed shalt thou be in the city, and cursed shalt thou be in the field.

			And thy life shall hang in doubt before thee; and thou shalt fear day and night, and shalt have none assurance of thy life:

			Deuteronomy 28:16, 66

			This is why God calls us to places in the city and in the field. Joseph was in a pit but he became a prime minister in the city of Egypt.

			Don’t be fixated to any city! Declare freedom from chains and the curse in the city.

			3. 	The curse of the field.

			Cursed shalt thou be in the city, and cursed shalt thou be in the field.

			Deuteronomy 28:16

			A field is an undeveloped place.

			Pray that you will be blessed in the field. Pray that any curse in the field shall not affect you.

			4. 	The curse of thy basket and thy store.

			Cursed shall be thy basket and thy store.

			Deuteronomy 28:17

			Your basket is your bank account. A basket is something that you pick something from easily. A store is a place that you go to when necessary. It is a curse when you owe more than what is in the bank.

			Pray and break whatever makes our basket and store empty.

			5. 	The curse on the fruit of your body.

			Cursed shall be the fruit of thy body, and the fruit of thy land, the increase of thy kine, and the flocks of thy sheep.

			Deuteronomy 28:18

			Every curse following my children - I break it now in the name of Jesus.

			6. 	The curse of the increase of thy kine, flocks and thy sheep.

			Cursed shall be the fruit of thy body, and the fruit of thy land, the increase of thy kine, and the flocks of thy sheep.

			Deuteronomy 28:18

			The Seven Areas to Increase in Your Ministry

			Of the increase of his government and peace there shall be no end, upon the throne of David, and upon his kingdom, to order it, and to establish it with judgment and with justice from henceforth even forever. The zeal of the Lord of hosts will perform this.

			2.	There must be an increase in peace.

			3.	The must be an increase in order.

			4.	There must an increase in establishment.

			5.	There must be an increase in judgement (decision 		taking).

			7.	There must be an increase in zeal (energy to continue 		the ministry).

			We invoke divine protection for any advancing repercussion and harvest of evil we sowed in our lives, and things we hid under the chair

CHAPTER 22




Prayer about Your Prayer Life; Prayer against Snares and Traps; Prayer for Escapes; Prayer from Psalm 106 & Prayer of Dedication to Build God’s House




		 			Watch ye therefore, and pray always, that ye may be accounted worthy to escape all these things that shall come to pass, and to stand before the Son of man.

			Luke 21:36

		 			Prayer about Your Prayer Life

			 1. 	Pray about your prayer life so that things will not surprise you; things will not surprise you when you are watching and praying.

			Watch ye therefore, and pray always, that ye may be accounted worthy to escape all these things that shall come to pass, and to stand before the Son of man.

			Luke 21:36

			You shall be counted worthy to escape any surprise attack when you pray.

			Our way out of any surprise is through PRAYER; therefore, pray about your prayer life.

			Father, influence my prayer life by your Spirit. Help me to pray more so that I may be accounted worthy.

			2. Pray that from whatever represents a surprise, a snare or a trap, you shall be granted divine escape.

			Pray that you shall escape any form of surprise, trap or a snare.

			Prayer against Any Form of a Snare or a Trap

			1. 	Pray against a financial snare.

			Pray that you will not fall prey to any financial snare.

			And Pharaoh’s servants said unto him, How long shall this man be a snare unto us? let the men go, that they may serve the Lord their God: knowest thou not yet that Egypt is destroyed?

			Exodus 10:7

			2. 	Pray against any man who is a snare.

			And Pharaoh’s servants said unto him, How long shall this man be a snare unto us? let the men go, that they may serve the Lord their God: knowest thou not yet that Egypt is destroyed?

			Exodus 10:7

			3. 	Pray against any woman who is a snare.

			And I find more bitter than death the woman, whose heart is snares and nets, and her hands as bands: whoso pleaseth God shall escape from her; but the sinner shall be taken by her.

			Ecclesiastes 7:26

			This scripture shows that a woman can be a snare. When a woman catches you in a snare, it is more bitter like death. One of the rewards for serving God is to escape from such women - a woman whose heart is snares.

			4. 	Pray against a snare of a prophet.

			The days of visitation are come, the days of recompence are come; Israel shall know it: the prophet is a fool, the spiritual man is mad, for the multitude of thine iniquity, and the great hatred. The watchman of Ephraim was with my God: but the prophet is a snare of a fowler in all his ways, and hatred in the house of his God.

			Hosea 9:7-8

			A prophet can be a snare.

			Pray that you will not be misled into captivity by any prophet or pastor.

			Prayer for Escapes

			1. 	Pray to escape from national and international snares.

			Give a portion to seven, and also to eight; for thou knowest not what evil shall be upon the earth.

			Ecclesiastes 11:2

			For man also knoweth not his time: as the fishes that are taken in an evil net, and as the birds that are caught in the snare; so are the sons of men snared in an evil time, when it falleth suddenly upon them.

			Ecclesiastes 9:12

			Father, grant me divine escape from national and international snares.

			2. 	Pray against Any Plane Crash. Whatever airline you sit in will never crash. It will take off and land safely.

			Watch ye therefore, and pray always, that ye may be accounted worthy to escape all these things that shall come to pass, and to stand before the Son of man.

			Luke 21:36

			Every flight is declared a safe flight. I shall not sit in a plane where the pilot is drunk. I shall go and return safely in the name of Jesus.

			Whatever is on any flight that is evil, pray that it shall not affect you.

			Therefore, lift your hands and pray that you are counted worthy to escape all such snares.

			3.	Pray to escape from any bus. Whichever bus you sit on, you are declared safe.

			4.	Pray against marital snares.

			And Michal Saul’s daughter loved David: and they told Saul, and the thing pleased him. And Saul said, I will give him her, that she may be a snare to him, and that the hand of the Philistines may be against him. Wherefore Saul said to David, Thou shalt this day be my son in law in the one of the twain.

			1 Samuel 18:20-21

			Whatever marital snare you are about to enter or have already entered is being broken now. Whatever represents a snare in your marriage is being broken now.

			Prayer from Psalm 106

			1. 	Pray for your soul’s well-being. Our prosperity depends on the health of our souls.

			Pray that you will not have leanness of your soul as you prosper.

			And he gave them their request; but sent leanness into their soul.

			Psalm 106:15

			2.	Pray that you will not make any of the mistakes of Dathan and Abiram.
