			Ten Mistakes of Dathan and Abiram

			1. 	The mistake of rising up against Moses (your leader).

			And they rose up before Moses, with certain of the children of Israel, two hundred and fifty princes of the assembly, famous in the congregation, men of renown:

			Numbers 16:2

			2. 	The mistake of gathering and organizing against your leader.

			And they gathered themselves together against Moses and against Aaron, and said unto them, Ye take too much upon you, seeing all the congregation are holy, every one of them, and the Lord is among them: wherefore then lift ye up yourselves above the congregation of the Lord?

			Numbers 16:3

			3. 	The mistake of saying to the man of God, “You are too big.”

			And they gathered themselves together against Moses and against Aaron, and said unto them, Ye take too much upon you, seeing all the congregation are holy, every one of them, and the Lord is among them: wherefore then lift ye up yourselves above the congregation of the Lord?

			Numbers 16:3

			4.	 The mistake of not recognizing a called and chosen person.

			And he spake unto Korah and unto all his company, saying, Even to morrow the Lord will shew who are his, and who is holy; and will cause him to come near unto him: even him whom he hath chosen will he cause to come near unto him.

			Numbers 16:5

			5. 	The mistake of not valuing the honour of being brought near.

			Seemeth it but a small thing unto you, that the God of Israel hath separated you from the congregation of Israel, to bring you near to himself to do the service of the tabernacle of the Lord, and to stand before the congregation to minister unto them?

			Numbers 16:9

			6. 	The mistake of not coming when you are called.

			Moreover thou hast not brought us into a land that floweth with milk and honey, or given us inheritance of fields and vineyards: wilt thou put out the eyes of these men? we will not come up.

			Numbers 16:14

			7. 	The mistake of murmuring.

			For which cause both thou and all thy company are gathered together against the Lord: and what is Aaron, that ye murmur against him?

			Numbers 16:11

			8. 	The mistake of grievous accusation.

			Is it a small thing that thou hast brought us up out of a land that floweth with milk and honey, to kill us in the wilderness, except thou make thyself altogether a prince over us?

			Numbers 16:13

			9. 	The mistake of saying that the man of God is making himself a prince.

			Is it a small thing that thou hast brought us up out of a land that floweth with milk and honey, to kill us in the wilderness, except thou make thyself altogether a prince over us?

			Numbers 16:13

			10. 	The mistake of provoking God.

			But if the Lord make a new thing, and the earth open her mouth, and swallow them up, with all that appertain unto them, and they go down quick into the pit; then ye shall understand that these men have provoked the Lord.

			Numbers 16:30

			What happened to these people who provoked God? The earth swallowed them.

			But if the Lord make a new thing, and the earth open her mouth, and swallow them up, with all that appertain unto them, and they go down quick into the pit; then ye shall understand that these men have provoked the Lord.

			Numbers 16:30

			Whatever mistake of Dathan and Abiram will not happen to you! Pray that you will not make the mistake of Dathan and Abiram. PRAY!

			Prayer of Dedication to Build God’s House

			And Jacob vowed a vow, saying, If God will be with me, and will keep me in this way that I go, and will give me bread to eat, and raiment to put on, So that I come again to my father’s house in peace; then shall the Lord be my God: And this stone, which I have set for a pillar, shall be God’s house: and of all that thou shalt give me I will surely give the tenth unto thee.

			Genesis 28:20-22

			 How do you make a stone, God’s house? The only answer is that THE HOUSE MUST BE BUILT.

			2.	Ask God for the forgiveness of your sins.

			Mark 7:21-22

			4.	Murders

			But lusted exceedingly in the wilderness, and tempted God in the desert.

			And the spirit entered into me when he spake unto me, and set me upon my feet, that I heard him that spake unto me.

			1.	Pray that you will receive the anointing when you listen to messages.

			2.	Pray for the anointing to come upon you as you listen to messages.

			3.	Pray that you will receive the anointing as you hear the word.

			4.	Pray that you will receive the anointing when you see certain things God wants you to see, or go to certain places that God wants you go.

			And the hand of the Lord was there upon me; and he said unto me, Arise, go forth into the plain, and I will there talk with thee.

			Then I arose, and went forth into the plain: and, behold, the glory of the Lord stood there, as the glory which I saw by the river of Chebar: and I fell on my face.

CHAPTER 24




Prayer for the Spirit of Power & Prayer against Curses




		 			For God hath not given us the spirit of fear; but of power, and of love, and of a sound mind.

			2 Timothy 1:7

		 		 			Prayer for the Spirit of Power

			For God hath not given us the spirit of fear; but of power, and of love, and of a sound mind.

			2 Timothy 1:7

			1.	Spirit of power

			2.	Spirit of love

			3.	Spirit of a sound mind

			You can fight evil spirits with power.

			And when he had called unto him his twelve disciples, he gave them power against unclean spirits, to cast them out, and to heal all manner of sickness and all manner of disease.

			Matthew 10:1

			With the Spirit of power, you are overcoming evil spirits that influence you, harass you, and make you act in a certain way.

			1. 	Power not to run away.

			Power is needed to drink the cup that God has for us to drink. Every bad thing we do is from our flesh. The flesh is weak so it tends to move away from the heart’s true desire.

			And he cometh unto the disciples, and findeth them asleep, and saith unto Peter, What, could ye not watch with me one hour?

			Watch and pray, that ye enter not into temptation: the spirit indeed is willing, but the flesh is weak.

			He went away again the second time, and prayed, saying, O my Father, if this cup may not pass away from me, except I drink it, thy will be done.

			And he came and found them asleep again: for their eyes were heavy.

			Matthew 26:40-43

			Pray for the Spirit of power.

			Whatever Satan has planned for us to do with our flesh will not happen because of the Spirit of power. The Holy Spirit is a Spirit of power; therefore, let’s pray for power.

			Whatever you are afraid of, especially fear, let us rise up and bind it.

			Some of us are afraid to marry; some of us are afraid to respond to the call of God.

			Rise up and bind this spirit of fear. Whatever you couldn’t handle before, you can now handle.

			2. 	Power to experience provocation without response. This is power not to react to earthly stimulation.

			Prayer secures angelic participation. Anybody who prays has a lot of angels around them.

			What think ye? They answered and said, He is guilty of death.

			Matthew 26:66

			Jesus became calm in the Garden of Gethsemane. Our calmness after hearing very bad news, like a death, shows the power we have.

			Then did they spit in his face, and buffeted him; and others smote him with the palms of their hands,

			Matthew 26:67

			Your response to bad treatment towards you shows the power that is working on you. Jesus was calm when they slapped Him and spat on Him. What would be your reaction if somebody slapped you?

			Pray for power to behave differently and respond differently; power to respond supernaturally.

			The power of God is coming into your life as you pray, to respond supernaturally!

			How would you respond if you saw pornography by accident? Do you feel sorry for people and have compassion?

			Pray for the power that will make you compassionate.

			3. 	Power not to tell lies or power to speak the truth.

			Now Peter sat without in the palace: and a damsel came unto him, saying, Thou also wast with Jesus of Galilee.

			But he denied before them all, saying, I know not what thou sayest.

			And when he was gone out into the porch, another maid saw him, and said unto them that were there, This fellow was also with Jesus of Nazareth.

			And again he denied with an oath, I do not know the man.

			Matthew 26:69-72

			Peter lied by saying that he did not know Jesus; although he had just dinner with him.

			When you lie, you change in the sight of the person you lie to. You betray the trust that the person had for you. The Spirit of power enables you to tell the truth.

			4. 	Power to identify with the right thing. Power to identify with somebody you have to identify with.

			And after a while came unto him they that stood by, and said to Peter, Surely thou also art one of them; for thy speech bewrayeth thee. Then began he to curse and to swear, saying, I know not the man. And immediately the cock crew.

			Matthew 26:73-74

			When you lack the power to identify with the right thing, you belong to different departments or compartments. When you have compartments, you have a double standard, depending where you are and which people you are with.

			This is where one compartment of people will think of you in one way and another compartment will think about you in a completely different way, yet you relate to both.

			Jesus didn’t change His stance

			Who do you identify with in the ministry? Who is your man of God? What do you stand for?

			Pray for the power to identify with one compartment or department.

			5. 	Power not to answer back or to defend yourself.

			This is the power not to answer back or defend yourself when something bad is being done to you or said about you or when you are being accused.

			And when he was accused of the chief priests and elders, he answered nothing.

			Then said Pilate unto him, Hearest thou not how many things they witness against thee?

			And he answered him to never a word; insomuch that the governor marvelled greatly.

			Matthew 27:12-14

			The governor was shocked when Jesus did not say anything because in the natural, human beings have an answer for everything.

			Pray for the Spirit of power not to answer back when you are being accused.

			6. 	Power to give.

			No man taketh it from me, but I lay it down of myself. I have power to lay it down, and I have power to take it again. This commandment have I received of my Father.

			John 10:18

			The power to prosper is the power to give. The power to give is the spirit of prosperity.

			i.	Pray for the power to give your life

			ii.	Pray for the power to build a church

			ii.	Pray for the power to give a car

			7. 	Power to wait for God. It takes power to leave things to God.

			The governor answered and said unto them, Whether of the twain will ye that I release unto you? They said, Barabbas.

			Pilate saith unto them, What shall I do then with Jesus which is called Christ? They all say unto him, Let him be crucified.

			And the governor said, Why, what evil hath he done? But they cried out the more, saying, Let him be crucified.

			When Pilate saw that he could prevail nothing, but that rather a tumult was made, he took water, and washed his hands before the multitude, saying, I am innocent of the blood of this just person: see ye to it.

			Then answered all the people, and said, His blood be on us, and on our children.

			Matthew 27:21-25

			And surely your blood of your lives will I require; at the hand of every beast will I require it, and at the hand of man; at the hand of every man’s brother will I require the life of man. Whoso sheddeth man’s blood, by man shall his blood be shed: for in the image of God made he man.

			Genesis 9:5-6

			There is a curse for shedding blood. So when the people said “let his blood upon us”, they were confessing a curse upon themselves.

			Dearly beloved, avenge not yourselves, but rather give place unto wrath: for it is written, Vengeance is mine; I will repay, saith the Lord.

			Romans 12:19

			Leave vengeance to God.

			Pray for the power to wait. Some of us need God to avenge us.

			8. 	Power to finish. Weakness is the opposite of power.

			Therefore, as we pray, the weakness is being destroyed.

			When Jesus therefore, had received the vinegar, he said, It is finished: and he bowed his head, and gave up the ghost.

			John 19:30

			As for the ministry, it is in our hearts to serve as lay ministers but we may not have the power to become missionaries.

			i.	Pray to finish your apostolic ministry; power to say one day, “It is finished.”

			ii.	Pray to finish your prophetic ministry; power to fulfill your prophetic ministry.

				Pray!

			iii.	Pray to finish your teaching and prophetic ministry like Moses, Elijah.

			iv.	Pray to finish your apostolic and prophetic ministry.

			This is where you are an apostle and a prophet at the same time.

			v.	Pray to finish your prophetic and evangelistic ministry.

			Pray for the combination of these two ministries in your life.

			Provide the Spirit of power to finish, Lord.

			vi.	Pray to finish your prophetic and pastoral ministry, like Kenneth Hagin. He was a prophet and a pastor.

			With these two combinations, you will know things about your sheep.

			A pastor is very important and we must feel sorry for anyone who does not have a pastor. Jesus felt sorry for the people he saw in Matthew chapter 9 because they were like people without a pastor.

			But when he saw the multitudes, he was moved with compassion on them, because they fainted, and were scattered abroad, as sheep having no shepherd.

			 				Matthew 9:36vii. Pray to finish your prophetic and helps ministry, an example of this ministry is the little girl who helped Naaman.

Mary Magdalene, a helps minister, is the one who told Peter that she had seen Jesus (John 20:1-18).

viii. Pray to finish your prophetic and giving ministry.

ix. 	Pray to finish your prophetic and governmental ministry.

This involves sharing things, setting things in order and arranging things in order.

Prayer against Curses that are Poured Upon Us

And I set my face unto the Lord God, to seek by prayer and supplications, with fasting, and sackcloth, and ashes:

Daniel 9:3

1. 	Prayer, supplication and sackcloth are the things that eradicate curses.

We have sinned, and have committed iniquity, and have done wickedly, and have rebelled, even by departing from thy precepts and from thy judgments:

Daniel 9:5

Neither have we hearkened unto thy servants the prophets, which spake in thy name to our kings, our princes, and our fathers, and to all the people of the land.

O Lord, righteousness belongeth unto thee, but unto us confusion of faces, as at this day; to the men of Judah, and to the inhabitants of Jerusalem, and unto all Israel, that are near, and that are far off, through all the countries whither thou hast driven them, because of their trespass that they have trespassed against thee.

Daniel 9:6-7

As it is written in the law of Moses, all this evil is come upon us: yet made we not our prayer before the Lord our God, that we might turn from our iniquities, and understand thy truth.

Daniel 9:13

2. 	Prayer will affect the curse. If you want the curse to be removed, then prayer is one of the keys.

Pray and confess your sins, iniquities and the sins of your fathers and mothers.

Father, be merciful unto us.

3. 	God visits the iniquities of the fathers to the third and fourth generations; but prayer eradicates and neutralizes curses.

Seventy weeks are determined upon thy people and upon thy holy city, to finish the transgression, and to make an end of sins, and to make reconciliation for iniquity, and to bring in everlasting righteousness, and to seal up the vision and prophecy, and to anoint the most Holy.

Daniel 9:24

i. 	Pray to bring an end to sins

ii. Pray to finish transgressions

iii. Pray for an end to iniquities - iniquities means something twisted

Father, bring an end of sins and finish transgressions.

Yea, all Israel have transgressed thy law, even by departing, that they might not obey thy voice; therefore, the curse is poured upon us, and the oath that is written in the law of Moses the servant of God, because we have sinned against him.

Daniel 9:11

iv. Pray for your life not to hang in the balance, without assurances. Pray against high levels of uncertainty. This brings depression.

And thy life shall hang in doubt before thee; and thou shalt fear day and night, and shalt have none assurance of thy life:

Deuteronomy 28:66

v. 	Pray against whatever makes your life uncertain and ask for forgiveness.

Whatever makes your life hang in doubt, whatever represents Deuteronomy 28:66, ask for forgiveness for it; even if it is coming from your ancestors. Whatever has happened in the past even to the fourth generation, ask God to forgive you.

Prayer against Something that You Cannot Escape From

Cursed shalt thou be when thou comest in, and cursed shalt thou be when thou goest out.

Deuteronomy 28:19

1. 	Pray against something that you cannot escape from no matter where you go.

You enter your first marriage, and then you come out; you enter your second marriage, and you experience the same problems. Therefore, pray!

As if a man did flee from a lion, and a bear met him; or went into the house, and leaned his hand on the wall, and a serpent bit him.

Amos 5:19

Everywhere I go, “it” comes there; “it” unsettles me. I plead the blood of Jesus to wash deeper. I apply the blood of Jesus to escape and finally destroy it. I am escaping from what I could not escape before.

I declare an end to every negativity that associates itself with me.

I reject it now in the name of Jesus!

Prayer against the Curse of No Vision and No Prosperity

1. 	Pray against aimlessness.

And thou shalt grope at noonday, as the blind gropeth in darkness, and thou shalt not prosper in thy ways: and thou shalt be only oppressed and spoiled evermore, and no man shall save thee.

Deuteronomy 28:29

2. 	Pray against no vision, no light, no zeal for life and lack of prosperity.

Whatever it is that takes away prosperity is being taken away now in the name of Jesus!

Father save us from the lack of prosperity!

Prayer against the Curse of Marital Disasters, Marital Contradictions and Marital Opposites

Thou shalt betroth a wife, and another man shall lie with her: thou shalt build an house, and thou shalt not dwell therein: thou shalt plant a vineyard, and shalt not gather the grapes thereof.

Deuteronomy 28:30

1. 	You come with love and the person gives you accusations - the opposite and a contradiction. You enter into marriage with a clean mind and a clean heart, but the marriage comes with a lot of problems that do not end.

Father, I pray for the forgiveness of iniquities and transgressions. Please let the curse end, Lord.

The marital contradictions, the marital opposites, Lord; please be merciful concerning any marital disaster following my life. Please be merciful.

Prayer against the Curse of the Lack of Good Harvest or Failed Harvest

1.	Pray against carrying much seeds, but not reaping much 	and not reaping a good harvest.

Thou shalt carry much seed out into the field, and shalt gather but little in; for the locust shall consume it.

Deuteronomy 28:38

The Curse

i.	Sowing much seeds in the church, sowing good seeds into people’s lives and get very little as result of your goodness.

ii. 	Sowing much as a wife and getting nothing back from your husband.

iii. Sowing many seeds as a husband and getting nothing back from your wife.

iv. Working hard and having nothing to show.

Prayer against the Curse of Captivity of Children

Thou shalt beget sons and daughters, but thou shalt not enjoy them; for they shall go into captivity.

Deuteronomy 28:41

You shall have sons and daughters but they will not be yours, for they will go into captivity.

Deuteronomy 28:41 NASB

The Curse

i. 	Children go into the captivity of alcoholism, fornication, bad friends and drugs. It can also be that through divorce, children end up being given to somebody else.

ii. 	Pray because children may belong to alcohol, sexual perversions, drugs, or they may belong to some strange boy or girl.

Pray against these captivities.

I pray for mercies Lord. I am asking for forgiveness. Please forgive our iniquities.

These curses are outside my walls. They are outside my dwelling place.

Prayer against the Curse of Sickness that Cannot Be Healed

The Lord will smite thee with the botch of Egypt, and with the emerods, and with the scab, and with the itch, whereof thou canst not be healed. The Lord shall smite thee in the knees, and in the legs, with a sore botch that cannot be healed, from the sole of thy foot unto the top of thy head. Moreover he will bring upon thee all the diseases of Egypt, which thou wast afraid of; and they shall cleave unto thee. Also every sickness, and every plague, which is not written in the book of this law, them will the Lord bring upon thee, until thou be destroyed.

Deuteronomy 28:27, 35, 60-61

The Curse

i. 	The main characteristic of this is something that cannot go. This is for all chronic diseases and whatever represents pain: back pain, knee pain and joint pain.

ii. 	Two main characteristics of the curse are found in Deuteronomy 28:60. One is where you are afraid of a disease and the other is disease shall also destroy you.

Father, be merciful on us. We are asking for healing and forgiveness of our sins, transgressions of our fathers even to the fourth generation.

Every fearful disease, every incurable disease which I am afraid of, I intercede Lord.

Every cancer and any form of cancer, I intercede, Lord.

Prayer against the Curse of Mental and Emotional Struggles

So that thou shalt be mad for the sight of thine eyes which thou shalt see.

Deuteronomy 28:34

1. 	Pray against mental and emotional instabilities in your life. Marital disasters can cause this.

Every mental and emotional distress in my life, I intercede now!

Anything that makes you cry is ending now. We overturn it and we overthrow it. We end it now in the name of Jesus.

Prayer against the Curse of Something being Stolen from You

1. 	Whatever the enemy wants to steal from you, pray against it.

2. 	Whatever is yours and has been stolen from you is ending today.

Therefore, we pray, Father let there be a total recovery and restoration. I break the curse Lord, I overcome it!

Father, give whoever is holding onto whatever is mine no rest, in the same way King Ahasuerus could not sleep until whatever reward was Mordecai’s, was given to him (Esther 6:1-3).

Pray against the Curse of Not being Promoted or the Curse of being Demoted

The stranger that is within thee shall get up above thee very high; and thou shalt come down very low.

Deuteronomy 28:43

The alien who is among you shall rise above you higher and higher, but you will go down lower and lower.

Deuteronomy 28:43 NASB

The foreigners living among you will become stronger and stronger, while you become weaker and weaker.

Deuteronomy 28:43 NLT

The Curse

i. 	Missing your promotion whilst others are being promoted. It is a curse when you are brought down very low.

This is the end of not being promoted. This is the end of your halt, this is the end of you going down and demoted.

Every cause of demotion, I reject it now. Whatever causes my demotion. Whatever causes me to be at one place all the time, I reject it in the name of Jesus!

Prayer against the Curse of the Anointing that Does Not Work for You

Thou shalt have olive trees throughout all thy coasts, but thou shalt not anoint thyself with the oil; for thine olive shall cast his fruit.

Deuteronomy 28:40

The Curse

i. 	The source of the anointing is there, but it does not change 	anything in your life. You are always around and have heard all messages, but there is no change in your life. You become like a salted ground, nothing grows on you. What is that grace that is in your ministry that is not affecting you?

ii. You are associated with a prosperous person, but you are poor. You are around the anointing, but nothing works in your life.

The anointing on your Prophet’s life is affecting you now! The grace that is upon him is coming upon you now.

Prayer against the Curse of Unanswered Prayer

And thy heaven that is over thy head shall be brass, and the earth that is under thee shall be iron.

Deuteronomy 28: 23

The Curse

1.	This is such that life on earth is not working and the heavens is also against you. The windows of heaven are closed upon you.

Whatever represents unanswered prayers, we cancel it, and Lord, we say be merciful onto us.

Father thank You for being merciful. Thank You for forgiving my transgressions and iniquities. Thank You Lord.

Communion Prayer

For you first, God raised up His Servant and sent Him to bless you by turning every one of you from your wicked ways.

Acts 3:26 NASB

Whatever I have in my cup is turning into the blood of Jesus right now

Indeed it is a cup of blessing

The cup of blessing which we bless, is it not the communion of the blood of Christ?

The bread which we break, is it not the communion of the body of Christ? (1 Corinthians 10:16)

Bind demons now, bind scorpions, and bind the power of snakes in your life

Block every retaliation of the enemy against your life

Cut off the head of the snake - we cut off the head of the snake!

Father, we thank You!

Amen.





CHAPTER 25




Prayer from Psalm 17; Prayer to Secure Angelic Intervention; Prayer Concerning Your Ministry & Prayer from Isaiah 40




		 			Hear the right, O Lord, attend unto my cry, give ear unto my prayer, that goeth not out of feigned lips.

			Psalm 17:1

			Speak ye comfortably to Jerusalem, and cry unto her, that her warfare is accomplished...

			Isaiah 40:2



		 			Prayer from Psalm 17

			Hear the right, O Lord, attend unto my cry, give ear unto my prayer, that goeth not out of feigned lips.

			Psalm 17:1

			Our prayers are cries to the Lord and God is hearing our cries.

			And it came to pass in process of time, that the king of Egypt died: and the children of Israel sighed by reason of the bondage, and they cried, and their cry came up unto God by reason of the bondage.

			Exodus 2:23

			They cried “by reason of the bondage”; our cry is our prayer. We are crying today and we are saying, “Lord attend unto our cry.”

			Let’s come to the Lord as we are. Through the blood of Jesus, we can come before the Lord. Let us pray from our hearts and let His judgment or decisions come from Him.

			Shew thy marvellous lovingkindness, O thou that savest by thy right hand them which put their trust in thee from those that rise up against them.

			Psalm 17:7

			We want God to show His loving kindness.

			Lift your hands and pray.

			Whatever is rising up against you, at work, home and in your finances, lift up your hands and declare judgment.

			Hear, O Lord, when I cry with my voice: have mercy also upon me, and answer me.

			Psalm 27:7

			We are crying unto the Lord for His mercies.

			Cry for mercy, cry for your sins and mistakes.

			We cry for mercy, Lord.

			Keep me as the apple of the eye, hide me under the shadow of thy wings,

			Psalm 17:8

			God can keep you as the apple of His eye. That means you are very special to God. It’s like somebody delicately holding an egg in his hands and moving around with it.

			Pray for the Lord to make you special. Pray for God to keep you as the apple of His eye and that He would hide you under the shadow of His wings.

			From the wicked that oppress me, from my deadly enemies, who compass me about. They are inclosed in their own fat: with their mouth they speak proudly.

			Psalm 17:9-10

			These “deadly enemies” are evil spirits and they use people to speak proudly.

			They have now compassed us in our steps: they have set their eyes bowing down to the earth; Like as a lion that is greedy of his prey, and as it were a young lion lurking in secret places.

			Psalm 17:11-12

			There are two types of Christians: Christians who are conscious of enemies and Christians who are NOT conscious of enemies. If you do not bind demons, it means you are not conscious of the enemy and the enemy will like to keep you in this state (in a state of ignorance).

			With the consciousness of demonic surprises and every young lion, and things moving in secret places ready to pounce on me, I curse the young lion that is lurking in secret places!

			Arise, O Lord, disappoint him, cast him down: deliver my soul from the wicked, which is thy sword:

			Psalm 17:13

			Arise O Lord and disappoint them, disappoint them Lord. Whatever seems to be a surprise against me, disappoint them Lord. Disappoint the young lion that is lurking in secret places in the name of Jesus!

			Rise up, Lord, confront them, bring them down; with your sword rescue me from the wicked. By your hand save me from such people, Lord, from those of this world whose reward is in this life.

			May what you have stored up for the wicked fill their bellies; may their children gorge themselves on it, and may there be leftovers for their little ones.

			Psalm 17:13-14 (NIV)

			Lord, fill the stomach of the wicked with what they have planned for me. May their children gorge themselves on it and may there be surplus left over for their little ones. Save me Lord from their hands.

			As for me, I will behold thy face in righteousness: I shall be satisfied, when I awake, with thy likeness.

			Psalm 17:15

			Pray for the blood of Jesus to cleanse you. Kneel down and ask God to wash you with the blood of Jesus.

			 Wash me Lord, with the blood of Jesus, please take away my wickedness. Take away my mistakes and cleanse me Lord with the blood of Jesus. Vindicate me please.

			Three Realms of Prayer or Three Approaches to God

			1.	Approach God as though you are talking to A FRIEND. Pray about little things.

			When relating with a friend you don’t need to expressly ask for what you need. You just gp ahead and take what you need.

			What do you tell your friend? You tell your friend everything you need and what is worrying you. You tell your friend little things that you will not tell a father or judge. You speak to your friend about what is financially or socially embarrassing - “I need just three loaves because I am embarrassed.”

			2.	Pray to God like you are talking to A FATHER. Fathers are softer than judges.

			After this manner therefore, pray ye: Our Father which art in heaven, Hallowed be thy name.

			Matthew 6:9

			Fathers do not give things on a legal basis. A father is more mystical. He takes decisions that you don’t understand. A father provides everything that you need which a judge will not do.

			Ask whatever you need and God our Father will do it for you.

			Fathers do not deal with legalities. They just give - 5 talents, 2 talents and 1 talent - it is not based on any legal basis.

			3.	Pray to God like you are talking to A JUDGE.

			And he spake a parable unto them to this end, that men ought always to pray, and not to faint; Saying, There was in a city a judge, which feared not God, neither regarded man:

			And there was a widow in that city; and she came unto him, saying, Avenge me of mine adversary. And he would not for a while: but afterward he said within himself, Though I fear not God, nor regard man;

			Yet because this widow troubleth me, I will avenge her, lest by her continual coming she weary me. And the Lord said, Hear what the unjust judge saith.

			And shall not God avenge his own elect, which cry day and night unto him, though he bear long with them?

			I tell you that he will avenge them speedily. Nevertheless when the Son of man cometh, shall he find faith on the earth?

			Luke 18:1-8

			The Laws of Prayer

			(This is about Praying to God Using Laws)

			a)	 Agree with someone now and pray: “Again I say unto you, That if two of you shall agree on earth as touching any thing that they shall ask, it shall be done for them of my Father which is in heaven”(Matthew 18:19).

			Thus saith the Lord against all mine evil neighbours, that touch the inheritance which I have caused my people Israel to inherit; Behold, I will pluck them out of their land, and pluck out the house of Judah from among them.

			Jeremiah 12:14

			One of the “legal prayers” we should pray is that the Lord should pluck out our evil neighbours. Whatever represents evil neighbours, may the Lord pluck them out.

			Pluck them Lord, pluck out evil neighbours out of my life.

			Do you know that what your neighbours do can affect you? Your neighbour is somebody who does something which can affect you.

			Father, any negative thing from my neighbours at work and home please pluck them out in the name of Jesus.

			b) 	Abide in Christ - this is also a rule or a law: If ye abide in me, and my words abide in you, ye shall ask what ye will, and it shall be done unto you. (John 15:7)

			If you have been a Christian for 3 years and you know 30 scriptures, then Christ abides in you. Therefore, claim His law and use it to pray for 1 Peter 5:10.

			But the God of all grace, who hath called us unto his eternal glory by Christ Jesus, after that ye have suffered a while, make you perfect, establish, strengthen, settle you.

			1 Peter 5:10

			Pray for God to settle you, to establish you and to strengthen you. Pray to God about your finances, your marriage, your job and your relationships.

			Father we come to You according to Your Word in 1 Peter 5:10. Settle us, establish us and strengthen us.

			Every lifting of hands! Settlement is coming! Establishment is coming! Strengthening is coming!

			c) 	Ask in His name: And in that day ye shall ask me nothing. Verily, verily, I say unto you, Whatsoever ye shall ask the Father in my name, he will give it you. Hitherto have ye asked nothing in my name: ask, and ye shall receive, that your joy may be full (John 16:23-24).

			This is about our desires and God is interested in our desires.

			Whatever your desire is, ask Him now.

			Therefore, I say unto you, What things soever ye desire, when ye pray, believe that ye receive them, and ye shall have them.

			Mark 11:24

			Prayer Secures Angelic Intervention

			And when he had opened the seventh seal, there was silence in heaven about the space of half an hour. And I saw the seven angels which stood before God; and to them were given seven trumpets.

			And another angel came and stood at the altar, having a golden censer; and there was given unto him much incense, that he should offer it with the prayers of all saints upon the golden altar which was before the throne.

			And the smoke of the incense, which came with the prayers of the saints, ascended up before God out of the angel’s hand.

			And the angel took the censer, and filled it with fire of the altar, and cast it into the earth: and there were voices, and thunderings, and lightnings, and an earthquake.

			Revelation 8:1-5

			1.	Pray for angelic participation and involvement.

			And the whole multitude of the people were praying without at the time of incense. And there appeared unto him an angel of the Lord standing on the right side of the altar of incense. And when Zacharias saw him, he was troubled, and fear fell upon him.

			But the angel said unto him, Fear not, Zacharias: for thy prayer is heard; and thy wife Elisabeth shall bear thee a son, and thou shalt call his name John. And thou shalt have joy and gladness; and many shall rejoice at his birth.

			For he shall be great in the sight of the Lord, and shall drink neither wine nor strong drink; and he shall be filled with the Holy Ghost, even from his mother’s womb. And many of the children of Israel shall he turn to the Lord their God.

			And he shall go before him in the spirit and power of Elias, to turn the hearts of the fathers to the children, and the disobedient to the wisdom of the just; to make ready a people prepared for the Lord. And Zacharias said unto the angel, Whereby shall I know this? for I am an old man, and my wife well stricken in years.

			And the angel answering said unto him, I am Gabriel, that stand in the presence of God; and am sent to speak unto thee, and to shew thee these glad tidings. And, behold, thou shalt be dumb, and not able to speak, until the day that these things shall be performed, because thou believest not my words, which shall be fulfilled in their season.

			Luke 1:10-20

			1.	Angels can do a lot of things. Whatever has stopped working, angels can cause to start again.

			2.	The presence of angels can bring joy and gladness; angels can cause a womb that is decayed to become revived again.

			Happiness is coming into your life and life is coming into your ministry.

			Prayer Concerning Your Ministry

			Speak ye comfortably to Jerusalem, and cry unto her, that her warfare is accomplished, that her iniquity is pardoned: for she hath received of the Lord’s hand double for all her sins.

			Isaiah 40:2

			1. 	Pray about whatever warfare or battle that must be won to release you into the ministry.

			Four Areas of Warfare

			1.	War to ENTER the ministry

			2.	War to STAY in the ministry

			3.	War to ADVANCE in ministry

			4.	War to FINISH your ministry

			Break out of any entanglement to ENTER the ministry, to STAY in the ministry, to ADVANCE in ministry and to FINISH your ministry.

			Any of us can be found in any of these four areas. Therefore, pray for the area that you find yourself in.

			Father, give me the grace to advance and finish my ministry.

			2. 	Pray against anything that will prevent you from entering the ministry.

			Begin to curse opposition, accusation and limitation that is blocking your door of entrance to another realm of ministry.

			Bind any form of fear that is preventing you from entering into another realm of ministry.

			Bind any opposition against you.

			3.	Pray against anything that will cause you not to stay in the ministry.

			Bind anything that does not want you to stay; accomplish the warfare.

			And I give unto them eternal life; and they shall never perish, neither shall any man pluck them out of my hand.

			John 10:28

			Satan wants to pluck you out of the ministry.

			Declare war in the spirit against any plucking force that wants to extract you from the ministry and any force that wants to stop you from advancing in ministry.

			And the king Ahasuerus laid a tribute upon the land, and upon the isles of the sea.

			And all the acts of his power and of his might, and the declaration of the greatness of Mordecai, whereunto the king advanced him, are they not written in the book of the chronicles of the kings of Media and Persia?

			Esther 10:1-2

			And Samuel said unto the people, It is the Lord that advanced Moses and Aaron, and that brought your fathers up out of the land of Egypt.

			1 Samuel 12:6

			Whatever has decided to kill you, there will be angelic intervention and you will be advanced in the ministry. Make war against anything that resists your advancement and progress in ministry.

			Bind anything in your life that is causing limitations. Remove the boundaries!

			4.	Pray for advancement in ministry. Study the Boaz anointing. This is the anointing to finish what you have started.

			Then said she, Sit still, my daughter, until thou know how the matter will fall: FOR THE MAN WILL NOT BE IN REST, UNTIL HE HAVE FINISHED THE THING THIS DAY.

			Ruth 3:18

			The man (Boaz) did not rest until he finished what he started. Likewise, there should be no rest for us until we finish the ministry.

			5. 	Pray for the Boaz anointing to finish your ministry.

			Father, give me the grace of Boaz to finish the ministry. Give me the anointing of Boaz to finish what I have started.

			[Prepare and dress - you have to know the right timing if you want to marry. Ruth depended on the wisdom of an elderly person, her mother-in-law. (Ruth 3:1-6).]

			Prayers from Isaiah

			Speak ye comfortably to Jerusalem, and cry unto her, that her warfare is accomplished, that her iniquity is pardoned: for she hath received of the Lord’s hand double for all her sins.

			Isaiah 40:2

			Three Types of Sins

			i.	Sins

			ii.	Iniquities

			iii.	Transgressions

			1. 	Pray Against any Iniquity in Your Life.

			Iniquity means a twistedness, perversion and orientation towards a certain direction.

			For example, a young child who says, “I want to kill myself” or a young child who is into pornography, means the child is seriously oriented in a certain direction.

			 Pray against any orientation towards treachery, disloyalty, unfaithfulness and accusations.

			Lord forgive me for whatever twistedness there is in my life.

			2. 	Pray to Prepare the Way for The Lord.

			The voice of him that crieth in the wilderness, Prepare ye the way of the Lord, make straight in the desert a highway for our God.

			Isaiah 40:3

			Pray to prepare the way for the Lord through preaching and through teaching from the books.

			Pray to prepare a highway for the Lord.

			Pray to make straight, a way in the desert for God.

			The voice of him that crieth in the wilderness, Prepare ye the way of the Lord, make straight in the desert a highway for our God.

			Isaiah 40:3

			Pray God will use you to make a straight path in the desert, a highway for our God. Every desert will have a highway and an easy way for God. Therefore, pray that 190 nations will be captured for the Lord.

			3. 	Pray Concerning Every Valley in Your Life.

			Every valley shall be exalted, and every mountain and hill shall be made low: and the crooked shall be made straight, and the rough places plain.

			Isaiah 40:4

			A valley is a low place in life. The low aspect of your life that makes you think that you cannot be in ministry shall be exalted.

			Every valley shall be filled!

			Every mountain and hill (big problems and small problems) shall be made low!

			Every crooked thing shall be made straight and every rough place shall be made plain!

			Every valley in my life is being filled. I command every mountain and every hill to be lowered. I speak to the crooked places in my life, it is being made straight, and every rough place in my life is being made plain.

			But thou, O Lord, art a God full of compassion, and gracious, longsuffering, and plenteous in mercy and truth.

			O turn unto me, and have mercy upon me; give thy strength unto thy servant, and save the son of thine handmaid.

			Shew me a token for good; that they which hate me may see it, and be ashamed: because thou, Lord, hast holpen me, and comforted me.

			Psalm 86:15-17

			Lord show me a token for good so that those who hate me may see it and be ashamed.

			The valleys are being filled. The mountains shall be brought low, crooked places shall be made straight and the rough places shall be made plain. Lord, strengthen Your anointed, help me and comfort me Lord according to Your word. Show me a token for good, shame them Lord.

			Thank God for the valleys you see today. You shall not see them again!

			The mountains and the hills you see today, you shall not see them again!

			 The crooked places you see today, you shall not see again!

			 The rough places you see today, you shall not see them again!

			4. 	Pray To Give Yourself to the Ministry of the Word.

			Hear the right, O Lord, attend unto my cry, give ear unto my prayer, that goeth not out of feigned lips.

			Psalm 17:1

			The prophetic word is cry.

			The voice said, Cry. And he said, What shall I cry? All flesh is grass, and all the goodliness thereof is as the flower of the field: The grass withereth, the flower fadeth: because the spirit of the Lord bloweth upon it: surely the people is grass.

			Isaiah 40:6-7

			The flower is the goodliness of man. God is showing us that the goodliness of our lives is very short. Therefore, we must give ourselves to the ministry of the Word.

			But we will give ourselves continually to prayer, and to the ministry of the word.

			Acts 6:4

			Father, show me how I can give myself to the ministry of the Word. Everything else is grass and flowers, they will just fade away soon. Give me the grace for the ministry of the Word.

			 The people are like grass and as the flower that fades away. Everything is useless.

			5. 	Pray to Lift your Voice with Strength to Bring Good Tidings.

			O Zion, that bringest good tidings, get thee up into the high mountain; O Jerusalem, that bringest good tidings, lift up thy voice with strength; lift it up, be not afraid; say unto the cities of Judah, Behold your God!

			Isaiah 40:9

			We must go all-out for the ministry of the Word!

			Lift up your voice with strength for the ministry of the Word.

			Pray that you will be part of those who bring good tidings, bringing the good tidings with strength, with financial strength, with television and media strength and with airwaves strength.

			Grant us this grace for Your church, Lord!

			6. 	Pray to Give yourself unreservedly to the Great Commission. Let’s give ourselves unreservedly to the Great Commission.

			Pray committing your pastoral ministry to God.

			Behold, the Lord God will come with strong hand, and his arm shall rule for him: behold, his reward is with him, and his work before him.

			Isaiah 40:10

			His work and His reward come together - in His right hand is work and in His left hand is the reward.

			He shall feed his flock like a shepherd: he shall gather the Lambs with his arm, and carry them in his bosom, and shall gently lead those that are with young.

			Isaiah 40:11

			Four Parts of Your Pastoral Ministry

			1.	FEEDING ministry of feeding the sheep with the Word.

			2.	GATHERING ministry of gathering the Lambs and the feeble ones in your arms.

			3.	CARRYING ministry of carrying the Lambs because they are fragile.

			4.	LEADING ministry of leading those who are young.

			Father, help me to fulfill these four parts of my pastoral ministry. Help me to feed your flock, grant me the grace to gather the Lambs, help me to carry the Lambs because they are fragile and give me the grace to lead the young ones, Lord.

			7. 	Pray and Break any Covenants that You Have Ever Made, Knowingly and Unknowingly.

			Yea, they shall not be planted; yea, they shall not be sown: yea, their stock shall not take root in the earth: and he shall also blow upon them, and they shall wither, and the whirlwind shall take them away as stubble.

			Isaiah 40:24

			Pray to abolish any covenant you have made knowingly and unknowingly.

			Break any covenant you have made knowingly and unknowingly, any covenant that is affecting your spiritual condition and spiritual state.

			Terminate it now, come out of it now, break it now in the name of Jesus! Any money that you have paid in an occultic place, cancel it now!

			Any satanic or demonic covenant that is affecting your resourcefulness and your response to the ministry, break it now. It shall not take root, any comment that has been spoken over you, it shall not take root and it shall not be established.

			8.	Pray against Anything that Has Affected the Hedge Around Us.

			Hast not thou made an hedge about him, and about his house, and about all that he hath on every side? thou hast blessed the work of his hands, and his substance is increased in the land.

			Job 1:10

			Pray against every break or perforation in the hedge that is not making us resourceful and response to the ministry.

			9. 	Pray against Negative Predictions over your life.

			Yea, they shall not be planted; yea, they shall not be sown: yea, their stock shall not take root in the earth: and he shall also blow upon them, and they shall wither, and the whirlwind shall take them away as stubble.

			Isaiah 40:24

			Any lie that has been spoken against us and is working against us, we intercept it now and curse it.

			I curse any negative prediction over my life. I cancel any negative prediction against my life, my family and my children, in the name of Jesus. The predictions shall not take root in my life. We intercept and destroy them.

			10.	Pray against Genetic Disorders or Generational Patterns.

			Any genetic disorder or generational influence or generational pattern, I cancel it now by the blood of the Lamb. Every repeated pattern, I disconnect from it. Psychological disorders, mental disorders, and financial disorders - I reject you from my life, my children’s life, my husband’s life / my wife’s life.

			11.	Pray against Sexual Defilement.

			Cancel any form of sexual defilement or sexual contamination. Pray against any form of contamination. Cancel every sexual corruption.

			Every satanic seed that is planted in my life, I pray for it to dissolved now!

			Yea, they shall not be planted; yea, they shall not be sown: yea, their stock shall not take root in the earth: and he shall also blow upon them, and they shall wither, and the whirlwind shall take them away as stubble.

			Isaiah 40:24

			Command the demonic seed planted in your body and mind, to dissolve now!

			I flush it out of my mind; I flush it out of my body; I flush it out of my system in the name of Jesus!

			12.	Pray against Traps or Devices that are designed to Catch You.

			I escape schemes, they shall not take root in my life. I demolish all traps. My soul has escaped from every trap. Every device designed to catch me, I destroy it. I decree and declare they shall not prosper.

			Thank God for the divine escapes and thank God for the divine freedom from the traps of the enemy.

			But thou, O Lord, art a God full of compassion, and gracious, longsuffering, and plenteous in mercy and truth.

			O turn unto me, and have mercy upon me; give thy strength unto thy servant, and save the son of thine handmaid. Shew me a token for good; that they which hate me may see it, and be ashamed: because thou, Lord, hast holpen me, and comforted me.

			Psalm 86:15-17

			Thank You, Lord, for giving me strength. Thank You Lord for being gracious to me, thank You for fighting for me.

			Every snake that has been attached to my leg that goes everywhere with me, I cut you off now! I bind you now and I cut off your head now.

			Communion Prayer

			Whatever medicine cannot solve, this communion can solve

			Let this communion present healing and protection

			When I see the blood, I will pass over

			Whatever represents death, it shall not touch you

			You will escape any planned death from the enemy because of this communion

			If you believe that this prayer has been a blessing, then raise an alter by giving a thanksgiving offering

			Thank you, Jesus.

			Amen.





CHAPTER 26




Prayer for Confessions of Sins & Prayer for the Gifts of the Holy Spirit




		 			Give ear to my words, O Lord, consider my meditation.

			Psalm 5:1



		 			Note that even when you are thinking, it is a type of prayer. This shows that you are getting closer to God.

			My voice shalt thou hear in the morning, O Lord; in the morning will I direct my prayer unto thee, and will look up.

			Psalm 5:3

			God must hear our voice in the morning. We must learn to pray to God without any special topic.

			And Azariah the son of Nathan was over the officers: and Zabud the son of Nathan was principal officer, and the king’s friend:

			1 Kings 4:5

			Just as David, had a friend called Zabud, we must also learn to be a friend of God, so we come to Him without any topic.

			Prayer to Confess Seven Types of Sins

			1. 	Pray to confess your wickedness.

			For thou art not a God that hath pleasure in wickedness: neither shall evil dwell with thee.

			Psalm 5:4

			God does not have pleasure in wickedness.

			•	Pray that whatever is wicked and is wrong in our lives, 		God would forgive us.

			•	Pray also about the wickedness of our fathers, mothers 		or our grandparents to our fourth generation.

			And as Jesus passed by, he saw a man which was blind from his birth. And his disciples asked him, saying, Master, who did sin, this man, or his parents, that he was born blind?

			John 9:1-2

			The scripture in John 9:1-2 shows that we can suffer so much for something that we have not done. We can suffer from something that our parents have done.

			Lord whatever represents wickedness in our lives please forgive us. Please take wickedness out of our lives. We confess our sins, Lord.

			2. 	Pray to confess your foolishness.

			Foolishness causes depression.

			Whatever represents foolishness in our lives and the lives of our fathers, please forgive us.

			Forsake the foolish, and live; and go in the way of understanding.

			Proverbs 9:6

			This verse shows that foolishness can lead to death.

			The proverbs of Solomon. A wise son maketh a glad father: but a foolish son is the heaviness of his mother.

			Proverbs 10:1

			A foolish son is a grief to his father, and bitterness to her that bare him.

			Proverbs 17:25

			We are praying against whatever foolishness we have applied that is affecting us now; using foolishness to make marital decisions which is now affecting us.

			Lord whatever foolishness has resulted in evil, please forgive us.

			Jesus answered, Thou couldest have no power at all against me, except it were given thee from above: therefore, he that delivered me unto thee hath the greater sin.

			John 19:11

			This verse shows us that there are levels of sins. Some sins are “greater” than others.

			3. 	Pray to confess your transgressions.

			These are boundaries that we should not have crossed, for example, insulting your parents, or David having an affair with Bathsheba and killing her husband. His punishment was going to be death until he confessed his transgression to Prophet Nathan.

			Wherefore hast thou despised the commandment of the LORD, to do evil in his sight? thou hast killed Uriah the Hittite with the sword, and hast taken his wife to be thy wife, and hast slain him with the sword of the children of Ammon.

			2 Samuel 12:9

			Seventy weeks are determined upon thy people and upon thy holy city, to finish the transgression, and to make an end of sins, and to make reconciliation for iniquity, and to bring in everlasting righteousness, and to seal up the vision and prophecy, and to anoint the most Holy.

			Daniel 9:24

			This verse contains three of the seven types of sin.

			Father, forgive my transgressions. Please forgive me for the lines I have crossed that I should not have crossed, be merciful unto me Lord.

			4. 	Prayer to confess our iniquities.

			An “iniquity” is to be oriented or twisted towards something abnormal.

			Seventy weeks are determined upon thy people and upon thy holy city, to finish the transgression, and to make an end of sins, and to make reconciliation for iniquity, and to bring in everlasting righteousness, and to seal up the vision and prophecy, and to anoint the most Holy.

			Daniel 9:24

			But he was wounded for our transgressions, he was bruised for our iniquities: the chastisement of our peace was upon him; and with his stripes we are healed.

			Isaiah 53:5

			If you put money in the hand of somebody who is twisted, it would not be good. Can God trust you with power? Can God trust you with people or His sheep? Can God trust you with money?

			Are you oriented towards quarrelling? Are you twisted towards fear?

			Pray and ask for forgiveness of your iniquities:

			•	Every orientation towards strife

			•	Every orientation towards pride and arrogance

			•	Every orientation towards the desire for more money

			•	Every orientation towards rebellion

			•	Every orientation towards disloyalty

			Please take it away in the name of Jesus.

			Please do not say bad things about your church in front of your children. They will be oriented or twisted in their mind and heart against the church. They will grow not to like the church and the leaders.

			Have mercy upon me, O God, according to thy loving kindness: according unto the multitude of thy tender mercies blot out my transgressions. Wash me thoroughly from mine iniquity, and cleanse me from my sin.

			Psalm 51:1-2

			Please wash me thoroughly from my orientations or iniquities Father. Cleanse me thoroughly with the blood of Jesus from my orientations. I confess my tendencies that are negative, Lord.

			Even where we come from has oriented or crooked us towards what is there. Father, as a Judge, please release me from all orientations by the blood of the Lamb. Release me from negative tendencies and crookedness, in the name of Jesus.

			5. 	Pray to confess your secret faults.

			These are the sins of the heart, these are the sins that nobody sees, except in the spirit realm.

			Who can understand his errors? cleanse thou me from secret faults.

			Keep back thy servant also from presumptuous sins; let them not have dominion over me: then shall I be upright, and I shall be innocent from the great transgression. Let the words of my mouth, and the meditation of my heart, be acceptable in thy sight, O Lord, my strength, and my redeemer.

			Psalm 19:12-14

			Therefore, do not go on passing judgment before the time, but wait until the Lord comes who will both bring to light the things hidden in the darkness and disclose the motives of men’s hearts; and then each man’s praise will come to him from God.

			1 Corinthians 4:5 NASB

			Notice - all judgments are based on our motives or what is in our mind.

			Father, whatever is in our minds and our hearts, please cleanse and forgive us.

			And it was so, when the days of their feasting were gone about, that Job sent and sanctified them, and rose up early in the morning, and offered burnt offerings according to the number of them all: for Job said, It may be that my sons have sinned, and cursed God in their hearts. Thus did Job continually.

			Now there was a day when the sons of God came to present themselves before the LORD, and Satan came also among them.

			And the LORD said unto Satan, Whence comest thou? Then Satan answered the LORD, and said, From going to and fro in the earth, and from walking up and down in it. And the LORD said unto Satan, Hast thou considered my servant Job, that there is none like him in the earth, a perfect and an upright man, one that feareth God, and escheweth evil?

			Then Satan answered the LORD, and said, Doth Job fear God for nought? Hast not thou made an hedge about him, and about his house, and about all that he hath on every side? thou hast blessed the work of his hands, and his substance is increased in the land.

			But put forth thine hand now, and touch all that he hath, and he will curse thee to thy face. And the LORD said unto Satan, Behold, all that he hath is in thy power; only upon himself put not forth thine hand. So Satan went forth from the presence of the LORD.

			Job 1:5-12

			“Does he serve you for nothing?” Satan accused Job of his motives for serving God faithfully and it opened a door for satan to attack Job and wipe away everything he owned.

			A great attack can come upon you based on what is in your heart or based on what your motive is. Motives change everything and motives alone can wipe out everything in our lives.

			Father, whatever our motive that is not good, please forgive us.

			Cleanse me from my secret faults, cleanse me from my wicked pursuits and cleanse me from my wicked plans, in the name of Jesus.

			6. 	Pray to confess your spiritual sins.

			Having therefore, these promises, dearly beloved, let us cleanse ourselves from all filthiness of the flesh and spirit, perfecting holiness in the fear of God.

			2 Corinthians 7:1

			Examples of Spiritual Sins

			You can have filthiness of the flesh and of the SPIRIT.

			 				UnbeliefLet us labour therefore, to enter into that rest, lest any man fall after the same example of unbelief.

Hebrews 4:11

•	Your fear of men and not standing up for what is right

And Saul said, They have brought them from the Amalekites: for the people spared the best of the sheep and of the oxen, to sacrifice unto the Lord thy God; and the rest we have utterly destroyed.

1 Samuel 15:15

•	Not knowing God

Ah sinful nation, a people laden with iniquity, a seed of evildoers, children that are corrupters: they have forsaken the Lord, they have provoked the Holy One of Israel unto anger, they are gone away backward.

Isaiah 1:4

•	Not being grateful or being unthankful

For God is not unrighteous to forget your work and labour of love, which ye have shewed toward his name, in that ye have ministered to the saints, and do minister.

Hebrews 6:10





			It is unrighteousness to forget somebody’s work and labour of love.

			Father, have mercy on me for unbelief, for being ungrateful and for not knowing You. Forgive us Lord.

			7.	 Pray to confess your debts.

			Debts are what you owe God.

			And forgive us our debts, as we forgive our debtors.

			Matthew 6:12

			Therefore, to him that knoweth to do good, and doeth it not, to him it is sin.

			James 4:17

			What we know to do that which is a good thing, and we do not do it, it is something that we owe God.

			Father, whatever debt we owe You, please forgive us.

			Whatever I have to do that I have not done, please forgive me.

			The souls that I have to win that I have not won, please forgive me.

			Father, whatever money I owe that I have not paid, please forgive me.

			So he called every one of his lord’s debtors unto him, and said unto the first, How much owest thou unto my lord?

			Then said he to another, And how much owest thou? And he said, An hundred measures of wheat. And he said unto him, Take thy bill, and write fourscore.

			Luke 16:5, 7

			How much do you owe the Lord? We owe the Lord so much that we are not paying.

			Forgive us our debts, Lord.

			If he hath wronged thee, or oweth thee ought, put that on mine account;

			I Paul have written it with mine own hand, I will repay it: albeit I do not say to thee how thou owest unto me even thine own self besides.

			Philemon 1:18-19

			Lord, I owe You my whole life. We owe You our lives, we owe our days to You and we owe our moments to You, Lord. I am sorry for giving my youthfulness to something else.

			Prayer for the Increase of the Anointing

			If ye then, being evil, know how to give good gifts unto your children: how much more shall your heavenly Father give the Holy Spirit to them that ask him?

			Luke 11:13

			We are praying for the Holy Spirit.

			We need You Holy Spirit. Come please, what we need is You.

			1. Pray for the Gifts of the Holy Spirit.

			The gift is an endowment. So what gift have you been given?

			A gift is something you do with ease whilst others struggle to do the same.

			Now there are diversities of gifts, but the same Spirit.

			1 Corinthians 12:4

			A man’s gift maketh room for him, and bringeth him before great men.

			Proverbs 18:16

			More gifts mean more opportunities; more gifts, more stages.

			Lord, don’t let me be “giftless” (without any gift).

			Every “giftless” person is receiving a gift. “Giftlessness” is ending. You will be there because of your gift. You will never be called dry. You will never be called empty.

			a) 	Pray for additional gifts - every man according to his ability to handle the gift.

			Father, please give me more talents according to my ability. Additional gifts, Lord.

			b) 	Pray for the stirring of the gifts.

			Wherefore I put thee in remembrance that thou stir up the gift of God, which is in thee by the putting on of my hands.

			2 Timothy 1:6

			Pray for all dormant gifts to be stirred. Whatever is unstirred must be stirred now in the name of Jesus.

			Father, stir up any sleeping gift in me, power of the Holy Spirit come.

			I am asking for additional gifts, I am asking for You to stir up the dormant gifts.

			Pray for any gift that has never been activated, to be activated now.

			It is happening now; it is stirring up now; it is being activated now. Because of the gifts, all sorts of people will come into your ministry; all sorts of people will come into your life!

			c) 	Pray for every gift that has been neglected.

			Neglect not the gift that is in thee, which was given thee by prophecy, with the laying on of the hands of the presbytery.

			1 Timothy 4:14

			Neglected things are neglected because they are not liked. That is why there are many dresses in our closets and wardrobes that we don’t wear.

			Pray for every neglected gift to be activated and pull the neglected gifts out of your closet.

			Neglect not the gift that is in given to you BY PROPHECY WITH THE LAYING ON OF THE HANDS.

			Neglect not the gift that is in thee, which was given thee by prophecy, with the laying on of the hands of the presbytery.

			1 Timothy 4:14

			What are the prophetic words or statements that have come to you? What are the comments that have been spoken over you?

			d) 	Pray for your proper gifts. A proper gift is a personal gift or private gifts.

			For I would that all men were even as I myself. But every man hath his proper gift of God, one after this manner, and another after that.

			1 Corinthians 7:7

			These are personalized abilities. They are super abilities - pray to receive them.

			For I long to see you, that I may impart unto you some spiritual gift, to the end ye may be established;

			Romans 1:11

			The spiritual gifts are what will establish us in ministry and in life.

			2. 	Pray for the Administration of the Holy Spirit.

			And there are differences of administrations, but the same Lord.

			1 Corinthians 12:5

			3.	Pray for the Operations of the Holy Spirit.

			And there are diversities of operations, but it is the same God which worketh all in all.

			1 Corinthians 12:6

			4. 	Pray for the Manifestations of the Holy Spirit.

			This the only part of the Holy Spirit that Paul explained.

			But the manifestation of the Spirit is given to every man to profit withal.

			1 Corinthians 12:7

			5.	Pray to bind any demon in your life, bind now.

			Bind the snake and cut off the head of any snake in your life.

			Bind spiritual lions and spiritual dogs.

			And seeing a fig tree afar off having leaves, he came, if haply he might find any thing thereon: and when he came to it, he found nothing but leaves; for the time of figs was not yet. And Jesus answered and said unto it, No man eat fruit of thee hereafter for ever. And his disciples heard it.

			Mark 11:13-14

			Lift your hands and declare judgment on every fig tree that represents mockery. Declare judgment now, may they wither to their root.

			Communion Prayer

			Father, thank You for victory over the enemy through the blood of the Lamb

			Right now, we come to Jesus with His body in our hands

			May this blessing come into my house now

			Father, the blood of Jesus, let it take away our sins

			Let the angel of death pass by us because of the body and the blood.

CHAPTER 27




Prayer for Forgiveness of Sins; Prayer for the Operation of the Holy Spirit & Prayer for a Blessing




		 			And Elijah the Tishbite, who was of the inhabitants of Gilead, said unto Ahab, As the Lord God of Israel liveth, before whom I stand, there shall not be dew nor rain these years, but according to my word.

			1 Kings 17:1



		 			And Elijah the Tishbite, who was of the inhabitants of Gilead, said unto Ahab, As the Lord God of Israel liveth, before whom I stand, there shall not be dew nor rain these years, but according to my word.

			1 Kings 17:1

			When Elijah introduced himself, his credentials was that he stands before God. Standing before the Lord means staying around the presence of God. This is one of the greatest credentials a person can have.

			And the angel answering said unto him, I am Gabriel, that stand in the presence of God; and am sent to speak unto thee, and to shew thee these glad tidings.

			Luke 1:19

			The greatest credentials you can ever have is standing in the presence of the Lord like Abraham, Elijah and angel Gabriel.

			Prayer for the Forgiveness of Your Sins and the Sins of Your Fathers

			Yea, all Israel have transgressed thy law, even by departing, that they might not obey thy voice; therefore, the curse is poured upon us, and the oath that is written in the law of Moses the servant of God, because we have sinned against him.

			Daniel 9:11

			There are 2 types of curses:

			i.	Curses that are written in the Bible

			ii.	Curses from life

			There are biblical curses in our lives, for example, “cursed is the ground” and the curse on Ham’s sons.

			There are other curses that may have been brought on from your life. There are also curses that may have brought into your life from your father and grandfathers’ lives.

			1.	Pray for the forgiveness of your sins and the sins of your parents.

			Prayer About Your Past Sins

			There are situations that you cannot escape from. The only way to escape is to pray for forgiveness.

			That upon you may come all the righteous blood shed upon the earth, from the blood of righteous Abel unto the blood of Zacharias son of Barachias, whom ye slew between the temple and the altar.

			Matthew 23:35

			This blood that was shed is not blood that you have shed yourself. It is blood that has already been shed and we are paying for it. From the blood of Abel to the blood of Zachariah - this is something that was done before the people being spoken to were born.

			Verily I say unto you, All these things shall come upon this generation.

			Matthew 23:36

			This is why sometimes we ask “Why me?” There are reasons for many mysterious things. Therefore, whatever represents a curse or judgment that is coming, which we have nothing to do with, let us pray for mercy.

			Father we confess our sins and the sins of our fathers. Whatever we have invoked, we pray for mercy in Jesus’ name!

			Prayer for the Holy Spirit, and for Blessings to Neutralize the Curses

			Behold, I set before you this day a blessing and a curse;

			A blessing, if ye obey the commandments of the Lord your God, which I command you this day: And a curse, if ye will not obey the commandments of the Lord your God, but turn aside out of the way which I command you this day, to go after other gods, which ye have not known.

			Deuteronomy 11:26-28

			There are blessings and curses. One of the ways to neutralize the curses is to pray for blessings. Even though we are saved, curses are still active.

			Which man can say “I don’t sweat for bread”? Which woman can say “I don’t go through the pain of marriage and having children”?

			Pray for the Spirit of wisdom and guidance as we escape the curses.

			Father, show me where the blessings are. I need the blessings, please show me.

			Prayer to Labour in the Spirit for Blessings

			1. 	Pray for the blessings for the poor in spirit.

			Blessed are the poor in spirit: for theirs is the kingdom of heaven.

			Matthew 5:3

			What does this mean?

			The word “poor” is “Tokos”, which means somebody who is crouching.

			Pray that God will bless you in whatever powerless and helpless state that you are in.

			Father by Your blessings, bless us. Let divine assistance come to the powerless and to the helpless.

			Whatever blessing belongs to the poor in spirit, please I lift up my hand, pour out Your blessing, Lord.

			Whatever the poverty-stricken state is, there is a blessing that comes to people in such a state.

			2. 	Pray for the blessing of your tears. There is a blessing that comes to those who mourn. There are things that make you cry.

			Blessed are they that mourn: for they shall be comforted.

			Matthew 5:4

			Mention 7 things that have made you cry and pray for blessings for each of them.

			Mention each of the 7 things that made you cry:

			1.	Maybe marriage made you cry.

			2.	Maybe education made you cry.

			3.	Maybe sin has made you cry.

			4.	Maybe rejection made you cry.

			5.	Maybe heartbreaks made you cry.

			6.	Maybe sickness or pain made you cry.

			7.	Maybe a diagnosis made you cry.

			Your tears are turning into blessings as you pray for blessings for each of these seven things.

			Pray for the blessings for the tears on your pillow.

			Father, we tap into the blessings of those who are disappointed, the blessings of funerals and deaths in the name of Jesus.

			3. 	Pray for the blessings for meek people.

			Blessed are the meek: for they shall inherit the earth.

			Matthew 5:5

			Pray for the blessings that come upon those that are meek. If we are proud, arrogant, and haughty, this blessing cannot come upon us. There is a blessing for someone who is humble, therefore, whatever pride is in me, the arrogance, please take them away, Lord.

			4.	Pray for the blessings of giving.

			I have shewed you all things, how that so labouring ye ought to support the weak, and to remember the words of the Lord Jesus, how he said, It is more blessed to give than to receive.

			Acts 20:35

			Perhaps you are a melancholic and you calculate for three months in advance before giving and it therefore, does not allow you to give easily.

			Pray that you will become a giver from today.

			Father, make me a giver, Lord, let the blessings of giving come upon me. Teach me, Lord.

			No man taketh it from me, but I lay it down of myself. I have power to lay it down, and I have power to take it again. This commandment have I received of my Father.

			John 10:18

			Financial wealth is based on the power to give.

			Father give me the power to give: power to give to support your church, power to give to support missionaries and missions in the name of Jesus.

			Blessed are they which do hunger and thirst after righteousness: for they shall be filled.

			Matthew 5:6

			5.	Pray for the blessings of hungering and thirsting after righteousness. This is the prayer for a strong desire for righteousness.

			Pray for a strong desire for the righteousness of God, a hunger for the righteousness of God, a thirst for the righteousness of God. Pray for the blessing of being merciful.

			Blessed are the merciful: for they shall obtain mercy.

			Matthew 5:7

			If you are a father, your household is your kingdom. If you are a pastor, you have the opportunity to show mercy. If you are in a human institution, you have the opportunity to show mercy.

			Please note that certain curses come to our lives because we are not merciful. Therefore, pray that you will be merciful to others.

			Father, make my heart a forgiving heart, one to forgive others.

			6.	Pray for the blessings of being pure in heart.

			Blessed are the pure in heart: for they shall see God.

			Matthew 5:8

			We are praying for blessings because a curse is poured out. For example, a curse was poured upon Eli’s children.

			The time is coming when I will put an end to your family, so it will no longer serve as my priests. All the members of your family will die before their time. None will reach old age. You will watch with envy as I pour out prosperity on the people of Israel. But no members of your family will ever live out their days.

			1 Samuel 2:31-32 NLT

			If you see poverty, and a lot of deaths happening, you may not easily know that they are coming from curses that have been poured out on that family line. Therefore, we are praying for blessings to neutralize these curses.

			A half pill of chloroquine in a glass of water changes everything, because chloroquine is very bitter - just like a drop of ink in water changes everything. Pray for the blessing to be pure in heart.

			Lay hands on your heart and ask God to make you pure in heart.

			7.	Pray for the blessing of peace-making.

			Blessed are the peacemakers: for they shall be called the children of God.

			Matthew 5:9

			There is a blessing for somebody who makes or brings peace. You are a maker of peace.

			Father, make me a peacemaker in my home, in my marriage and in my family in the name of Jesus.

			8.	Pray for the blessings for those who are persecuted for righteousness’ sake.

			Blessed are they, which are persecuted for righteousness’ sake: for theirs is the kingdom of heaven.

			Matthew 5:10

CHAPTER 28




Prayer that Changes Things; Prayer on Angelic Activities & Prayer to Bind Demons




		 			And as he prayed, the fashion of his countenance was altered, and his raiment was white and glistering.

			Luke 9:29



		 			When you pray, you change. The two keys here are PRAYED and ALTERED. Prayer is not about asking for milk and sugar. Prayer brings you closer to God. Let us pray for the Lord to give us a different life.

			Father, change things in our lives, alter things in our ministries as we pray.

			1.	Pray to know things

			And I say unto you, Ask, and it shall be given you; seek, and ye shall find; knock, and it shall be opened unto you.

			Luke 11:9

			Let them bring them forth, and shew us what shall happen: let them shew the former things, what they be, that we may consider them, and know the latter end of them; or declare us things for to come.

			What are “the former things”? What are the former things in our ministries? What are the former things in our lives? Anything that happens has a latter end. Whatever has happened in our lives, we must consider and know the latter end.

			In my ministry, show me the former things that I may consider them and know the latter end.

			In my relationships, show me the former things that I may consider them and know the latter end.

			In my marriage, show me the former things that I may consider them and know the latter end.

			Please show me how things are going to end - what is the wisdom, Lord? Let me have the right mind and the right thinking.

			2.	Pray for God to show you the future

			Let them bring them forth, and shew us what shall happen: let them shew the former things, what they be, that we may consider them, and know the latter end of them; or declare us things for to come.

			Isaiah 41:22

			“Let them bring them forth, and shew us what shall happen:”

			Pray for God to show you the future.

			And Ham, the father of Canaan, saw the nakedness of his father, and told his two brethren without.

			And it came to pass, when all the kings of the Amorites, which were on the side of Jordan westward, and all the kings of the Canaanites, which were by the sea, heard that the Lord had dried up the waters of Jordan from before the children of Israel, until we were passed over, that their heart melted, neither was there spirit in them any more, because of the children of Israel.

			And Noah began to be an husbandman, and he planted a vineyard: And he drank of the wine, and was drunken; and he was uncovered within his tent. And Ham, the father of Canaan, saw the nakedness of his father, and told his two brethren without. And Shem and Japheth took a garment, and laid it upon both their shoulders, and went backward, and covered the nakedness of their father; and their faces were backward, and they saw not their father’s nakedness. And Noah awoke from his wine, and knew what his younger son had done unto him. And he said, Cursed be Canaan; a servant of servants shall he be unto his brethren. And he said, Blessed be the Lord God of Shem; and Canaan shall be his servant. God shall enlarge Japheth, and he shall dwell in the tents of Shem; and Canaan shall be his servant.

			And Ham, the father of Canaan, saw the nakedness of his father, and told his two brethren without... And he said, Cursed be Canaan; a servant of servants shall he be unto his brethren.
