			Behold, when we come into the land, thou shalt bind this line of scarlet thread in the window which thou didst let us down by: and thou shalt bring thy father, and thy mother, and thy brethren, and all thy father’s household, home unto thee.

CHAPTER 29




Prayer on Psalm 31; Prayer Concerning Your Ministry; Prayer for Humility & Prayer to Bind Demons




		 			And God said, Let there be light: and there was light. And God saw the light, that it was good: and God divided the light from the darkness. And God called the light Day, and the darkness he called Night. And the evening and the morning were the first day... And God created great whales, and every living creature that moveth, which the waters brought forth abundantly, after their kind, and every winged fowl after his kind: and God saw that it was good. And God blessed them, saying, Be fruitful, and multiply, and fill the waters in the seas, and let fowl multiply in the earth.

			Genesis 1:3-5, 21-22

		 			The burden of Dumah. He calleth to me out of Seir, Watchman, what of the night? Watchman, what of the night? The watchman said, The morning cometh, and also the night: if ye will enquire, enquire ye: return, come.

			Isaiah 21:11-12

			1. 	Because we pray, we won’t be afraid of what is coming in the morning. Therefore, pray and let the will of God be done in our lives.

			In thee, O Lord, do I put my trust; let me never be ashamed: deliver me in thy righteousness.

			Psalm 31:1

			2. 	Father, let me not be ashamed by the decisions I have taken in my life.

			Whatever moves l have made in my life, let me never be ashamed. As I am serving You, let me never be ashamed.

			Bow down thine ear to me; deliver me speedily: be thou my strong rock, for an house of defence to save me.

			Psalm 31:2

			3. 	Whatever case you are in, no case is too hard for the Lord.

			Ask God to deliver you speedily and defend you.

			For thou art my rock and my fortress; therefore, for thy name’s sake lead me, and guide me.

			Psalm 31:3

			4. 	Father, lead me and guide me.

			Lead me in my ministry, lead me in my finances, lead me in my marriage and lead me in my friendships and relationships. Whatever blinds me from following you please take it away.

			Lead me Lord! Every unleadable and unguidable spirit and attitude, please take it away.

			 Pull me out of the net that they have laid privily for me: for thou art my strength.

			Psalm 31:4

			5.	Pray for divine help to pull us out of a net.

			Whatever represents a net, you are coming out of it.

			Every private meeting to lay a net on me, every private conspiracy to lay a net on me, let me come out.

			Into thine hand I commit my spirit: thou hast redeemed me, O Lord God of truth.

			Psalm 31:5

			Father, into Thy hand I commit my spirit. May I not be led by circumstances. Father I commit my life and I commit my soul to You.

			6.	Pray to hate lies and vanity.

			I have hated them that regard lying vanities: but I trust in the Lord.

			Psalm 31:6

			A lot of things are not real, e.g., people drive cars that are not real and people live in houses that are not real.

			Pray that God will not let you be impressed with things that are not real. Pray that God will alert you in your spirit that you will not regard lying vanities.

			Deliver me from being impressed with mortgages.

			Deliver me from being impressed with people who speak “word salads”.

			Deliver me from being impressed with people who speak worldly wisdom.

			I will be glad and rejoice in thy mercy: for thou hast considered my trouble; thou hast known my soul in adversities;

			Psalm 31:7

			Whatever represents trouble, Lord consider me and help me. Father, know my soul in adversities, whatever be the trouble in my ministry, my finances and my marriage, please consider me that I may be rejoice again.

			And hast not shut me up into the hand of the enemy: thou hast set my feet in a large room.

			Psalm 31:8

			7. 	Pray for a large room in your ministry.

			Father, set my feet in large room in my ministry

			Father, set my feet in large room in my business(es)

			8.	Pray that every restriction in your life is taken away.

			If you don’t have a visa or status to travel, you cannot travel.

			Pray that God will take away all restrictions.

			Father set my feet in a large room. Father, give me a larger operating space, in my ministry, in my finances and in my business(es). Please give me a large room.

			Prophetically, I put my feet in many airports to preach in the name of Jesus!

			And hast not shut me up into the hand of the enemy: thou hast set my feet in a large room.

			Psalm 31:8

			9. 	Pray that you will not be shut up into the hand of the enemy.

			You will not be shut up.

			Have mercy upon me, O Lord, for I am in trouble: mine eye is consumed with grief, yea, my soul and my belly.

			For my life is spent with grief, and my years with sighing: my strength faileth because of mine iniquity, and my bones are consumed.

			Psalm 31:9-10

			Verse 10 shows us how sin affects our health. Therefore, ask God to have mercy for your iniquities and forgive you for your sins.

			Have mercy upon me Lord.

			I was a reproach among all mine enemies, but especially among my neighbours, and a fear to mine acquaintance: they that did see me without fled from me.

			I am forgotten as a dead man out of mind: I am like a broken vessel.

			Psalm 31:11-12

			10.	Pray that whatever is not good about you at close quarters, God will take away.

			Neighbours are those who know us closely. People can look at us and say, “I’d like to be like you.” Others who are near you, don’t want to be like you because they know you.

			Whatever it is about you that is not nice, ask God to take it away.

			Father, cleanse me from whatever is not nice about me at close quarters. Those who are close to us are not happy, but those who are afar are always praising us. Whatever is not nice about me, Lord extract it by the power of the Holy Spirit!

			I am forgotten as a dead man out of mind: I am like a broken vessel.

			Psalm 31:12

			11.	Pray that anyone who has forgotten you will remember you.

			Like the chief butler who forgot Joseph and later remembered him, pray that your chief butler will remember you.

			Yet did not the chief butler remember Joseph, but forgat him.

			Genesis 40:23

			To be forgotten is not a nice thing.

			12.	Pray that when people are trying to find beloveds, you will be remembered.

			13.	Pray that when people are looking for somebody to promote, you will be remembered.

			14.	Pray that when people are looking for business partners, you will be remembered.

			15.	Pray that whoever ought to remember you, will surely remember you.

			Father, may I not be around and be like a dead man. May I not be forgotten like a dead man.

			Who have you also forgotten about that you should remember?

			Pray that God will bring to your mind, anyone that you should remember whom you have forgotten.

			Father, who have I forgotten that I should remember? Stir up my memories and cause me to remember anyone I have to remember.

			16.	Slandering is saying bad things about somebody behind their back. Slandering is the serpent seed in human nature.

			Pray against:

			 				Slandering your pastor.

				Slandering your ministry.

				Slandering the church.

				Slandering your marriage.

				Slandering your children.



			We declare holy fire against slandering, we declare divine judgment on slanderers, we tie the tongues of slanderers and we terminate the activities of slanderers.

			17.	Pray against premature death in any form.

			But I trusted in thee, O Lord: I said, Thou art my God.

			My times are in thy hand: deliver me from the hand of mine enemies, and from them that persecute me.

			Psalm 31:14-15

			Lift your hands and pray against any scheme, any plan to take away your life prematurely. Pray against any sickness, any pandemic, any accident, any bad news, designed to take away your life prematurely.

			Wilt thou shew wonders to the dead? shall the dead arise and praise thee? Selah. Shall thy lovingkindness be declared in the grave? or thy faithfulness in destruction? Shall thy wonders be known in the dark? and thy righteousness in the land of forgetfulness? But unto thee have I cried, O Lord; and in the morning shall my prayer prevent thee.

			Psalm 88:10-13

			Father there is no point for me to die at this time. Therefore, I come against premature death. I reject the spirit of death. I reject evil diagnoses.

			18.	Pray and ask God to shine His face upon you. A smiling face makes one look beautiful, especially with girls.

			Father, shine Your face upon thy servant. Please don’t turn away Your face from me, Lord.

			Make thy face to shine upon thy servant: save me for thy mercies’ sake.

			Psalm 31:16

			Let me not be ashamed, O Lord; for I have called upon thee: let the wicked be ashamed, and let them be silent in the grave.

			Psalm 31:17

			Let me not be ashamed, Lord. Let the wicked be ashamed.

			Let the lying lips be put to silence; which speak grievous things proudly and contemptuously against the righteous.

			Psalm 31:18

			All lying lips that speak grievously against me, may they be put to silence. Tie their tongues O Lord, Father expose them.

			Oh how great is thy goodness, which thou hast laid up for them that fear thee; which thou hast wrought for them that trust in thee before the sons of men!

			Psalm 31:19

			Father, let me see goodness in my life, let me see goodness in my ministry and let me see goodness in my marriage. Father, I pray for your goodness to be released in my life.

			Thou shalt hide them in the secret of thy presence from the pride of man: thou shalt keep them secretly in a pavilion from the strife of tongues.

			Psalm 31:20

			19.	Pray for the presence of God in your life.

			And Joseph was brought down to Egypt; and Potiphar, an officer of Pharaoh, captain of the guard, an Egyptian, bought him of the hands of the Ishmeelites, which had brought him down thither.

			And the LORD was with Joseph, and he was a prosperous man; and he was in the house of his master the Egyptian.

			And his master saw that the LORD was with him, and that the LORD made all that he did to prosper in his hand.

			And Joseph found grace in his sight, and he served him: and he made him overseer over his house, and all that he had he put into his hand.

			And it came to pass from the time that he had made him overseer in his house, and over all that he had, that the LORD blessed the Egyptian’s house for Joseph’s sake;

			and the blessing of the LORD was upon all that he had in the house, and in the field.

			And he left all that he had in Joseph’s hand; and he knew not ought he had, save the bread which he did eat. And Joseph was a goodly person, and well favoured.

			Genesis 39:1-6

			The presence of God makes you prosper. The people that encountered Joseph, entrusted and left things to him; both in prison and in the palace. Pharaoh left everything to him.

			Father hide me in Your presence. Let Your presence be with me like It was with Joseph. Father, in the same way Joseph found grace in the sight of his master, may I find grace in the sight of my manager.

			20.	Pray that you will find grace in the sight of Jesus. May You be pleased with me, Lord; may I find grace in Your sight.

			Thank You Lord for making me overseer over Your special people. Thank You for Your presence in my ministry and thank You for Your presence in my life.

			And it came to pass after these things, that his master’s wife cast her eyes upon Joseph; and she said, Lie with me.

			But he refused, and said unto his master’s wife, Behold, my master wotteth not what is with me in the house, and he hath committed all that he hath to my hand;

			There is none greater in this house than I; neither hath he kept back any thing from me but thee, because thou art his wife: how then can I do this great wickedness, and sin against God?

			And it came to pass, as she spake to Joseph day by day, that he hearkened not unto her, to lie by her, or to be with her.

			Genesis 39:7-10

			Whatever wicked thing that would like to take away the presence of God from your life is aborted now.

			I separate myself from whatever wickedness is trying to attach itself to me.

			21.	Pray against great wickedness after you have been entrusted, anointed, appointed, promoted, elevated and have been introduced to a high place.

			Blessed be the Lord: for he hath shewed me his marvellous kindness in a strong city.

			Psalm 31:21

			Lord show me your marvelous kindness in this state and in this city. Show me O Lord.

			For I said in my haste, I am cut off from before thine eyes: nevertheless thou heardest the voice of my supplications when I cried unto thee. O love the Lord, all ye his saints: for the Lord preserveth the faithful, and plentifully rewardeth the proud doer.

			Psalm 31:22-23

			22.	Pray that you will love the Lord.

			That Christ may dwell in your hearts by faith; that ye, being rooted and grounded in love, May be able to comprehend with all saints what is the breadth, and length, and depth, and height; And to know the love of Christ, which passeth knowledge, that ye might be filled with all the fulness of God.

			Ephesians 3:17-19

			23.	Pray that you will understand the length, the breadth and the depth and height of the love of God.

			24.	Pray that you will know the love of Christ. It is only by love that you can know something. You will never know God until you start loving God. You will never know an author’s books until you start loving the books.

			Lord, let me be rooted and grounded in Your love, I want to be rooted and grounded in Your love Lord.

			Be of good courage, and he shall strengthen your heart, all ye that hope in the Lord.

			Psalm 31:24

			That he would grant you, according to the riches of his glory, to be strengthened with might by his Spirit in the inner man;

			Ephesians 3:16

			Father, we pray for strength for the inner man. May I be strengthened in the inner man.

				Prayers Concerning Your Ministry

			1.	Pray to move on from your wounds.

			a)	Pray that personally you will move on in ministry to other places and countries.

			b)	Pray to move ahead on God’s calling. No more stagnation! I am moving on with great authority!

			c)	Pray to move on because the Lord told Moses to go forward.

			d)	Pray for courage to move on.

			Father, I ask for courage to move, courage to move to the next level of my calling.

			2.	Pray to go through any frightening door of your ministry.

			For a great door and effectual is opened unto me, and there are many adversaries.

			1 Corinthians 16:9

			A great door is open. Please give me the courage and the faith to go through, in the name of Jesus.

			3.	Pray for the Spirit or the mantle of humility.

			Humility makes you great in the kingdom of God. A manifestation of pride is when a person becomes too confident of something.

			Father, take “Luciferic” pride away from me,

			When you are humble, you will do whatever work that you are ask to do; cleaning gutters, cleaning toilets and going to villages for mission work, etc.

			4.	Pray that you will give your life to God.

			After you are saved, you cannot live for yourself anymore.

			Father, take my life, take my strength, take my car and take my money, etc.

			5.	Pray that you will be able to use the map of Matthew 16:25 to find your way to the mountain.

			For whosoever will save his life shall lose it: and whosoever will lose his life for my sake shall find it.

			Matthew 16:25

			More Prayers for Humility

			A soul is healed by being with children; it cleans us of “kingliness” and “queenliness”.

			 				Father, I want to be like Jesus. I want the mind of Jesus who made Himself of no reputation. Father, take away “Luciferian” spirits, take away “Luciferian” traits and please take away any bigness from me.

				Being with children heals the soul; therefore, please give me a childlike attitude. Father give me a childlike spirit. I need this mantle of humility.



			And said, Verily I say unto you, Except ye be converted, and become as little children, ye shall not enter into the kingdom of heaven.

			Matthew 18:3

			Prayers to Bind Demons

			Anything that is following you like a pet dog, we bind it. Pray against distortion of your life and your life story.

			Pray against any familiar spirit and monitoring spirit - I bind you now.

			Every secret snake that has entered my dwelling place - I bind you now.

			Every record that is against me, by the blood of Jesus - I wipe you out.

			I resist all satanic agents to destroy my life. Every demon power, I bind your power. Go out of my house, out of my room now!

			Lift up your hands and declare judgment on every impending danger, declare judgment now.

			Lift your right hand and rebuke every devourer that has walked through your house, through your ministry and through your finances.

			Satan, I curse you now and I demand total recovery of everything that has been stolen from me.

			Any set back that is staring you in your face and every disappointment - we bind it.

			We demand cancellation of all setbacks in the name of Jesus!

			Thank God for the superior blood of Jesus that speaks better things than any blood.

			I curse the spirit of the frog that has come to my house.

			Victory for the children of God, victory for the house of God and victory for Zion.

			Communion Prayer

			The Red Sea speaks of the blood

			When the Israelites went through the Red Sea that was the end of Pharaoh

			Any chariots following me are stopping today

			This is the end of any dog following you; the blood marks its end

			We thank God that through the body of Jesus, we are healed

			Everything that is chasing us, we are delivered from it

			As we all partake of this holy meal, bless the families, bless the houses and bless the children

			Let no darkness, dark shadow, or bad news come to anyone in this family

			And the blood shall be to you for a token upon the houses where ye are: and when I see the blood, I will pass over you, and the plague shall not be upon you to destroy you, when I smite the land of Egypt.

			Exodus 12:13

			Shew me a token for good; that they which hate me may see it, and be ashamed: because thou, Lord, hast holpen me, and comforted me.

			Psalm 86:17

			The blood of Jesus is a token for good

			Father we lift up the blood and we say this is the blood, let this be a token for our good

			 A good thing is happening, our sins are forgiven and our lives are made new

			Our mistakes and crookedness are rectified by this token for good.

			The Prophetic Blessings

			According to Your Word, a good token has been released

			A good omen and a good sign have been released

			All the bad things of your life, through the blood of Jesus, they are converted into something better and higher

			Let the blood that flowed through Jesus, flow through your life

			Let every mistake of your past, every shame and every disgraceful part of you be washed clean

			May you be a new person because of the blood

			Have a new house, a new home, a new family, and a better existence because of the mercies of God through the blood of Jesus

			Whatever you couldn’t lay hands on as an unbeliever, may you lay hands on them because of the mercies that have been shown you

			Show us a token for good O Lord, that they which hate me may see it

			Let this blessing be on you and your whole family in Jesus’ Name.

			Amen.





CHAPTER 30




Prayer on Seeing Signs & Prayer to Anoint Eyes




		 			And there was one Anna, a prophetess, the daughter of Phanuel, of the tribe of Aser: she was of a great age, and had lived with an husband seven years from her virginity;

			And she was a widow of about fourscore and four years, which departed not from the temple, but served God with fastings and prayers night and day.

			Luke 2:36-37

			There was also a prophet, Anna, the daughter of Penuel, of the tribe of Asher. She was very old; she had lived with her husband seven years after her marriage,

			and then was a widow until she was eighty-four. She never left the temple but worshiped night and day, fasting and praying.

			Luke 2:36-37

		 			This is the time for us to choose to serve God voluntarily because, how old will most of us be in the next 15 years? The choices we make with our free will are proof that we love God. We must serve God with fasting and prayers.

			Give thanks to God for the opportunity to serve Him.

			Lord, it is only a matter of time and we will stand before You. Therefore, we choose to serve You and we thank You for the call to serve You.

			There was also a prophet, Anna, the daughter of Penuel, of the tribe of Asher ... She never left the temple but worshiped night and day, fasting and praying. Coming up to them at that very moment, she gave thanks to God and spoke about the child to all who were looking forward to the redemption of Jerusalem.

			Luke 2:36-38 (NIV)

			Anna never left the temple she was there every day.

			Pray that your spiritual eyes will open like Anna so that when you see Jesus, you will “see”.

			Who is blind, but my servant? or deaf, as my messenger that I sent? who is blind as he that is perfect, and blind as the Lord’s servant?

			Seeing many things, but thou observest not; opening the ears, but he heareth not.

			Isaiah 42:19-20

			Israel, listen to me! You can hear, but you do not understand. Look to me! You can see, but you do not know what you are seeing.

			Isaiah 42:18 (NIRV)

			You may see, but not know what you are seeing. The physical things we see, what do they mean to us?

			Pray that as you serve God day and night, you will see well. Pray that you will be spiritually sensitive.

			I counsel thee to buy of me gold tried in the fire, that thou mayest be rich; and white raiment, that thou mayest be clothed, and that the shame of thy nakedness do not appear; and anoint thine eyes with eyesalve, that thou mayest see.

			Revelation 3:18

			Father, anoint my eyes with eye-salve so that I may see.

			Who is blind but my servant, and deaf like the messenger I send? Who is blind like the one in covenant with me, blind like the servant of the Lord?

			You have seen many things, but you pay no attention; your ears are open, but you do not listen.

			Isaiah 42:19-20 (NIV)

			Signs You Must be Able to See as You Become More Spiritual

			“Seeing” means you must be able to recognize them as a sign when you see physical things.

			1. 	The sign of a baby

			And this shall be a sign unto you; Ye shall find the babe wrapped in swaddling clothes, lying in a manger.

			Luke 2:12

			And Simeon blessed them, and said unto Mary his mother, Behold, this child is set for the fall and rising again of many in Israel; and for a sign which shall be spoken against;

			Luke 2:34

			Then Simeon blessed them and said to Mary, his mother: “This child is destined to cause the falling and rising of many in Israel, and to be a sign that will be spoken against,

			Luke 2:34 (NIV)

			2. 	The sign of humility

			All disloyal people are proud so a child is a sign of humility.

			Humility is harmlessness, a child is harmless.

			What is in your life that God is revealing to you?

			Pray that you will see the humility in a child and that you can also be like a child.

			Father teach me humility.

			3.	The sign of a baby and the sign of a child

			Babies and children are very attractive.

			Pray that you will be like a baby and a child and become very attractive.

			But rise, and stand upon thy feet: for I have appeared unto thee for this purpose, to make thee a minister and a witness both of these things which thou hast seen, and of those things in the which I will appear unto thee;

			Acts 26:16

			To open their eyes, and to turn them from darkness to light, and from the power of Satan unto God, that they may receive forgiveness of sins, and inheritance among them which are sanctified by faith that is in me.

			Acts 26:18

			First Anointing of the Eyes

			My eyes have changed. I am seeing many things. I will observe as the Prophet has led me to anoint my eyes.

			Lord, open my eyes to see the signs of babies and children. Show me the sign of my purpose to make me a witness of the things that I have seen, and open my eyes to turn me from darkness into light.

			4. 	The sign of trouble

			The sign of certain bad things happening to other people - this is a sign that what has happened to me can happen to you too.

			Say, I am your sign: like as I have done, so shall it be done unto them: they shall remove and go into captivity.

			Ezekiel 12:11

			Tell them, ‘The things I’ve done are a picture of what’s going to happen to you. “ ‘So what I’ve done will happen to you. You will be forced to leave home. You will be taken to Babylon as prisoners.

			Ezekiel 12:11 (NIRV)

			Which bad things have happened to somebody, which are going to happen to you if you do not change or if you do the same thing?

			A sign makes you think but nothing makes a spiritually dull person think.

			Signs and wonders are not only healing and manifestations of the Holy Spirit. Human beings are walking signs.

			Father, let my eyes be open to see many things.

			5. 	The sign of a family

			Behold, I and the children whom the Lord hath given me are for signs and for wonders in Israel from the Lord of hosts, which dwelleth in mount Zion.

			Isaiah 8:18

			Sometimes a spiritual family is a sign. The family next door can also be a sign. A genuinely rich family can be a sign of a big and very beautiful house with unhappiness inside it.

			What is the sign to you?

			6. 	The sign of nakedness and poverty

			After Ashdod was captured, the LORD said, “My servant Isaiah has gone around barefoot and naked for three years. He is a sign and reminder to Egypt and Cush about what will happen to them.

			Isaiah 20:3 (NIRV)

			The following are bad signs:

			a) Mortgages

			b) Debts

			c) Building a house when your kids are in university or college is a bad sign. It is very difficult to build at that time. However, building in your 20s or 30s is a good sign because it is much easier to build at that time.

			Father, what sign are you showing me that can help me prophetically?

			The sign of poverty is one of the commonest signs.

			Father, show me Isaiah (Isaiah 20:3) type of signs.

			Please let me see any poverty sign and show me the way out.

			7. 	A sign of an unusual death

			And this shall be a sign unto thee, that shall come upon thy two sons, on Hophni and Phinehas; in one day they shall die both of them.

			1 Samuel 2:34

			And Moses said, Hereby ye shall know that the Lord hath sent me to do all these works; for I have not done them of mine own mind.

			If these men die the common death of all men, or if they be visited after the visitation of all men; then the Lord hath not sent me.

			Numbers 16:28-29

			Then Moses said, “What is about to happen wasn’t my idea. The LORD has sent me to do everything I’m doing. Here is how you will know I’m telling you the truth.

			These men won’t die a natural death. Something will happen to them that doesn’t usually happen to people. If what I’m telling you doesn’t happen, then you will know that the LORD hasn’t sent me.

			Numbers 16:28-29 (NIRV)

			Death is part of life; but an unnatural and unusual death is a sign.

			Seeing many things, but thou observest not; opening the ears, but he heareth not.

			Isaiah 42:20

			“Seeing many things. they see not”. Open my eyes Lord.

			8. 	A sign of a man on a mission

			The word of the Lord also came unto me, saying, Son of man, thou dwellest in the midst of a rebellious house, which have eyes to see, and see not; they have ears to hear, and hear not: for they are a rebellious house.

			Therefore, thou son of man, prepare thee stuff for removing, and remove by day in their sight; and thou shalt remove from thy place to another place in their sight: it may be they will consider, though they be a rebellious house.

			Then shalt thou bring forth thy stuff by day in their sight, as stuff for removing: and thou shalt go forth at even in their sight, as they that go forth into captivity.

			Dig thou through the wall in their sight, and carry out thereby.

			In their sight shalt thou bear it upon thy shoulders, and carry it forth in the twilight: thou shalt cover thy face, that thou see not the ground: for I have set thee for a sign unto the house of Israel.

			Ezekiel 12:1-6

			Rebellion makes you blind and sin makes you blind.

			Father, show me the sign of a traveller, the sign of my future ministry.

			Second Anointing of the Eyes

			Father, thank You for the healing of blindness, from today may we see. We will have open visions.

			9. 	A sign of a desire of the eyes - a sign of a desire being taken away

			 It is an unusual thing when God strikes something we like with one stroke.

			Son of man, behold, I take away from thee the desire of thine eyes with a stroke: yet neither shalt thou mourn nor weep, neither shall thy tears run down.

			Ezekiel 24:16

			Whatever represents the desire of my eyes, please show me Lord.

			10. 	The sign of Jonas

			This is the sign of the U-turn and the sign of repentance.

			But he answered and said unto them, An evil and adulterous generation seeketh after a sign; and there shall no sign be given to it, but the sign of the prophet Jonas:

			For as Jonas was three days and three nights in the whale’s belly; so shall the Son of man be three days and three nights in the heart of the earth.

			Matthew 12:39-40

			What is the sign of Jonas? This is a sign of a disobedient minister who experienced difficulties. It is a sign of repentance and a sign of the U-turn.

			But Jonah rose up to flee unto Tarshish from the presence of the Lord, and went down to Joppa; and he found a ship going to Tarshish: so he paid the fare thereof, and went down into it, to go with them unto Tarshish from the presence of the Lord.

			Jonah 1:3

			11. 	The sign of a letter

			This is a letter from our fruits. The fruit of a person is a letter speaking to you.

			You yourselves are our letter. You are written on our hearts. Everyone knows you and reads you.

			You make it clear that you are a letter from Christ. You are the result of our work for God. You are a letter written not with ink but with the Spirit of the living God. You are a letter written not on tablets made out of stone but on human hearts.

			2 Corinthians 3:2-3 (NIRV)

			God gives us a message in our hearts. What kind of message is your life giving?

			Pray for fruits that can speak.

			Pray for fruits that can read.

			Pray for fruits that everybody can see.

			Third Anointing of the Eyes

			David was anointed three times.

			But strong meat belongeth to them that are of full age, even those who by reason of use have their senses exercised to discern both good and evil.

			Hebrews 5:14

			And of the rest of the oil that is in his hand shall the priest put upon the tip of the right ear of him that is to be cleansed, and upon the thumb of his right hand, and upon the great toe of his right foot, upon the blood of the trespass offering:

			Leviticus 14:17

			None of us will be blind according to the prophetic declaration by the Prophet. The anointing breaks the yoke of blindness.

			Seeing many things, but thou observest not; opening the ears, but he heareth not.

			Isaiah 42:20

			By this anointing (of our eyes) the verse - Isaiah 42:20 - shall not happen to us, but in seeing we will see.

			Prayer on Father / Son Relationships

			And he shall turn the heart of the fathers to the children, and the heart of the children to their fathers, lest I come and smite the earth with a curse.

			Malachi 4:6

			1.	Prayer from “The Final Quest1” by Rick Joyner

			Our pride prevents us from having real spiritual fathers. All children are rebellious, self-centered and that is why they need spiritual fathers. It is always the greater who must lay down his life for the lesser, that is why Christ laid down His life for us. Fathers lay down their lives for children.

			2.	Prayer for God to turn the hearts of the children to the fathers and the fathers to the children

			And he shall turn the heart of the fathers to the children, and the heart of the children to their fathers, lest I come and smite the earth with a curse.

			Malachi 4:6

			Lord, let all relationships between sons and fathers and fathers and sons that bring curses be made right today. Please turn the hearts of the children to their fathers and the hearts of the fathers to the children. Let there be a change in the sons and also a change in the fathers.

			Fathers are important, that we may live long.

			We are praying as sons and also praying as fathers.

			3.	Prayer to confess our sins, iniquities and transgressions

			Iniquity is an orientation towards a bad thing and transgression is crossing boundaries that we are not supposed to cross.

			Father, forgive me for iniquities, transgressions and sins. Please have mercy on me and forgive me.

			Communion Prayer

			The Bread

			Father, we lift up the bread everywhere

			Thank You for dying for us on the cross and thank You for forgiving us

			We lift up the body and we receive the body of Jesus Christ

			He was bruised for our iniquities, the chastisement of our peace was on Him

			The Blood

			The wine is for our sins

			Father for our mistakes, we beg and pray

			You were bruised for iniquities because we are twisted and the chastisement of our peace was upon Him

			Father, let this neutralize our curse, our mistake, our sin, our transgression, and our iniquities

			Receive it right now, in the name of Jesus Christ.

CHAPTER 31




Prayer for the Influence of the Holy Spirit; Prayer for Forgiveness; Prayer for Wounds to be Healed & Prayer to Bind Snake Spirits




		 			Now when all the people were baptized, it came to pass, that Jesus also being baptized, and praying, the heaven was opened,

			And the Holy Ghost descended in a bodily shape like a dove upon him, and a voice came from heaven, which said, Thou art my beloved Son; in thee I am well pleased.

			Luke 3:21-22

		 			This is what it open heavens means - the Holy Spirit comes upon your life. He comes to change and influence your life, and then you hear the voice of God, so you may please God.

			1.	Pray for open heavens.

			Father, grant me open heavens, please let me hear from you.

			Please note that when you pray, the heavens open.

			Therefore, believe that as you are praying the heavens will open and you will hear the voice of God.

			And the Holy Ghost descended in a bodily shape like a dove upon him, and a voice came from heaven, which said, Thou art my beloved Son; in thee I am well pleased.

			Luke 3:22

			2.	Pray that you will please God.

			To be pleasing to a great person is a mystery.

			Later, the great anger of King Xerxes calmed down. Then he remembered Vashti and what she had done. He also remembered the royal order he had sent out concerning her. At that time the king’s personal attendants made a suggestion. They said, “King Xerxes, let a search be made for some beautiful young virgins for you. Appoint some officials in every territory in your kingdom. Have them bring all these beautiful young women into the fort of Susa. Put them in the special place where the virgins stay. Then put Hegai in charge of them. He’s the official who serves you. He’s in charge of the women. Let beauty care be given to the new group of women. Then let the young woman who pleases you the most become queen in Vashti’s place.” The king liked that advice. So he followed it.

			There was a Jew living in the fort of Susa. He was from the tribe of Benjamin. His name was Mordecai. He was the son of Jair. Jair was the son of Shimei. Shimei was the son of Kish. Nebuchadnezzar had forced Mordecai to leave Jerusalem. He was among the prisoners who were carried off along with Jehoiachin. Jehoiachin had been king of Judah. Nebuchadnezzar was king of Babylon. Mordecai had a cousin named Hadassah. He had raised her because she didn’t have a father or mother. Hadassah was also called Esther. She had a lovely figure and was very beautiful. Mordecai had adopted her as his own daughter. He had done it when her father and mother died.

			After the king’s order and law were announced, many young women were brought to the fort of Susa. Hegai was put in charge of them. Esther was also taken to the king’s palace. She was put under the control of Hegai. He was in charge of the place where the virgins stayed. Esther pleased him. He showed her how happy he was with her. Right away he provided her with her beauty care and special food. He appointed seven female attendants to help her. They were chosen from the king’s palace. He moved her and her attendants into the best part of the place where the virgins stayed.

			Esther 2:1-9 (NIRV)

			Every one of the ladies was beautiful, a virgin and every one of the ladies was going to go through beauty treatments. What pleased Hegai for Esther to be chosen? It’s a mystery.

			Esther pleased him. He showed her how happy he was with her. Right away he provided her with her beauty care and special food. He appointed seven female attendants to help her. They were chosen from the king’s palace. He moved her and her attendants into the best part of the place where the virgins stayed.

			Esther 2:9 (NIRV)

			Pray that God will reveal to you the mystery of pleasing Him.

			Lord, show me the mystery to please You. Please grant me open heavens so that I may please You.

			When you work somewhere, find out who is important to please. Esther pleased Hegai. Is Hegai the final person to please? Can Esther stop at Hegai because Hegai likes her?

			Pray that you will not settle at “Hegai” in your life, at your work place and in your marital home, where “the Hegai” is a visitor.

			If a visitor is pleased with you, is that important in your marital home?

			Pray that you will not settle at any Hegai in your journey of life.

			Father, deliver us from the Hegais: the alternatives that are not the ultimate.

			Esther pleased Hegai. He showed her how happy he was with her. Right away he provided her with her beauty care and special food. He appointed seven female attendants to help her. They were chosen from the king’s palace. He moved her and her attendants into the best part of the place where the virgins stayed.

			 Esther 2:9 (NIRV)

			Hegai gave Esther certain benefits:

			 				He provided her with her beauty care.

				He provided her with special food.

				He appointed seven female attendants to help her.



			But the question is: is Hegai the final decider? It is rather the king that Esther has to please in order to be chosen.

			Pray that whatever represents deception in our lives, the Lord will take it away. Pray and bind the spirit of deception. Pray and bind every “feel good” deception.

			Note that Esther obeyed and listened to Mordecai; but she also listened and obeyed Hegai.

			In the evening she would go there. In the morning she would leave. Then she would go to the special place where the king’s concubines stayed. She would be put under the control of Shaashgaz. He was the king’s official who was in charge of the concubines. She would never return to the king unless he was pleased with her. He had to send for her by name before she could go to him again.

			Esther 2:14 (NIRV)

			Pray that you will not end up under a “Shaashgaz” place; and that even when you end up there, the king will remember your name.

			Pray that your name will be remembered at any organization that you are become part of.

			Pray that your name will be remembered in this great church.

			The king liked Esther more than he liked any of the other women. She pleased him more than any of the other virgins. So he put a royal crown on her head. He made her queen in Vashti’s place.

			Esther 2:17 (NIRV)

			The king liked Esther more than he liked any of the other women.

			May this happen to us in heaven as we stand in front of our king.

			3.	Pray for God’s correction

			Pray for God to correct you so that you may serve Him perfectly. Pray that God will correct you that you may serve Him more perfectly.

			4.	Pray for the anointing of Apelles

			Salute Apelles approved in Christ. Salute them which are of Aristobulus’ household.

			Romans 16:10

			Pray for the anointing, for the anointing of Apelles.

			Is your marriage a yoke of love or a yoke of bondage?

			a)	Pray against every marriage that is rotten inside, but looks good outside.

			b)	Pray that deception will not be found in your marriage.

			c)	Pray against hypocrisy in your marriage.

			d)	Pray that your marriage will not be a yoke of bondage.

			e)	Pray that your marriage will not destroy your ministry.

				(We are now fighting marriage demons. We are praying against all the demons in our marriages.)

			f)	Pray that your marriages will experience the yoke of love.

			g)	Pray for any yoke of bondage to turn into a yoke of love.

			Every ill marriage is turning into a blessed marriage.

			Prayer for Forgiveness

			We need to pray for God to forgive us.

			Drop down, ye heavens, from above, and let the skies pour down righteousness: let the earth open, and let them bring forth salvation, and let righteousness spring up together; I the Lord have created it.

			Isaiah 45:8

			And he shall take the two goats, and present them before the Lord at the door of the tabernacle of the congregation.

			Leviticus 16:7

			There are two goats here. One goat is going for our sins.

			And Aaron shall bring the goat upon which the Lord’s lot fell, and offer him for a sin offering.

			Leviticus 16:9

			The second one is the scapegoat.

			But the goat, on which the lot fell to be the scapegoat, shall be presented alive before the Lord, to make an atonement with him, and to let him go for a scapegoat into the wilderness.

			Leviticus 16:10

			And when he hath made an end of reconciling the holy place, and the tabernacle of the congregation, and the altar, he shall bring the live goat:

			And Aaron shall lay both his hands upon the head of the live goat, and confess over him all the iniquities of the children of Israel, and all their transgressions in all their sins, putting them upon the head of the goat, and shall send him away by the hand of a fit man into the wilderness:

			And the goat shall bear upon him all their iniquities unto a land not inhabited: and he shall let go the goat in the wilderness.

			Leviticus 16:20-22

			Sin must physically be taken away from us. We need to do something to physically take it away into the wilderness. You may need to physically dissociate yourself from bad friends or destroy certain devices that causes you to watch bad things.

			Pray for God to show you the “fit man” who is supposed to carry your scapegoat far away into the wilderness.

			Types of Sins

			i.	Sins - often has to do with our minds.

			ii.	Transgressions - these cross certain boundaries that we are not supposed to cross.

			iii.	Iniquities - these are orientations towards bad things, perversions, crookedness, twistedness etc. Some of us watched a movie and changed, and we have not been the same again.

			Prayer for Wounds to be Healed

			Wounds are a source of attraction for flies and demons.

			Whatever is a wound that is causing you to be crooked, transgressed and sinful, that wound is healing now in the name of Jesus.

			Remember we have a great capacity to be healed. Therefore, any demonic open wound that is causing demons to attack us is being healed now.

			We abort every demonic wound that is causing demons to be attracted to us.

			All open wounds from relationships, friendships and associations are receiving a healing band in the name of Jesus.

			We are uprooting all demonic deposits, demonic residues, demonic plantings and demonic fluids in the name of Jesus. Every planting is uprooted, every residue is uprooted and every emotional attachment is being removed now. We deny access to devils, familiar spirits and demons in the name of Jesus.

			The residues and deposits from interactions, associations and past relationships are being erased now. We renounce and discount them now by the blood of Jesus.

			Abnormalities in a Relationship

			 				Jealousy

				Criticism

				Revenge

				Self-centeredness

				Inability to forgive

				Inability to love

				Inability to trust

				Inability to flow

				Inability to enjoy

				Selfishness



			Prayer to Bind Snake Spirits

			Snakes swallow everything of their prey. When a snake is attacking your finances, it swallows everything; therefore, we bind any snake that is trying to swallow your finances, your job, your house and your car.

			We are coming out of any financial set back.

			Prayer Against the Spirit of a Viper

			Just a small injection of venom from a viper, and you can easily die.

			Pray against anything that is threatening your life.

			We cut off the head of every viper in our lives. I strike the head of any viper operating in my life.

			Prayer Against Black Mamba Snake Spirits

			This is the spirit of sudden death.

			Pray against every sudden attack in your ministry, marriage and finances.

			Pray against every surprise attack. I crush your head and I say be roasted by fire, I bind you and I strike you out from every sudden tragedy.

			Prayer Against Anacondas and Python Spirits

			This spirit squeezes you until you cannot breathe.

			We cut off every anaconda spirit that tries to squeeze life out of my marriage, out of my life, and out of my ministry.

			I bind and I strike the head of every anaconda and python. Whatever represents an anaconda and a python assigned to buffet me and to destroy me, I curse it and I terminate it.

			Prayer Against Cobra Spirits

			This spirit threatens you.

			Every threat to your ministry, to your marriage, to your children and to embarrass you, I bind it now. Anything that says,“You are finished” is boomeranging now - it will rather be finished. It shall fall into the pit it has dug. I renounce all threats, all actions against my life and ministry. I declare judgment on all such things.

			Prayer Against a “Very Small Snake”

			This represents the presence of evil that you cannot see easily. Any presence of evil in your home and life is ending now.

			Every undetected presence in my house is cursed. I uncover every evil and all conspiracies that are near me.

			Communion Prayer

			The Bread

CHAPTER 32




Prayer from Psalm 74; Prayer against Dragons; Prayer for Forgiveness and Mercy & Prayer for Fresh Anointing and Fruitfulness

			Saying, Touch not mine anointed, and do my prophets no harm.

			Luke 2:30

			Lord, break the head of every leviathan spirit in my church. Crash their heads.

			1. 	Prayer to bind the strong grip of a dragon

			A dragon is something that holds us and does not allow us to go. It is something that is very strong. Perhaps a man has taken hold of your life, perhaps a woman has taken hold of your life or perhaps drugs has has taken hold of life. Sexual perversion is like a dragon. It is very strong and it does not let you to go easily.

			Rise up and bind all strongholds in your life -

			 				Fornication

				Bad relationships

				Drugs

				Pornography

				Sexual perversions



			We bind the strongholds in our lives in the name of Jesus. Whatever is strong and holding me down, I demand immediate release; I break its jaws.

			2. 	Prayer to bind every demonic surprise dragon

			Whatever is a demonic surprise, I am coming against it right now. Surprise of sudden death, surprise of being laid off work and surprise of sickness and illness.

			Everything that represents a surprise, I break it in the name Jesus. I will never be caught by a demonic surprise; I bind every surprise attack. I bind it in the name of Jesus

			3. 	Prayer to bind every silent and dangerous dragon near you

			It may be that there is somebody near you who is very dangerous, but he or she has not done anything yet.

			Whatever represents silent danger, I command it now to be uncovered. I demand immediate termination of every silent danger around and near me - whether it is a friend, a relative or a business.

			4. 	Prayer to bind every breaking-up dragon

			The dragon does not chew, it breaks you into two or tears you apart. Have you heard somebody saying, “I am broken?”

			Whatever it is that wants to break your life, break your home into two, break your marriage into two or your ministry, bind it now. Bind anything that destroys lives, ministry, marriages and homes.

			5. 	Prayer to bind fear-generating dragons

			A dragon usually presents as something that has huge teeth, so they represent fear.

			I am overcoming any fear, the fear of the unknown. For God has not given us the spirit of fear. I demand every dragon of fear that is holding me back to be terminated now. I demand my freedom from the jaws of the dragon.

			6. 	Prayer to bind every sharp-sighted dragon that has targeted you

			Another attribute of the dragon is its sharp eyesight. It uses its sharp eyesight to target its prey.

			I bind every sharp-sighted dragon that has targeted my ministry, marriage, children, finances and my job. I bind you now. I demand your immediate destruction in the name of Jesus.

			7. 	Prayer to bind every attack of violence - such as armed robbery, terrorist attacks, gunfire, violence, threatenings and rape.

			I bind every attack of the dragon. I demand my soul and spirit out of any dragon.

			The Prophetic Blessings (1)

CHAPTER 33




Prayer for the Spirit of Counsel & Prayer for Your Apostolic Ministry




		 			They soon forgat his works; they waited not for his counsel.

			Psalm 106:13



		 			The meek will he guide in judgment: and the meek will he teach his way.

			Psalm 25:9

			Father, cause me to hear good counsel. Father, give me good counsel.

			May I not forget good counsel, especially from my pastors. Please make me into somebody who waits for Your counsel.

			I pray for the Spirit of counsel for my life.

			I pray for the Spirit of counsel for my ministry.

			Prayer for the Spirit of Counsel in 9 Areas of Our Lives

			1.	Pray for the Spirit of counsel concerning whom to marry.

			Now the birth of Jesus Christ was on this wise: When as his mother Mary was espoused to Joseph, before they came together, she was found with child of the Holy Ghost. Then Joseph her husband, being a just man, and not willing to make her a publick example, was minded to put her away privily. But while he thought on these things, behold, the angel of the Lord appeared unto him in a dream, saying, Joseph, thou son of David, FEAR NOT TO TAKE UNTO THEE MARY THY WIFE: for that which is conceived in her is of the Holy Ghost.

			Matthew 1:18-20

			The angel of the Lord gave counsel to Joseph not to be afraid to marry.

			Pray for the Spirit of counsel concerning whom to marry.

			2. 	Pray for the Spirit of counsel concerning your marriage and marriage issues.

			Wherefore she said unto Abraham, Cast out this bondwoman and her son: for the son of this bondwoman shall not be heir with my son, even with Isaac. And the thing was very grievous in Abraham’s sight because of his son. And God said unto Abraham, Let it not be grievous in thy sight because of the lad, and because of thy bondwoman; in all that Sarah hath said unto thee, hearken unto her voice; for in Isaac shall thy seed be called.

			Genesis 21:10-12

			Abraham was not happy in his home at all. Sometimes your past mistake can mock your miracle.

			If we ask God to advise us concerning our current marriage, He will surely advise us.

			God’s advice is the best advice for our lives. Therefore, pray for the Spirit of counsel for whatever type of marriage you are in.

			3. 	Pray for the Spirit of counsel concerning every crisis in your life.

			And the counsel of Ahithophel, which he counselled in those days, was as if a man had enquired at the oracle of God: so was all the counsel of Ahithophel both with David and with Absalom.

			2 Samuel 16:23

			Moreover Ahithophel said unto Absalom, Let me now choose out twelve thousand men, and I will arise and pursue after David this night:

			And Hushai said unto Absalom, The counsel that Ahithophel hath given is not good at this time. And Absalom and all the men of Israel said, The counsel of Hushai the Archite is better than the counsel of Ahithophel. For the Lord had appointed to defeat the good counsel of Ahithophel, to the intent that the Lord might bring evil upon Absalom.

			2 Samuel 17:1, 7, 14

			When you are doomed, you do not listen to good advice. Counsel [bad counsel] can destroy your life.

			There are people who when they speak, and if you can discern, you will notice that it is actually satan who has spoken.

			4. 	Pray for the Spirit of counsel for blessed advice.

			And David said to Abigail, Blessed be the Lord God of Israel, which sent thee this day to meet me: And blessed be thy advice, and blessed be thou, which hast kept me this day from coming to shed blood, and from avenging myself with mine own hand.

			1 Samuel 25:32-33

			Whenever you experience wrong treatment in life, it becomes a crossroad in your life. Satan can take advantage of us when we are hurt. When David was hurt, satan nearly took advantage of him.

			5. 	Pray for the Spirit of counsel concerning new things you are entering into.

			But he forsook the counsel of the old men, which they had given him, and consulted with the young men that were grown up with him, and which stood before him:

			And he said unto them, What counsel give ye that we may answer this people, who have spoken to me, saying, Make the yoke which thy father did put upon us lighter?

			And the young men that were grown up with him spake unto him, saying, Thus shalt thou speak unto this people that spake unto thee, saying, Thy father made our yoke heavy, but make thou it lighter unto us; thus shalt thou say unto them, My little finger shall be thicker than my father’s loins.

			And now whereas my father did lade you with a heavy yoke, I will add to your yoke: my father hath chastised you with whips, but I will chastise you with scorpions.

			So Jeroboam and all the people came to Rehoboam the third day, as the king had appointed, saying, Come to me again the third day.

			And the king answered the people roughly, and forsook the old men’s counsel that they gave him;

			And spake to them after the counsel of the young men, saying, My father made your yoke heavy, and I will add to your yoke: my father also chastised you with whips, but I will chastise you with scorpions.

			Wherefore the king hearkened not unto the people; for the cause was from the Lord, that he might perform his saying, which the Lord spake by Ahijah the Shilonite unto Jeroboam the son of Nebat.

			So when all Israel saw that the king hearkened not unto them, the people answered the king, saying, What portion have we in David? neither have we inheritance in the son of Jesse: to your tents, O Israel: now see to thine own house, David. So Israel departed unto their tents.

			1 Kings 12:8-16

			We need to pray for good friends because most of our counsel comes from our friends.

			Pray for good friends in your life.

			You usually have the same counsel as your friends therefore, you cannot ignore your friends. Friendship means you are in agreement.

			Counsel is very important. Absalom died because of bad counsel. Rehoboam was demoted and lost the opportunity to reign over Israel because of bad counsel.

			6. 	Pray for the Spirit of counsel concerning where to stay or where to be.

			On my way to the province of Macedonia, I advised you to stay in Ephesus. Well, I haven’t changed my mind. STAY RIGHT THERE on top of things so that the teaching stays on track. Apparently some people have been introducing fantasy stories and fanciful family trees that digress into silliness instead of pulling the people back into the center, deepening faith and obedience.

			1 Timothy 1:3-4 (MSG)

			7. 	Pray for the Spirit of counsel concerning whatever you are fighting.

			Every purpose is established by counsel: and WITH GOOD ADVICE MAKE WAR.

			Proverbs 20:18

			Pray for counsel to war a good warfare. Whatever you are fighting, receive wisdom to have the upper hand.

			8. 	Pray for the Spirit of counsel on how to finish what you have started.

			HERE IS MY ADVICE: IT WOULD BE GOOD FOR YOU TO FINISH WHAT YOU STARTED A YEAR AGO. Last year you were the first who wanted to give, and you were the first to begin doing it.

			Now you should finish what you started. Let the eagerness you showed in the beginning be matched now by your giving. Give in proportion to what you have.

			2 Corinthians 8:10-11 (NLT)

			What good work have you started doing? Pray that you will finish it.

			9. 	Pray for the Spirit of counsel concerning your life and death, such that even death cannot have victory on your life.

			In those days was Hezekiah sick unto death. And the prophet Isaiah the son of Amoz came to him, and said unto him, Thus saith the Lord, Set thine house in order; for thou shalt die, and not live.

			And Isaiah said, Take a lump of figs. And they took and laid it on the boil, and he recovered.

			2 Kings 20:1, 7

			It is good counsel to set your house in order. Perhaps you have to write a Will.

			 Prayer for Your Apostolic Ministry

			An apostle is someone who is sent.

			1. 	Pray for your apostolic ministry of “abasing yourself” (humility).

			Have I committed an offence in abasing myself that ye might be exalted, because I have preached to you the gospel of God freely?

			2 Corinthians 11:7

			2. 	Pray for your apostolic “non-chargeableness”.

			And when I was present with you, and wanted, I WAS CHARGEABLE TO NO MAN: for that which was lacking to me the brethren which came from Macedonia supplied: and in all things I have kept myself from being burdensome unto you, and so will I keep myself.

			2 Corinthians 11:9

			3. 	Pray for your apostolic region.

			As the truth of Christ is in me, no man shall stop me of this boasting in the REGIONS OF ACHAIA.

			2 Corinthians 11:10

			4. 	Pray for your apostolic love.

			Wherefore? because I love you not? God knoweth.

			2 Corinthians 11:11

			5. 	Pray against the works of the devil.

			Pray against whatever seems to:

			i. Accuse you

			ii. Tempt you

			iii. Resist you

			iv. Oppose you

			v. Shame you

			I declare judgement against all evils!

			Bind every demonic influence that is guiding and misleading you!

			Dismantle every plot for your premature death right now!

			 Every plan for my premature departure from this world is dismantled now!

			Every satanic disease for my mind and body and organs is aborted in the name of Jesus!

			I abort every mysterious disease in the name of Jesus!

			Bind and kill any serpent that has infiltrated your space and your life!

			I cut off the head of every invasion and penetration of demons and serpents in my life right now!

			Every trap is destroyed by fire!

			Every pit that has been dug for you to fall into it, we declare that the person who dug it shall fall into it in the name of Jesus!

			Whoso diggeth a pit shall fall therein: and he that rolleth a stone, it will return upon him.

			Proverbs 26:27

			Communion Prayer

			The Bread

			You are now interacting with the Holy Body of Christ

			Father, we present this bread as the body of Christ

			Let power come into our lives, giving us supernatural capability we never had before

			We receive the power in the blood and body of Jesus Christ

			We commune right now with the body of Christ. (Eat It)

			The Blood

			Your mistake will no longer be a reason for not doing well

			This blood washes away every mistake and every deviation that has occurred

			Every distraction, every wrong turn you’ve taken, and time wasting that has happened in your life, is neutralized by the blood

			The blood of Jesus is your portion right now. (Drink It)

			The Prophetic Blessings

			“And the Lord spoke to Moses saying, On this wise, you shall bless the children of Israel” (Numbers 6:23)

			May the Lord help you to do what you could not do before

			May you be supernatural

			May you have a supernatural component to your life and existence

			You will not perish like a goat

			You will not end prematurely

			The Lord bless you, and the Lord keep you

			The Lord make His face smile at you

			Receive a smile from God

			A smile of approval and recognition

			God recognizes you, knows you, and acknowledges your presence

			The Lord lift up His countenance

			May the countenance that was down looking be lifted up

			May the anger change to favour

			The Lord lift up His countenance over you and give you peace

			The Lord intercept your enemies

			The Lord encounter and meet with those that hate you

			The Lord send His angel to fight for you and defend you

			May the Lord contend against those that contend against you

			May the Lord send an agent and emissary to meet and intercept your enemy

			May the Lord send a bird to meet your enemy in the way

			May the Lord lift His countenance towards you and give you peace

			May the Lord remember you today

			May the Lord fight for you

			May the Lord help you

			You will not perish!

			Amen.

CHAPTER 35




More Prayers from Psalm 106 & Prayer Against Devils that are Likely to Follow You




		 			Who can utter the mighty acts of the Lord? who can shew forth all his praise?

			Psalm 106:2

		 			1.	Pray and acknowledge the mighty acts of God.

			Who can utter the mighty acts of the Lord? who can shew forth all his praise?

			Psalm 106:2

			We do not thank God well if we do not utter His mighty acts.

			Give Him thanks and mention the mighty acts of God.

			2.	Pray that you will do righteousness at all times.

			Pray that your life will be one that does righteousness at all times.

			Blessed are they that keep judgment, and he that doeth righteousness at all times.

			Psalm 106:3

			Many people do righteousness when they are with their pastor. We all do righteousness when we are in church, but pray to do that at all times.

			Father, I need Your help and your grace to do righteousness at all times.

			3.	Pray for God to remember you with favour.

			Favour means being chosen WITHOUT any good reason.

			Remember me, O Lord, with the favour that thou bearest unto thy people: O visit me with thy salvation;

			Psalm 106:4

			God is the one who gives us favour. Leah was favoured. Mary was favoured and she was chosen to be Jesus’ mother. Life is such that at a point, it is favour that moves us to another level.

			The blessing of favour is that you cannot understand why you; it is to be chosen WITHOUT any apparently sensible reason.

			Pray and ask for favour. Pray that whatever favour you seem to have lost will be restored.

			And Joseph was brought down to Egypt; and Potiphar, an officer of Pharaoh, captain of the guard, an Egyptian, bought him of the hands of the Ishmeelites, which had brought him down thither.

			And the Lord was with Joseph, and he was a prosperous man; and he was in the house of his master the Egyptian.

			And his master saw that the Lord was with him, and that the Lord made all that he did to prosper in his hand.

			And Joseph found grace in his sight, and he served him: and he made him overseer over his house, and all that he had he put into his hand.

			And it came to pass from the time that he had made him overseer in his house, and over all that he had, that the Lord blessed the Egyptian’s house for Joseph’s sake; and the blessing of the Lord was upon all that he had in the house, and in the field.

			And he left all that he had in Joseph’s hand; and he knew not ought he had, save the bread which he did eat. And Joseph was a goodly person, and well favoured.

			Genesis 39:1-6

			In life, there reaches a place where you can only move forward if you are chosen.

			4.	Pray to see the good of the chosen of God.

			THAT I MAY SEE THE GOOD OF THY CHOSEN, that I may rejoice in the gladness of thy nation, that I may glory with thine inheritance.

			Psalm 106:5

			Father, please let me see the good of thy chosen.

			A good thing that God gives to His chosen is humility.

			Anything that gives us humility is a great gift from God.

			5.	Ask God for the forgiveness of sin.

			We have sinned with our fathers, we have committed iniquity, we have done wickedly.

			Psalm 106:6

			6.	Pray for mercy for the sins of your fathers and mothers.

			Our fathers understood not thy wonders in Egypt; they remembered not the multitude of thy mercies; but provoked him at the sea, even at the Red sea.

			Psalm 106:7

			What are the Sins of our Fathers?

			 				They understood not

				They remembered not

				They provoked Him (God)



			Therefore:

			 				Pray for the Spirit of understanding

				Pray that you will remember

				Pray that you will not provoke God



			Ten Things that Provoke God

			But if the Lord make a new thing, and the earth open her mouth, and swallow them up, with all that appertain unto them, and they go down quick into the pit; then ye shall understand that these men have provoked the Lord.

			Numbers 16:30

			These men (in Numbers 16:27-33, 35) met an unusual death - the way they died is a sign that they provoked God.

			1. Pray that you will not provoke God by rising up against God’s servants.

			Now Korah, the son of Izhar, the son of Kohath, the son of Levi, and Dathan and Abiram, the sons of Eliab, and On, the son of Peleth, sons of Reuben, took men: And they rose up before Moses, with certain of the children of Israel, two hundred and fifty princes of the assembly, famous in the congregation, men of renown: And they gathered themselves together against Moses and against Aaron, and said unto them, Ye take too much upon you, seeing all the congregation are holy, every one of them, and the Lord is among them: wherefore then lift ye up yourselves above the congregation of the Lord?

			Numbers 16:1-3

			2. Pray that you will not provoke God by organizing people against God’s servants.

			And they gathered themselves together against Moses and against Aaron, and said unto them, Ye take too much upon you, seeing all the congregation are holy, every one of them, and the Lord is among them: wherefore then lift ye up yourselves above the congregation of the Lord?

			Numbers 16:3

			3. 	Pray that you will not provoke God by accusing the man of God of exalting himself.

			Do not resent the man of God because of the greatness God has given him.

			Pray that dislike and resentment will not develop in you against God’s servants.

			And they gathered themselves together against Moses and against Aaron, and said unto them, Ye take too much upon you, seeing all the congregation are holy, every one of them, and the Lord is among them: wherefore then lift ye up yourselves above the congregation of the Lord?

			Numbers 16:3

			Saying things like: “He is not always right, why do you have to listen to him all the time? Is he the only man of God?” - are all sayings against the man of God and are provocations to God.

			4. 	Pray that every dislike in you for a man of God will disappear so that you will not provoke God.

			Pray that resentment will not develop in you against God’s institutions.

			5. Pray that you will not provoke God by not valuing the opportunity to be called and separated.

			6. 	Pray that will not provoke God by not valuing the privilege to be chosen.

			Pray that will not provoke God by not valuing the opportunity to be nearby.

			7. 	Pray that will not provoke God by not valuing the opportunity to stand and minister. Your ministry can be preaching, serving, singing, arranging chairs, playing drums, etc.

			Seemeth it but a small thing unto you, that the God of Israel hath separated you from the congregation of Israel, to bring you near to himself to do the service of the tabernacle of the Lord, and to stand before the congregation to minister unto them?

			Numbers 16:9

			These three things (points 5, 6 and 7) are all different spiritual events.

			Pray that you will understand these 3 spiritual events.

			Do you value the spiritual event to be called and separated?

			Do you value the opportunity to be nearby?

			Do you value spiritual events to stand to minister?

			8. 	Pray that you will not provoke God by murmuring against God’s servants.

			Pray that you will not murmur.

			What is Murmuring?

			Murmuring is grumbling and grumbling is speaking secretly and negatively. It is speaking in a negative, low, half-suppressed voice.

			For which cause both thou and all thy company are gathered together against the Lord: and what is Aaron, THAT YE MURMUR AGAINST HIM?

			Numbers 16:11

			Curse not the king, no not in thy thought; and curse not the rich in thy bedchamber: for a bird of the air shall carry the voice, and that which hath wings shall tell the matter.

			Ecclesiastes 10:20

			9. 	Pray that will not provoke God by refusing to come when you are called.

			And Moses sent to call Dathan and Abiram, the sons of Eliab: which said, We will not come up:

			Numbers 16:12

			10. 	Pray that will not provoke God by accusing God’s servants of big things. Moses was accused of murder.

			Pray that will not provoke God by accusing God’s servants of stealing.

			Is it a small thing that thou hast brought us up out of a land that floweth with milk and honey, to kill us in the wilderness, except thou make thyself altogether a prince over us?

			Numbers 16:13

			And Moses was very wroth, and said unto the Lord, Respect not thou their offering: I have not taken one ass from them, neither have I hurt one of them.

			Numbers 16:15

			The unusual death of these men is a sign that they provoked God.

			But if the Lord make a new thing, and the earth open her mouth, and swallow them up, with all that appertain unto them, and they go down quick into the pit; then ye shall understand that these men have provoked the Lord.

			Numbers 16:30

			7.	Pray against whatever represents the Red Sea in your life.

			The Red Sea represents the greatest and biggest problem in your life.

			Nevertheless he saved them for his name’s sake, that he might make his mighty power to be known. He rebuked the Red sea also, and it was dried up: so he led them through the depths, as through the wilderness.

			Psalm 106:8-9

			Write Down Your 3 Red Sea Problems

			Red Sea Problem 1:

			Red Sea Problem 2:

			Red Sea Problem 3:

			By faith, you will overcome and pass through your Red Sea.

			Faith is partnering with the invisible God to accomplish something impossible.1

			1See Chapter 26 of my book, Faith Secrets; (2019) Parchment House, Accra,

			By faith they passed through the Red sea as by dry land: which the Egyptians assaying to do were drowned.

			Hebrews 11:29

			Seven Red Seas that You May Pass Through

			1.	A Red Sea of complicated problems

			2.	A Red Sea of marital problems

			3.	A Red Sea of your relationships

			4.	A Red Sea of your ministry

			5.	A Red Sea of sickness

			6.	A Red Sea of financial problems

			7.	A Red Sea of sin

			8. 	Pray for God to save you from the hand of him that hates you.

			And he saved them from the hand of him that hated them, and redeemed them from the hand of the enemy.

			Psalm 106:10

			Ask God to save you from the hand of your enemy. Pray for God to deliver you from their traps.

			Prayer against Seven Devils that are Likely to Follow You

			These are seven devils that are most likely to be following you:

			i) Sickness

			ii) Quarreling

			iii) Divorce

			iv) Demon of early death

			v) Poverty

			vi) Breaking up of relationships

			vii) Breaking up a relationship with your pastor

			Demons of “Near Success Syndrome”

			1.	Pray against every snare

			Examples of the snare:

			i. 	Bad choices

			ii. 	Bad associations

			I disconnect myself now from every form of bad choice and bad association.

			Thou art snared with the words of thy mouth, thou art taken with the words of thy mouth.

			Proverbs 6:2

			Every word, every thought, every speech that opens you up to evil, every discussion that has connected you to the devil, RENOUNCE IT RIGHT NOW; REJECT IT RIGHT NOW; CANCEL THOSE WORDS RIGHT NOW.

			I release myself from every snare, I release myself from every trap in the name of Jesus!

			2.	Pray against satanic covenants

			3. 	Pray against every access point that opens you up to demons

			4. 	Pray and activate a heavy perimeter around you

			5.	Pray and lift up a heavy perimeter around your life

			6.	Pray to cast down imaginations

			Every stronghold against your future, your children, your ministry, reject it now.

			Every “half-cow”, and every half animal that is after my life, I rebuke in the name of Jesus!

			Communion Prayer

			The Bread

			Father we declare this to be the body of Christ

			We thank You for this interaction with the body

			Whatever is in the body of Christ comes into us

			We receive and partake of the body of Christ

			We thank You for the blessing of this body

			We thank You for healing

			We thank You that the stripes of this body are ministering healing

			The stripes and the body of Christ are ministering healing

			Receive healing from every impossible disease

			And may your life be extended for an extra five years by the power and the communion of the body of Jesus Christ. (Eat It)

			The Blood

			Every bad choice you ever made,

			Every mistake, every snare you ever got into,

			May you escape through the Red Sea

			This blood represents the Red Sea of escape

			The Egyptians you see today, the enemies you see today

			You will see them no more

			The crossing of the Red Sea

			The blood of Jesus (Drink It)

			The Prophetic Blessings

			I bless you to serve God beautifully

			Free of demon affectations

			Free of evil powers that seek to harass you and torment you

			I bless you to be a blessing

			As Abraham was a blessing, so shall you also be a blessing to many people

			From today people won’t call you blessed; they will say you are a blessing

			May the blessing of the Lord overflow so much, so throughout that your whole life, you are being a blessing to others

			Father I thank You that this child shall be called a blessing

			I bless all my sons and daughters with the blessing of a father

			May the blessing of the Lord be upon you until people call you a blessing, in the name of Jesus.

			Amen.





CHAPTER 36




Prayer against Things to Come & Prayer from Psalm 103




		 			And it came to pass, when Jesus had finished all these sayings, he said unto his disciples, Ye know that after two days is the feast of the passover, and the Son of man is betrayed to be crucified.

			Matthew 26:1-2

		 			When Jesus said the Passover was coming, for Him it meant, treachery and betrayal were coming. So, for you as an individual, what “is coming” for you?

			What to Pray and Ask God For

			1.	Pray and ask God to let you know what is coming for you.

			2.	Pray and ask God to give you wisdom to know what is coming.

			3.	Pray and ask God to prepare you for what is coming.

			The disciple is not above his master: but every one that is perfect shall be as his master.

			Luke 6:40

			Betrayal has to do with trust, trusting somebody who turns to betray you. Betrayal can destroy you. If Jesus, our master was betrayed then we are not excluded from betrayal. But can we recover when we are betrayed?

			And he said, Go into the city to such a man, and say unto him, The Master saith, My time is at hand; I will keep the passover at thy house with my disciples. And the disciples did as Jesus had appointed them; and they made ready the passover. Now when the even was come, he sat down with the twelve. And as they did eat, he said, Verily I say unto you, that one of you shall betray me. And they were exceeding sorrowful, and began every one of them to say unto him, Lord, is it I? And he answered and said, He that dippeth his hand with me in the dish, the same shall betray me. The Son of man goeth as it is written of him: but woe unto that man by whom the Son of man is betrayed! it had been good for that man if he had not been born.

			Matthew 26: 18-24

			Twenty Prayers against Things to Come

			1. 	Pray against every form of betrayal at all levels.

			And it came to pass, when Jesus had finished all these sayings, he said unto his disciples, ye know that after two days is the feast of the passover, and the Son of man is betrayed to be crucified.

			Matthew 26:1-2

			Betrayal can be in three levels:

			i. Betrayal from somebody above you.

			ii. Betray at your level.

			iii. Betrayal from somebody below you.

			2.	Pray against destruction from betrayal from those above you.

			3.	Pray against destruction from betrayal from those at your level.

			4.	Pray against destruction from betrayal from those below you.

			5.	Pray that you will never be a betrayer.

			6..	Pray that you will recover when you are betrayed. Betrayal leads to crucifixion and the destruction of your life. It changes your life. There can be betrayal in the church, betrayal in your marriage, betrayal at your work place.

				Everybody who has been betrayed has a potential to be destroyed. Therefore, pray against that destruction.

			7.	Pray that your personality will not be changed because of betrayal.

			8.	Pray that your temperament will not change.

			9.	Pray that you will fall into the arms of Jesus deeper than ever before. The answer to betrayal and destruction of life is Jesus!

			The thief cometh not, but for to steal, and to kill, and to destroy: I am come that they might have life, and that they might have it more abundantly. I am the good shepherd: the good shepherd giveth his life for the sheep.

			John 10:10-11

			10. 	Pray against conspiracies.

			Then assembled together the chief priests, and the scribes, and the elders of the people, unto the palace of the high priest, who was called Caiaphas, And consulted that they might take Jesus by subtilty, and kill him.

			Matthew 26:3-4

			11.	Pray that every conspiracy against your life will be rejected.

			Let them be ashamed and confounded that seek after my soul: let them be turned backward, and put to confusion, that desire my hurt. Let them be turned back for a reward of their shame that say, Aha, aha.

			Psalm 70:2-3

			12.	Pray that those who have consulted and planned against you will be shamed and confounded.

			Father, let them be shamed. Let their plans turn back against them.

			Reverse every decision against your life.

			The kings of the earth set themselves, and the rulers take counsel together, against the Lord, and against his anointed, saying, let us break their bands asunder, and cast away their cords from us. He that sitteth in the heavens shall laugh: the Lord shall have them in derision. Then shall he speak unto them in his wrath, and vex them in his sore displeasure.

			Psalm 2:2-5

			We are the anointed today and people are having meetings against us.

			Father, vex every person who has a conspiracy against me. Scatter them! Vex them Lord! We reverse every negative decision and judgement.
