			13.	Pray against tricks.

			To be tricked is to be fooled. It is a deceitful action. Pray that you will not be taken in by the tricks of the devil.

			But I fear, lest by any means, as the serpent beguiled Eve through his subtilty, so your minds should be corrupted from the simplicity that is in Christ.

			2 Corinthians 11:3

			14.	Pray against any attempt to fool you. It falls to the ground now. Every attempt to fool you falls to the ground now. Pray that you will not be fooled by mortgages.

			15.	Pray against anything, including every sickness that is not even diagnosed, that will try to kill you.

			And consulted that they might take Jesus by subtilty, and kill him.

			Matthew 26:4

			16.	Pray against every form of accident.

			17.	Pray against every diagnosis of cancer. Bind the demon of death.

			18.	Pray against sudden death.

			We cancel every up-and-coming accident.

			They also that seek after my life lay snares for me: and they that seek my hurt speak mischievous things, and imagine deceits all the day long.

			Psalm 38:12

			19.	Pray against anything that seeks your life. Pray against every attack on your life.

			20. Pray to expect the Holy Spirit or an agent of the Holy Spirit.

			There came unto him a woman having an alabaster box of very precious ointment, and poured it on his head, as he sat at meat.

			Matthew 26:7

			This woman was an agent of the Holy Spirit. Since the Holy Spirit is a helper, anybody who helps you is an agent of the Holy Spirit.

			Pray for the Holy Spirit to come into your life.

			Pray also that an agent of the Holy Spirit will be fully manifested in your life.

			Seven Things the Holy Spirit, or an Agent of the Holy Spirit, Brings

			1.	The Holy Spirit, or an agent of the Holy Spirit, brings honour.

			2.	The Holy Spirit, or an agent of the Holy Spirit, brings comfort.

			3.	The Holy Spirit or an agent of the Holy Spirit brings something that makes you precious.

			4.	The Holy Spirit, or an agent of the Holy Spirit, brings a rebuke for those who do not honour you.

			5.	The Holy Spirit, or the agent of the Holy Spirit, brings a memorial or something to remember.

			6.	The Holy Spirit, or the agent of the Holy Spirit, brings money.

			7.	The Holy Spirit, or the agent of the Holy Spirit, brings a message from God. Jesus told the people to leave the woman alone, for she has done what she could.

			The agent of the Holy Spirit (the woman in Matthew 26:7) rebuked familiarity.

			Pray that all the above seven things will come into your life as you are praying for the Holy Spirit and the agent of the Holy Spirit.

			I pray for the seven blessings above.

			Receive these 7 things or blessings from the agent of the Holy Spirit.

			Pray against familiarity.

			You get used to what makes you great. The disciples did not honour Jesus.

			Pray! No more familiarity.

			4. 	Pray to expect a house, food and a man.

			And he said, Go into the city to such a man, and say unto him, The Master saith, My time is at hand; I will keep the passover at thy house with my disciples.

			Matthew 26:18

			Father, send the man who is supposed to help me to have a house. I pray for houses, Lord.

			Lift up your hand and thank God for a house, food and a man. Thank God!

			5. Pray that you will be obedient in small things.

			And the disciples did as Jesus had appointed them; and THEY MADE READY THE PASSOVER.

			Matthew 26:19

			Write down 5 things that the Holy Spirit has told you to do.

			Pray that the Holy Spirit brings these things to your remembrance.

			What small instruction has been given to you? What has God told you that you are not doing? Where has God sent you that you are not going?

			6. 	Pray about your company of twelve (who you sit with).

			Now when the even was come, HE SAT DOWN WITH THE TWELVE.

			Matthew 26:20

			There are 12 important people in your life. Some people should be part of your life! Others do not need to be in your life. Who are you sitting down with?

			Pray about your company of twelve.

			Lord, who do You want me to be with?

			7.	Pray that you will never be a betrayer.

			And as they did eat, he said, Verily I say unto you, that ONE OF YOU SHALL BETRAY ME.

			Matthew 26:21

			Pray that you will never be a betrayer. Pray that you will not be a trusted person who breaks that trust.

			8.	Pray against every form of acting and pretence in your life.

			Then Judas, which betrayed him, answered and said, MASTER, IS IT I? He said unto him, Thou hast said.

			Matthew 26:25

			Judas asked “Is it I?” when he knew Jesus was talking about him.

			Pray that you will stop acting and being unreal.

			9. 	Pray that God will take away every form of weakness, sickness and death that comes from not discerning the Lord’s body.

			For he that eateth and drinketh unworthily, eateth and drinketh damnation to himself, not discerning the Lord’s body. For this cause many are weak and sickly among you, and many sleep.

			1 Corinthians 11:29-30

			10.	Pray that you will not touch or fight against any part of the body of Christ.

			And he said, Who art thou, Lord? And the Lord said, I am Jesus whom thou persecutest: it is hard for thee to kick against the pricks.

			Acts 9:5

			Father, help me to recognise and discern Your body all the time. Help me not to fight Your body. Help me not to attack Your body. Help me not to kick against Your body.

			If you touch the Lord’s anointed, you are touching the head of the body. Expect to be weak, to be sick and even die.

			11. Pray that you will overcome offences in your life.

			Then saith Jesus unto them, All ye shall be offended because of me this night: for it is written, I will smite the shepherd, and the sheep of the flock shall be scattered abroad.

			Matthew 26:31

			An “offence” is to cause somebody to trip, it is to put something in the way of someone to make them go down.

			12. Pray against every form of pride in you.

			Peter answered and said unto him, Though all men shall be offended because of thee, yet will I never be offended.

			Matthew 26:33

			13. Pray for grace not to follow a bad example.

			Pray against every bad example that you must not follow. Pray that you will not follow a bad example, even if it is coming from your leader. Follow your leader as he or she follows Christ.

			Peter said unto him, Though I should die with thee, yet will I not deny thee. Likewise also said all the disciples.

			Matthew 26:35

			Simon Peter saith unto them, I go a fishing. They say unto him, We also go with thee. They went forth, and entered into a ship immediately; and that night they caught nothing.

			John 21:3

			14. Pray about your Gethsemane ministry.

			Gethsemane means a place of prayer.

			Then cometh Jesus with them unto a place called Gethsemane, and saith unto the disciples, Sit ye here, while I go and pray yonder.

			Matthew 26:36

			Father. let it be born! A place where we go and wait on you. Please teach me how to go somewhere for three or more days to wait on you. Teach me Lord!

			Drawing closer to God depends on us and not God.

			Draw nigh to God, and he will draw nigh to you. Cleanse your hands, ye sinners; and purify your hearts, ye double minded.

			James 4:8

			Strength is coming to you as you wait on God.

			15. Pray about things that Jesus implied but He didn’t say (the unspoken instructions and the instructions by example).

			Then cometh Jesus with them unto a place called Gethsemane, and saith unto the disciples, Sit ye here, while I go and pray yonder.

			Matthew 26:36

			The unspoken instruction here is “Sit ye here, while I go and pray yonder”. The unspoken part is normally what a person is doing. Jesus intended for the disciples to also pray as He was going to pray.

			Grant me divine wisdom to do what You have not said, but implied. Give me the divine wisdom to do the unspoken instructions. Help me Lord!

			Write down 3 things that you believe God is showing you to do.

			16. Pray that you will be part of the innermost group.

			And he took with him Peter and the two sons of Zebedee, and began to be sorrowful and very heavy.

			Matthew 26:37

			Pray that you will be in the right group that God has ordained for you to be part of. Pray that you will be part of the innermost group. I receive all my groups that I am supposed to be part of.

			17. Pray that you will have prayer targets.

			And he cometh unto the disciples, and findeth them asleep, and saith unto Peter, What, could ye not watch with me one hour?

			Matthew 26:40

			Seven Prayer Targets

			1. Prayer target of one hour.

			2. Prayer target of three hours.

			3. Prayer target of seven hours.

			4. Prayer target of twelve hours.

			5. Prayer target of all night - a personal all-night prayer.

			6. Prayer target of an all-day prayer.

			7. Prayer target of one minute prayer.

			18.	Pray concerning whatever makes you sorrowful and heavy in your life.

			And he took with him Peter and the two sons of Zebedee, and began to be sorrowful and very heavy.

			Matthew 26:37

			Prayer from Psalm 103

			1. 	Pray for forgiveness of the sins and iniquities that lead to sicknesses in your life.

			Bless the Lord, O my soul: and all that is within me, bless his holy name. Bless the Lord, O my soul, and forget not all his benefits: Who forgiveth all thine iniquities; who healeth all thy diseases.

			Psalm 103:1-3

			2. 	Pray concerning what wants to destroy your life.

			Who redeemeth thy life from destruction; who crowneth thee with lovingkindness and tender mercies

			Psalm 103:4

			Any agent of destruction is blocked today. Ask for your crowns of loving kindness and tender mercies.

			3. 	Pray that your youth is renewed.

			Who satisfieth thy mouth with good things; so that thy youth is renewed like the eagle’s.

			Psalm 103:5

			Old things are replaced with new things. Replacement is avoided when your youth is renewed.

			4. 	Pray against oppression in your life.

			The Lord executeth righteousness and judgment for all that are oppressed.

			Psalm 103:6

			5. 	Pray that God manages His anger against us.

			The Lord is merciful and gracious, slow to anger, and plenteous in mercy. He will not always chide: neither will he keep his anger for ever.

			Psalm 103:8-9

			6. 	Pray that God’s anger will be slow in coming and when it comes, it shouldn’t last forever.

			He hath not dealt with us after our sins; nor rewarded us according to our iniquities. For as the heaven is high above the earth, so great is his mercy toward them that fear him. As far as the east is from the west, so far hath he removed our transgressions from us.

			Psalm 103:10-12

			7. 	Pray that as God shows us His mercy, we will also show others mercy.

			Pray for the heart of the Father and the nature of God.

			8. 	Pray concerning the end of your life.

			Pray for the day when you’re gone. How will you go? May God guide us with wisdom with the realities that we are facing.

			Deliver us from premature death.

			As for man, his days are as grass: as a flower of the field, so he flourisheth. For the wind passeth over it, and it is gone; and the place thereof shall know it no more.

			Psalm 103:15-16

			9.	Pray that you fear God.

			But the mercy of the Lord is from everlasting to everlasting upon THEM THAT FEAR HIM, and his righteousness unto children’s children; To such as keep his covenant, and to those that remember his commandments to do them.

			Psalm 103:17-18

			Your covenant is what you told God that you would do for Him. God’s commandments are His instructions to us.

			10. Pray to keep God’s covenants and commandments.

			But the mercy of the Lord is from everlasting to everlasting upon them that fear him, and his righteousness unto children’s children; TO SUCH AS KEEP HIS COVENANT, and to those that remember his commandments to do them.

			Psalm 103:17-18

			11. 	Pray that you finish your ministry and receive the mercy of the Lord.

			But the mercy of the Lord is from everlasting to everlasting upon them that fear him, and his righteousness unto children’s children; To such as keep his covenant, and to those that remember his commandments to do them.

			Psalm 103:17-18

			12.	Pray that we will be ministers who make God have pleasure. God’s ministers are people that do His pleasure.

			Bless ye the Lord, all ye his hosts; ye ministers of his, that DO HIS PLEASURE.

			Psalm 103:21

			13. Pray about your prophetic calling.

			Pray for the prophetic to increase in your life. Pray about every prophetic dream that keeps coming to you. Pray for the quality of your prophetic dreams to improve. Pray for high quality dreams.

			And they dreamed a dream both of them, each man his dream in one night, each man according to the interpretation of his dream, the butler and the baker of the king of Egypt, which were bound in the prison. And Joseph came in unto them in the morning, and looked upon them, and, behold, they were sad. And he asked Pharaoh’s officers that were with him in the ward of his lord’s house, saying, Wherefore look ye so sadly to day? And they said unto him, We have dreamed a dream, and there is no interpreter of it. And Joseph said unto them, Do not interpretations belong to God? tell me them, I pray you. And the chief butler told his dream to Joseph, and said to him, In my dream, behold, a vine was before me; and in the vine were three branches: and it was as though it budded, and her blossoms shot forth; and the clusters thereof brought forth ripe grapes: and Pharaoh’s cup was in my hand: and I took the grapes, and pressed them into Pharaoh’s cup, and I gave the cup into Pharaoh’s hand. And Joseph said unto him, This is the interpretation of it: The three branches are three days:

			Genesis 40:5-12

			Pray and ask for interpretation of dreams. Pray that you will see prophetic visions.

			14. Pray that you will receive prophetic trances.

			He hath said, which heard the words of God, which saw the vision of the Almighty, falling into a trance, but having his eyes open.

			Numbers 24:4

			And he became very hungry, and would have eaten: but while they made ready, he fell into a trance.

			Acts 10:10

			15. Pray for the prophetic burden of the Lord.

			And as for the prophet, and the priest, and the people, that shall say, The burden of the Lord, I will even punish that man and his house.

			And the burden of the Lord shall ye mention no more: for every man’s word shall be his burden; for ye have perverted the words of the living God, of the Lord of hosts our God.

			But since ye say, The burden of the Lord; therefore, thus saith the Lord; Because ye say this word, The burden of the Lord, and I have sent unto you, saying, Ye shall not say, The burden of the Lord;

			Jeremiah 23:34, 36, 38

			16. 	Pray for your prophetic speaking.

			But he that prophesieth speaketh unto men to edification, and exhortation, and comfort.

			1 Corinthians 14:3

			May your speech be laced with prophecies.

			Communion Prayer

			The Bread

			Father we come before You as we take this bread, forgive our sins

			As we receive this communion, we thank You that this is the body of Jesus Christ

			Let this body, broken for us do something supernatural in our lives

			Heal us! Bring us out of our sicknesses, pain, cancers, rashes, eye problems, depression, and sorrows

			Save us by the broken body of Jesus

			Let damnation fall upon him that touches Your body

			Those that tamper with it and irreverently strike at it, strike them Lord according to Your Word, in the name of Jesus. (Eat It)

			The Blood

			As many are weak, sick, and die

			May we be strengthened, be well, and live

			Because we come before this drink as the blood of Jesus, let the power of this blood enter every part of us

			Heal us and show Yourself strong

			Thank You for the blood that is answering for our iniquities, transgressions, and sins

			Cleanse us in Jesus’ Name. (Drink It)

			The Prophetic Blessing

			May the Lord who called you bless you

			May you experience His healing in your life

			May you experience His strength in your life

			May all your prayers be answered

			May Jehovah intercede for you.

			At the judgement seat may you be one of those who do the pleasure of God

			May you not die prematurely, but may you live to declare the works of the Lord and to finish giving pleasure to the Father who called us to Himself

			The Lord help you, remember you, and bless you

			He who heals all your diseases and has forgiven your iniquities, blesses you today.

			Amen.





CHAPTER 37




Prayer Concerning Your Works and Labours & Prayer against Demonic Activities




		 			I know thy works, and thy labour, and thy patience, and how thou canst not bear them which are evil: and thou hast tried them which say they are apostles, and are not, and hast found them liars.

			Revelation 2:2

		 			If God puts a flashlight on your works, what will He see? You may have some good accomplishments, but under scrutiny, your works may fall short.

			Prayer Concerning Your Works

			1. 	Pray about your works for God.

			Lord, grant mercies to the works I have been involved in and completion to all works I have started.

			Seven Characteristics of Good Works

			1. Is it costly?

			Now if any man build upon this foundation gold, silver, precious stones, wood, hay, stubble; Every man’s work shall be made manifest: for the day shall declare it, because it shall be revealed by fire; and the fire shall try every man’s work of what sort it is.

			1 Corinthians 3:12-13

			Good works of gold will cost you something.

			Pray about the price you paid for your works.

			Lord, I am ready to start paying the price to have golden works.

			2. Does it cost money?

			What have your works cost you financially? Money consciousness has killed God’s work.

			3. Is it international?

			Gold can be used everywhere because it has value everywhere. Wood and grass do not have value everywhere.

			Pray for international works.

			4. Is it long lasting?

			The voice said, Cry. And he said, What shall I cry? All flesh is grass, and all the goodliness thereof is as the flower of the field: The grass withereth, the flower fadeth: because the spirit of the Lord bloweth upon it: surely the people is grass.

			Isaiah 40:6-7

			Gold lasts for generations but hay only lasts for a few weeks.

			Pray that your church and ministry will remain for years.

			5. Does it survive fire?

			Fires are troubles. Does your church survive a pandemic? Does your church survive “orangus”1?

			Pray that your ministry will survive fires.

			6. Is it truly valuable?

			One small ring can be worth more than a truck full of hay.

			Pray that your ministry will not just be impressive on the outside but will have real value.

			7. Will it withstand death?

			Pray that your golden ministry will withstand death.

			2. 	Pray that your works are a blessing to many.

			Works of hay only help a few cows but gold helps people all over the world.

			3. 	Pray that your golden ministry will shine.

			Shine through me Lord, so that my Ministry will touch many.

			1Author’s colloquial expression for rebels

			4. 	Pray that your works will shine bright and beautiful to God.

			5. 	Pray that each aspect of your ministry will be valuable.

			Every aspect of the ministry has a certain value. There is gold but there is also diamond and silver. God wants some diamonds, some gold, some silver.

			Prayers Concerning Your Labours

			I know thy works, AND THY LABOUR, and thy patience, and how thou canst not bear them which are evil: and thou hast tried them which say they are apostles, and are not, and hast found them liars.

			Revelation 2:2

			1. 	Your labour in prayers

			Epaphras, who is one of you, a servant of Christ, saluteth you, always labouring fervently for you in prayers, that ye may stand perfect and complete in all the will of God.

			Colossians 4:12

			God is looking at our labours in prayer.

			Pray about your prayer life.

			Lord, let my labour in prayer never cease.

			2. 	Your labour in the lay ministry

			Neither did we eat any man’s bread for nought; but wrought with labour and travail night and day, that we might not be chargeable to any of you

			2 Thessalonians 3:8

			Pray that your labour as a lay pastor will be more beautiful and abundant.

			3. 	Your labour in preaching

			Let the elders that rule well be counted worthy of double honour, especially they who labour in the word and doctrine.

			1 Timothy 5:17

			Pray concerning your labour in the Word.

			4. 	Your labour of love

			Remembering without ceasing your work of faith, and labour of love, and patience of hope in our Lord Jesus Christ, in the sight of God and our Father.

			1 Thessalonians 1:3

			Most of ministry is a labour of love.

			Pray ye therefore, the Lord of the harvest, that he will send forth labourers into his harvest.

			Matthew 9:38

			Pray that God makes you a labourer of love.

			5. 	Your labour as a minister

			For God is not unrighteous to forget your work and LABOUR of love, which ye have shewed toward his name, in that YE HAVE MINISTERED to the saints, and do minister.

			Hebrews 6:10

			Pray that God perfects your service as a minister.

			6.	Your women labourers

			And I entreat thee also, true yokefellow, help THOSE WOMEN WHICH LABOURED with me in the gospel, with Clement also, and with other my fellowlabourers, whose names are in the book of life.

			Philippians 4:3

			Pray that God will release more women labourers.

			7. 	You labour with your own hands

			And labour, working with our own hands: being reviled, we bless; being persecuted, we suffer it:

			1 Corinthians 4:12

			You do not always have to send people, some things you must do yourself. What is God saying that you should do yourself?

			Pray concerning the labour that you must do yourself.

			8. 	Your labour on hard ground

			I am afraid of you, lest I have bestowed upon you labour in vain.

			Galatians 4:11

			Sometimes work is very difficult because the ground is hard.

			Pray concerning fearful missions and people.

			9. 	Your labour in vain

			Except the Lord build the house, they labour in vain that build it: except the Lord keep the city, the watchman waketh but in vain.

			Psalm 127:1

			Come against every labour you are doing in vain.

			Pray that your labour will be accepted.

			Wherefore we labour, that, whether present or absent, we may be accepted of him.

			2 Corinthians 5:9

			But by the grace of God I am what I am: and his grace which was bestowed upon me was not in vain; but I laboured more abundantly than they all: yet not I, but the grace of God which was with me.

			1 Corinthians 15:10

			Pray against These Six Demonic Activities against Your Life

			1. Embarrassment

			2. Hindrances

			3. Harassment - to disturb persistently

			4. Intimidation

			5. Deterrence - to prevent you from preaching and working

			6. Retardation - to slow you down, the spirit of delay

			I bind every evil power that is sent to enforce these things.

			I curse you now! I have victory over satanic works! I have victory over enforcing spirits!

			When you have light and revelation, the ruling of demons stops because they are the rulers of darkness.

			Now whatever represents light which you need to have, to prevent these demons from working upon your life, pray for that light now! Pray because this is the end of the rule of these rulers. The rule of the rulers of darkness is over.

			Lift up your hands and receive the Spirit of light. God is light. The word of God is light.

			Father, thank You for victory over demonic powers.

			Communion Prayer

			The Bread

			Father, we present this holy bread before You

			We say now that this is the body of our Saviour Jesus Christ

			Thank You Lord for Your body which was broken for us

			By His stripes we are healed

			We receive healing and long life as we receive this holy communion today (The body of Jesus)

			As this body enters you, whatever could not be in Jesus cannot be in you

			What diseases could not be in Jesus cannot be in you

			A virus that could not be in Jesus cannot be in you

			Every virus dies!

			Every bacterium dies!

			Every sickness ends!

			Every pestilence ends!

			Every bad news comes to a conclusion in Jesus’ name!

			You are set free!

			You are healed, covered, and helped by the body of our Saviour Jesus Christ. (Eat It)

			The Blood

			By one man, sin entered

			By one man sin, has been removed

			My mistakes, my father’s mistakes, my mother’s mistakes - may their sin, their iniquities, and their transgressions which are passed on to my generation and my children’s generation be neutralised by this cup, the cup of blessing

			Let the curse that has operated in every family be neutralised by this blood

			Let the sins that I have come into the world to perform, and my iniquities and transgressions be washed today and neutralised and their power be nullified by the blood of the Lamb that taketh away the sins of the world.

			May I be restored to God and recover from the effects of my sins, transgressions, and iniquities

			May my sins not be recounted to me anymore

			May my mistakes not be mentioned again

			Any demonic record that are accessed by devils and used against me are neutralised and wiped away by this powerful blood

			The blood that washes away and makes me white as wool

			I receive this cup of blessing and the blood of Jesus. (Drink It)

			The Prophetic Blessing

			May the Passover eradicate what was supposed to stop in your house to bring sadness and destruction

			I ask it to move on, out of your house in the name of Jesus Christ

			What visited you cannot stay, it must go

			What evil came to you cannot stay on

			You are cleansed, forgiven, and restored

			May the visitation of darkness end, and be replaced by the visitation of God

			Receive healing, receive blessing, receive restoration, and health from above

			These are the days of Elijah, days of supernatural involvement with the affairs of our life.

			Amen.





CHAPTER 38





Prayer on the Prophetic Direction in Revelation Chapter 3 & Prayer from Psalm 65




		 			Brethren, be not children in understanding: howbeit in malice be ye children, but in understanding be men. In the law it is written, With men of other tongues and other lips will I speak unto this people; and yet for all that will they not hear me, saith the Lord.”

			1 Corinthians 14:20 -21



		 			When you are speaking in other tongues, God can be guiding you. Value the guidance of the Spirit through speaking in tongues.

			But of the tree of the knowledge of good and evil, thou shalt not eat of it: for in the day that thou eatest thereof thou shalt surely die.

			Genesis 2:17

			When Adam and Eve ate the fruit, they died. They died by:

			i.	Not being close to God.

			ii.	Not being in God’s presence.

			iii.	Not hearing God’s voice.

			We are fighting to get to the presence of God and to hear His voice.

			To be put into effect when the times reach their fulfillment—to bring unity to all things in heaven and on earth under Christ.

			Ephesians 1:10 (NIV)

			The presence which was lost is coming back to your life. God’s presence is coming back to your life. His guidance is coming back to your life. His voice and participation are coming back to your life.

			Begin to pray!

			Prayer Concerning the Prophetic Direction in Revelation Chapter 3

			1. 	Pray that you will be delivered from a false reputation.

			And unto the angel of the church in Sardis write; These things saith he that hath the seven Spirits of God, and the seven stars; I know thy works, that THOU HAST A NAME THAT THOU LIVEST, AND ART DEAD.

			Revelation 3:1

			You may have a certain reputation among men, but in reality it’s not the truth. What do people think of you that is not true? God is showing you and correcting those things as you pray.

			2. 	Pray that the things in your life that are ready to die will be strengthened.

			Be watchful, and STRENGTHEN THE THINGS which remain, THAT ARE READY TO DIE: for I have not found thy works perfect before God.

			Revelation 3:2

			Pray for anything that is not good and ready to die. God has not found our works perfect.

			Have mercy on me, Lord.

			The voice of God is never the voice of only praises. It is a mixture of praise and rebuke.

			Or despisest thou the riches of his goodness and forbearance and longsuffering; not knowing that the goodness of God leadeth thee to repentance?

			Romans 2:4

			3. 	Pray against fornication, adultery, and every form of defilement and contamination.

			Thou hast a few names even in Sardis which HAVE NOT DEFILED THEIR GARMENTS; and they shall walk with me in white: for they are worthy.

			Revelation 3:4

			Pray to come out of fornication and adultery. Pray to come out of all forms of defilements of fornication, unforgiveness, malice, etc.

			Keys to Escaping Defilements

			i. 	Total separation from the person you fornicate with – your “fellow fornicant”.

			Remove thy way far from her, and come not nigh the door of her house:

			Proverbs 5:8

			Decide to remove yourself far away from the person. Don’t do anything together with them.

			Pray for permanent and total separation from the person.

			Father, help us to separate ourselves from this “fellow fornicant”.

			ii. 	Obey instructions against fornication.

			And have not obeyed the voice of my teachers, nor inclined mine ear to them that instructed me!

			Proverbs 5:13

			Pray that you will obey instructions against defilements. Pray about instructions.

			Father, whatever instructions that You have given to me, help me to obey it. Show me instructions that will keep me on the path of righteousness.

			iii. 	Do not despise, mock at and laugh at instructions and 		reproofs.

			We mock at the advice that is always given to us.

			In relation to marriage, we despise counsel and instructions.

			The fear of the Lord is the beginning of knowledge: but fools despise wisdom and instruction.

			Proverbs 1:7

			Pray against the despisement of advice.

			There is no engineering problem that does not have an engineering solution. Therefore, there is no problem in life that does not have a solution.

			4. 	Pray concerning seven things you have received and heard.

			Remember therefore, how thou hast received and heard, and hold fast, and repent. If therefore, thou shalt not watch, I will come on thee as a thief, and thou shalt not know what hour I will come upon thee.

			Revelation 3:3

			Pray that you will remember seven things that God has told you that you are not doing.

			Help me to remember what I have heard and received.

			5. 	Pray to overcome whatever problem that has been placed before you.

			If you don’t have any problem, then there is nothing to overcome. If you have a problem, then there is something to overcome.

			He that overcometh, the same shall be clothed in white raiment; and I will not blot out his name out of the book of life, but I will confess his name before my Father, and before his angels.

			Revelation 3:5

			Pray that you will overcome all your problems! There is no mountain that you cannot overcome! Whatever stands before you, pray and overcome it!

			Pray and overcome:

			 				the Mountain of marital problems!

				the Mountain of financial problems!

				the Mountain of darkness!6. 	Pray concerning your works - “I know thy works.”

And unto the angel of the church in Sardis write; These things saith he that hath the seven Spirits of God, and the seven stars; I know thy works, that thou hast a name that thou livest, and art dead.

Revelation 3:1

7. 	Pray for divine recommendation on earth and in heaven.

He that overcometh, the same shall be clothed in white raiment; and I will not blot out his name out of the book of life, but I will confess his name before my Father, and before his angels.

Revelation 3:5





			May I be recommended and mentioned in a good light in very important places.

			Prayer from Psalm 65

			1. 	Pray to perform every vow that we have made.

			Praise waiteth for thee, O God, in Zion: and unto thee shall the vow be performed.

			Psalm 65:1

			Lord help me perform whatever vow I have made.

			Thou visitest the earth, and waterest it: thou greatly enrichest it with the river of God, which is full of water: thou preparest them corn, when thou hast so provided for it.

			Psalm 65:9

			Lord, visit me and water me until I look fresh. Father visit me, I need divine visitation. Please visit my ministry. Water my ministry. Let the river pass through my life.

			2. 	Pray for God to make us soft with showers.

			Thou waterest the ridges thereof abundantly: thou settlest the furrows thereof: thou makest it soft with showers: thou blessest the springing thereof.

			Psalm 65:10

			Lord, make me soft. Take away all my hardness. Take away all my unyielding attitude. By the showers of your Spirit, make us soft.

			3. 	Pray for God to crown the year with goodness.

			Thou crownest the year with thy goodness; and thy paths drop fatness.

			Psalm 65:11

			Father, superimpose the year with your goodness!

			4. 	Pray for our pastures or churches to be clothed with flocks.

			They drop upon the pastures of the wilderness: and the little hills rejoice on every side. The pastures are clothed with flocks; the valleys also are covered over with corn; they shout for joy, they also sing.

			Psalm 65:12-13

			Communion Prayer

			The Bread

			Through the body of Jesus your salvation is complete

			He said, “Forgive that you may be forgiven.

			Be merciful, that you may receive mercy

			Condemn not, that you may not be condemned.

			Judge not, that you may not be judged”

			Every condemnation, judgement, and lack of mercy in your life; through the blood of Jesus that represents the mercies of God, through the body of Jesus, that represents the goodness of God, may you partake of the goodness of God that comes through Jesus Christ

			Lord Jesus, we honour You and receive Your power as we receive of Your body

			Let power come into our lives, healing across the globe

			Whoever is a part of this prayer will receive healing. (Eat It)

			The Blood

			Your mistakes are in the cup

			They are washed away by the blood that cleanses and washes us from all our sins

			Be forgiven!

			You are not condemned, you are not judged,

			You are forgiven by the blood of Jesus

			Father, let Your healing flow across the globe

			Crown the year with Your goodness instead of fear

			Let there be healing and assurance of life

			Let there be healing and assurance of life! I ask it in the name of Jesus

			Let there be healing and assurance of life

			Thank You for healing and assurance of life

			Receive forgiveness!

			Receive your healing!

			Receive your great blessing in the name of Jesus! (Drink It)





CHAPTER 39




Prayer for the Spirit of Revelation & Prayer for the Blessing in Deuteronomy 28




		 			There was in the days of Herod, the king of Judaea, a certain priest named Zacharias, of the course of Abia: and his wife was of the daughters of Aaron, and her name was Elisabeth. And they were both righteous before God, walking in all the commandments and ordinances of the Lord blameless. And they had no child, because that Elisabeth was barren, and they both were now well stricken in years. And it came to pass, that while he executed the priest’s office before God in the order of his course, According to the custom of the priest’s office, his lot was to burn incense when he went into the temple of the Lord. And the whole multitude of the people were praying without at the time of incense.

			Luke 1:5-10

		 			Elisabeth and her husband walked in all the commandments of God, but they still didn’t have a child. These are the mysteries of life. There is more to life than what meets the eye.

			1. 	Pray to God, come to Him with your life because we need His supernatural involvement.

			But the angel said unto him, Fear not, Zacharias: for thy prayer is heard; and thy wife Elisabeth shall bear thee a son, and thou shalt call his name John. And thou shalt have joy and gladness; and many shall rejoice at his birth.

			Luke 1:13-14

			2. 	Through the intervention of God, you will experience joy and gladness.

			But the angel said unto him, Fear not, Zacharias: for thy prayer is heard; and thy wife Elisabeth shall bear thee a son, and thou shalt call his name John. And THOU SHALT HAVE JOY AND GLADNESS; and many shall rejoice at his birth.

			Luke 1:13-14

			3. 	Things happen when you come to the mountain of God. Therefore, expect supernatural things to happen in your ministry and life.

			Now Moses kept the flock of Jethro his father in law, the priest of Midian: and he led the flock to the backside of the desert, and came to the mountain of God, even to Horeb. And the angel of the Lord appeared unto him in a flame of fire out of the midst of a bush: and he looked, and, behold, the bush burned with fire, and the bush was not consumed.

			Exodus 3:1-2

			4.	Prayer brings angelic participation.

			This is Moses’ supernatural encounter during prayer on the mountain (Exodus 3:1-2). Everybody who knows God goes to God for the supernatural. From today anything in your life that is natural is changing to the supernatural!

			Reasons Why God’s Supernatural Intervention is Needed

			1. 	There are other forces which often have nothing to do with us.

			Wherefore, as by one man sin entered into the world, and death by sin; and so death passed upon all men, for that all have sinned: Nevertheless death reigned from Adam to Moses, even over them that had not sinned after the similitude of Adam’s transgression, who is the figure of him that was to come.

			Romans 5:12, 14

			The sin of Adam is affecting all of us. Death reigned from Adam to Moses - 28 generations. Sin doesn’t end with us. It keeps affecting all generations after us.

			Our fathers have sinned, and are not; and we have borne their iniquities.

			Lamentations 5:7

			Pray to confess your sins and the sins of your fathers. Believe that as you pray, God will be merciful to you.

			Father, have mercy and forgive us our sins and the sins of our fathers.

			Pray and Confess Your Sins

			1. 	Pray to confess your sins of pride.

			Pride is like HIV. It manifests in different ways.

			2. 	Pray and confess your sins of disobedience.

			These are prayers that bring us closer to God.

			Whatever God has asked us to do in ministry that we have not done, let’s pray and ask for forgiveness.

			3. 	Prayer for forgiveness of lusts.

			This is not only about sexual sins, but also other desires that destroy us.

			This is the book of the generations of Adam. In the day that God created man, in the likeness of God made he him.

			Genesis 5:1

			And Adam lived an hundred and thirty years, and begat a son in his own likeness, after his image; and called his name Seth:

			Genesis 5:3

			When Adam gave birth to Seth, his son, Seth, took Adam’s (sinful) image instead of the image of God. Each generation continues to sin because we keep reproducing after our sinful image.

			We lust after other people’s cars, husbands, wives, houses, jobs, etc. Therefore, we must pray for the forgiveness of lust.

			4. 	Pray for forgiveness of your debts.

			And forgive us our debts, as we forgive our debtors.

			Matthew 6:12

			There are things that are not sins, but debts. God should forgive us for not giving our lives to Him. We owe Him our lives.

			Prayer for the Spirits of God

			1. 	Pray for the Spirit of revelation.

			This is prayer for light. This is what changes people.

			Father, reveal what needs to be revealed. Please don’t hide it from me Lord.

			Prayer for the Eight Spirits of Revelation

			1. 	The Spirit of revelation in the KNOWLEDGE OF GOD.

			The knowledge of God is your Christian experience.

			Cease not to give thanks for you, making mention of you in my prayers; That the God of our Lord Jesus Christ, the Father of glory, may give unto you the spirit of wisdom and revelation in the knowledge of him:

			Ephesians 1:16-17

			This prayer will help you to know all the experiences and benefits of Christianity. There are compartments of knowledge. Some people don’t even know that it is a nice thing to serve the Lord.

			Pray that whatever compartment of Christianity is hidden from you shall be revealed to you.

			2. 	The Spirit of revelation in TEACHING YOU.

			This is revelation in teaching you through life and ministry experiences.

			But I certify you, brethren, that the gospel which was preached of me is not after man. For I neither received it of man, neither was I taught it, but by the revelation of Jesus Christ.

			Galatians 1:11-12

			What has God revealed to you? Whatever God wants to teach you, it is the Spirit of revelation who will teach you.

			Teachings, Lord! What you want me to learn, through painful and hard things? Teach me Lord. Lord what I have not learnt, please reveal it to me. Please show me.

			3. The Spirit of revelation that brings VISIONS and DREAMS.

			This is revelation of eternal things or the heavens.

			Your life is changing now as you are being caught up in the third heavens.

			It is not expedient for me doubtless to glory. I will come to VISIONS AND REVELATIONS of the Lord. I knew a man in Christ above fourteen years ago, (whether in the body, I cannot tell; or whether out of the body, I cannot tell: God knoweth;) such an one caught up to the third heaven. And I knew such a man, (whether in the body, or out of the body, I cannot tell: God knoweth;) How that he was caught up into paradise, and heard unspeakable words, which it is not lawful for a man to utter.

			2 Corinthians 12:1-4

			4. 	Pray for an abundance of REVELATION.

			Pray for the spirit of revelation that leads to ministry.

			And lest I should be exalted above measure through the abundance of the revelations, there was given to me a thorn in the flesh, the messenger of Satan to buffet me, lest I should be exalted above measure.

			2 Corinthians 12:7

			5. 	The Spirit of revelation into MYSTERIES and SECRETS.

			Revelation come when the mysteries and secrets around us is revealed.

			Now to him that is of power to stablish you according to my gospel, and the preaching of Jesus Christ, according to the REVELATION OF THE MYSTERY, WHICH WAS KEPT SECRET since the world began.

			Romans 16:25

			There are mysteries and secrets which can help us in ministry and life.

			All secrets and mysteries that surround us are being revealed.

			6. 	The Spirit of revelation of things that MUST SHORTLY COME TO PASS.

			This is a revelation about the future.

			The Revelation of Jesus Christ, which God gave unto him, to shew unto his servants things which must shortly come to pass; and he sent and signified it by his angel unto his servant John:

			Revelation 1:1

			7. 	The Spirit of revelation of JESUS CHRIST.

			This is the revelation of who Jesus is. Jesus is everything. There is nobody like Jesus.

			Wherefore gird up the loins of your mind, be sober, and hope to the end for the grace that is to be brought unto you at the REVELATION OF JESUS CHRIST;

			1 Peter 1:13

			And I fell at his feet to worship him. And he said unto me, See thou do it not: I am thy fellowservant, and of thy brethren that have the testimony of Jesus: worship God: for the testimony of Jesus is the spirit of prophecy.

			Revelation 19:10

			The testimony of Jesus is the Spirit of prophecy. Nobody raised the dead, healed the sick, fed 5,000 men, died and resurrected, and the list goes on. If you have the revelation that there is nobody like Jesus, you will never backslide from Christianity.

			8. 	The Spirit of revelation of JUDGEMENT.

			Most people do not have a revelation of judgement. Everyday God allows us to see and hear many things and these are all revelations.

			Therefore, thou art inexcusable, O man, whosoever thou art that judgest: for wherein thou judgest another, thou condemnest thyself; for thou that judgest doest the same things. But we are sure that the judgment of God is according to truth against them which commit such things. And thinkest thou this, O man, that judgest them which do such things, and doest the same, that thou shalt escape the judgment of God? Or despisest thou the riches of his goodness and forbearance and longsuffering; not knowing that the goodness of God leadeth thee to repentance? But after thy hardness and impenitent heart treasurest up unto thyself wrath against the day of wrath and revelation of the righteous judgment of God;

			Romans 2:1-5

			Areas in Which You Must Have the Spirit of Revelation of Judgment

			1.	The revelation of not attacking your father.

			2.	The revelation of what it means not to honour your father.

			3.	The revelation of what it means not to honour your spiritual father.

			4.	The revelation of what it means to be disloyal, to be a traitor, to be a liar, to practice fornication, and the effect of not paying your tithe.

			5.	The revelation of studying hard when you are in school.

			6.	The revelation of debts. If we knew what it means not to be in debt, life would be different.

			Prayer for the Blessings in Deuteronomy 28

			1. 	Pray for the blessing to be above the nations.

			Pray shamelessly for the blessing to be on high just as Jacob did.

			And it shall come to pass, if thou shalt hearken diligently unto the voice of the Lord thy God, to observe and to do all his commandments which I command thee this day, that the Lord thy God will set thee on high above all nations of the earth:

			Deuteronomy 28:1

			And he said, Let me go, for the day breaketh. And he said, I will not let thee go, except thou bless me.

			Genesis 32:26

			2. 	Pray for the blessing in the city and the blessing in the field.

			Pray for it shamelessly.

			Blessed shalt thou be in the city, and blessed shalt thou be in the field.

			Deuteronomy 28:3

			3. 	Pray for the blessing of the fruit of thy body.

			Blessed shall be the fruit of thy body, and the fruit of thy ground, and the fruit of thy cattle, the increase of thy kine, and the flocks of thy sheep.

			Deuteronomy 28:4

			Father, bless my family. Bless my children. Please bless my grandchildren.

			4. 	Pray for a blessing on your business, your profession, your job.

			Blessed shall be the fruit of thy body, and the fruit of thy ground, and the fruit of thy cattle, the increase of thy kine, and the flocks of thy sheep.

			Deuteronomy 28:4

			Pray that your business will increase. Pray that you will be promoted at your work place. Pray that you will not die while you are going to work.

			Father, bless the works of our hands.

			5. 	Pray for your basket and your store to be full.

			Your basket is your bank account, your wallet and your purse.

			Blessed shall be thy basket and thy store.

			Deuteronomy 28:5

			6. 	Pray for the blessing to come in and to go out.

			This is a blessing for traveling.

			Blessed shalt thou be when thou comest in, and blessed shalt thou be when thou goest out.

			Deuteronomy 28:6

			I will not die when I am going out! I will not die when I am coming in! I will not die in my journeys, by air, by sea or on land.

			7. 	Pray against your enemies who shall come against you.

			May they be smitten by the Lord!

			The Lord shall cause thine enemies that rise up against thee to be smitten before thy face: they shall come out against thee one way, and flee before thee seven ways.

			Deuteronomy 28:7

			Father, scatter them in the name of Jesus! Break them into pieces according to Your Word, Lord. I declare that my enemies are broken into pieces!

			Pray against any power that conspires, intimidates and blasphemes. Bind them! Bind all your mockers!

			We condemn every tongue that rises against us.

			8. 	Pray for a blessing on your storehouse and whatever you set your hand to do.

			The Lord shall command the blessing upon thee in thy storehouses, and in all that thou settest thine hand unto; and he shall bless thee in the land which the Lord thy God giveth thee.

			Deuteronomy 28:8

			Father, please bless whatever I am doing. Bless whatever I have set my hands to do. I ask for this blessing, Lord.

			9. 	Pray for God to establish you in holiness.

			Holy means special and separated.

			The Lord shall establish thee an holy people unto himself, as he hath sworn unto thee, if thou shalt keep the commandments of the Lord thy God, and walk in his ways.

			Deuteronomy 28:9

			God is going to establish you as holy. You will not feel sick of yourself anymore. You will not feel dirty. You will not be a perverted person.

			Please establish me as a holy person unto You Lord. Thank you for establishing me.

			10. 	Pray for God to make you plenteous in goods, in the fruit of thy cattle and in the fruit of thy ground. Plenteous means “no lack”.

			And the Lord shall make thee plenteous in goods, in the fruit of thy body, and in the fruit of thy cattle, and in the fruit of thy ground, in the land which the Lord sware unto thy fathers to give thee.

			Deuteronomy 28:11

			Let there be no want. Father, let there be no scarcity. Please let there be no lack, but rather let me be plenteous in goods and plenteous in the fruit of my body; plenteous in the fruit of my cattle; plenteous in the fruit of ground in the name of Jesus.

			11. 	Pray for God to open His good treasure in His season.

			The Lord shall open unto thee his good treasure, the heaven to give the rain unto thy land in his season, and to bless all the work of thine hand: and thou shalt lend unto many nations, and thou shalt not borrow.

			Deuteronomy 28:12

			12. 	Pray for God to cause you to lend and NOT borrow.

			The Lord shall open unto thee his good treasure, the heaven to give the rain unto thy land in his season, and to bless all the work of thine hand: and thou shalt lend unto many nations, and thou shalt not borrow.

			Deuteronomy 28:12

			13. 	Pray that you shall be above ONLY and NOT beneath, you shall be the head and not the tail.

			And the Lord shall make thee the head, and not the tail; and thou shalt be above only, and thou shalt not be beneath; if that thou hearken unto the commandments of the Lord thy God, which I command thee this day, to observe and to do them:

			Deuteronomy 28:13

			I shall be above in ministry! My wife shall be above and not beneath in all her endeavours. My children shall be above in all their endeavours, in church, in school, etc.

			14. 	Pray and bind any particular devil that harasses you.

			Bind whatever personal devil is in your life, harassing and frustrating you. Bind it now in the name of Jesus!

			I say - out of my life! Out of my marriage! Out of my wife’s life! Out of my children’s life! I cast you out of my home! I curse you to the roots!





CHAPTER 40




Prayer for the Spirit of Grace and Supplications & Prayer for Our Ministries




		 			And I will pour upon the house of David, and upon the inhabitants of Jerusalem, the spirit of grace and of supplications: and they shall look upon me whom they have pierced, and they shall mourn for him, as one mourneth for his only son, and shall be in bitterness for him, as one that is in bitterness for his firstborn.

			Zechariah 12:10

		 			Pray for the Spirit of Grace and Supplications

			1.	Pray about your mistakes.

			Look upon the one whom you have pierced or hurt, which is God, and pray about it.

			2.	Pray about your calling.

			3. 	Pray concerning the things that you said you would do and have not done - failed promises.

			Pray and mourn about it now and not when you go to heaven.

			4. 	Pray about those whom you have hurt or pierced (marital relationships).

			Through the Spirit of grace and supplications you can change or recompense some of the hurts caused in your marital relationship. Normally we don’t feel the pain we cause to somebody.

			Lord, we pray for the opportunity to recompense for the pain we caused our partners. We pray for mercy. We pray for time, and for the spirit of grace and supplications.

			5.	Pray for other people that you may have hurt in your life.

			...and they shall look upon me whom they have pierced…

			Zechariah 12:10

			These are the reasons why curses come on upon us: being treacherous, disloyal, unfaithful to others - former boyfriends, former girlfriends, etc - people who were promised marriage but never were married. Their lives were changed when they were left. Mercy Lord!

			Lord, have mercy on us.

			Sometimes we do certain things to people as if we don’t have feelings ourselves.

			Lord, have mercy and forgive us. Lord, have mercy for all the mourning that we have caused others.

			The governor answered and said unto them, Whether of the twain will ye that I release unto you? They said, Barabbas. Pilate saith unto them, What shall I do then with Jesus which is called Christ? They all say unto him, Let him be crucified. And the governor said, Why, what evil hath he done? But they cried out the more, saying, Let him be crucified. When Pilate saw that he could prevail nothing, but that rather a tumult was made, he took water, and washed his hands before the multitude, saying, I am innocent of the blood of this just person: see ye to it. Then answered all the people, and said, His blood be on us, and on our children.

			Matthew 27:21-25

			When you see what you have done, you will realise that you need grace and supplications.

			6. 	By the spirit of grace and supplications, pray and overturn generational curses.

			Our fathers have sinned, and are not; and we have borne their iniquities.

			Lamentations 5:7

			Noah cursed Ham’s children. Noah’s hurt caused him to curse Ham’s children and affect children who were not born. That may also be the case with our mothers and fathers who have pierced or hurt somebody’s home. Therefore, we need grace and supplications.

			Thank you, Lord, for the intervention of the Holy Spirit for overturning all curses.

			7. 	Pray against conspiracies and meetings to take counsel against you.

			Why do the heathen rage, and the people imagine a vain thing? The kings of the earth set themselves, and the rulers take counsel together, against the Lord, and against his anointed, saying,

			Psalm 2:1-2

			Father, we pray against any conspiracy, any meeting to take counsel against our ministries, jobs, marriages and children. I reverse any counsel against my job. I reverse any conspiracy against my job. I cancel their plans.

			Lift your hands in judgement against conspiracies, meetings, decisions against you.

			Every decision to dismiss me from my job is not going to work in Jesus’ Name!

			Associate yourselves, O ye people, and ye shall be broken in pieces; and give ear, all ye of far countries: gird yourselves, and ye shall be broken in pieces; gird yourselves, and ye shall be broken in pieces. TAKE COUNSEL TOGETHER, AND IT SHALL COME TO NOUGHT; speak the word, and it shall not stand: for God is with us.

			Isaiah 8:9-10

			We condemn every tongue that rises against our jobs, marriages, children, ministry, finances in the name of Jesus.

			You may be a young lady whom a brother wants to marry. He may be discussing the idea with his friends but by the time they finish the meeting, the decision to marry you is cancelled. Therefore, pray!

			Lord, they shall take counsel together and it shall come to nought. Let them speak the word and it shall not stand. For God is with me.

			8. 	The prayer of grace and supplications secures angelic intervention.

			Pray to be sensitive to the guidance of the Holy Spirit.

			And when it was determined that we should sail into Italy, they delivered Paul and certain other prisoners unto one named Julius, a centurion of Augustus’ band. Now when much time was spent, and when sailing was now dangerous, because the fast was now already past, Paul admonished them, and said unto them, Sirs, I perceive that this voyage will be with hurt and much damage, not only of the lading and ship, but also of our lives. Nevertheless the centurion believed the master and the owner of the ship, more than those things which were spoken by Paul.

			Acts 27:1, 9-11

			Paul admonished the people on the ship, yet the centurion did not believe what the Spirit revealed to Paul.

			9. 	Pray for direction and spiritual guidance.

			Pray that you will be very sensitive to the voice of the Holy Spirit.

			May I be very sensitive to Your voice, Holy Spirit. I pray for supernatural direction, supernatural leadings and supernatural navigations of the Holy Spirit.

			10. 	Pray against all voices that prevent you from hearing the voice of God.

			Nevertheless the centurion believed the master and the owner of the ship, more than those things which were spoken by Paul.

			Acts 27:11

			There is a third voice influencing you and therefore, you cannot hear the voice of the Holy Spirit.

			Pray against whatever represents a third voice in your life. Pray against all other voices.

			Father, we silence alternative voices. We silence every “ship owner’s” voice. We pray against every misleading spirit.

			We pray against every advice that sounds good, but is actually bad. We curse the gentle voice that leads to destruction.

			Thou in thy mercy hast led forth the people which thou hast redeemed: thou hast guided them in thy strength unto thy holy habitation.

			Exodus 15:13

			11. 	Pray against committing such a terrible mistake like what the centurion did, of not listening to the prophetic Word from Paul.

			Pray that you will be sensitive to prophetic Words.

			And said unto them, Sirs, I perceive that this voyage will be with hurt and much damage, not only of the lading and ship, but also of our lives. Nevertheless the centurion believed the master and the owner of the ship, more than those things which were spoken by Paul.

			Acts 27:10-11

			We have also a more sure word of prophecy; whereunto ye do well that ye take heed, as unto a light that shineth in a dark place, until the day dawn, and the day star arise in your hearts:

			2 Peter 1:19

			We do well by paying attention to the prophetic Word. When we are in the dark place, we do well by paying attention to the light that shines. What is the prophetic Word? The Bible.

			12.	Pray against the “Euroclydon” which comes for not listening to the prophetic Word.

			Pray against whatever represents “Euroclydon” in your life - ministerial Euroclydons, marital Euroclydons, financial Euroclydons and business Euroclydons.

			But not long after there arose against it a tempestuous wind, called Euroclydon.

			Acts 27:14

			We rebuke the Euroclydon! We rebuke ministry tornadoes, financial tornadoes and every economic Euroclydon. We bind them. We come out of any tempestuous wind.

			And when the ship was caught, and could not bear up into the wind, we let her drive.

			Acts 27:15

			“The ship was caught” means that the ship was trapped. They couldn’t control it. You are coming out of whatever ship you cannot control, right now, in Jesus’ Name! Now! Out of the chain! Out of the trap which you cannot come out of! Out of the pit!

			And we being exceedingly tossed with a tempest, the next day they lightened the ship; And the third day we cast out with our own hands the tackling of the ship.

			Acts 27:18-19

			These things will not happen to us! Our lives will not come to nothing in Jesus’ Name!

			But after long abstinence Paul stood forth in the midst of them, and said, Sirs, ye should have hearkened unto me, and not have loosed from Crete, and to have gained this harm and loss.

			Acts 27:21

			It is angels that bring supernatural recoveries.

			There shall be no loss. If we have lost “Section A”, we will not lose “Section B”.

			And now I exhort you to be of good cheer: for there shall be no loss of any man’s life among you, but of the ship. For there stood by me this night the angel of God, whose I am, and whom I serve, Saying, Fear not, Paul; thou must be brought before Caesar: and, lo, God hath given thee all them that sail with thee. Wherefore, sirs, be of good cheer: for I believe God, that it shall be even as it was told me. Howbeit we must be cast upon a certain island.

			Acts 27:22-26

			Lord, let Your will be done and let my destiny be fulfilled. I must be cast upon a certain island. Thy will be done. I must testify before a wicked king. Lord, let Your will be done.

			The destiny and the plan of God may be to be cast you on a certain island.

			Thy will be done, Lord. I will achieve the maximum plan of my ministry. Thy will be done in my ministry, in my marriage, in my businesses and in my children’s’ life.

			And the soldiers’ counsel was to kill the prisoners, lest any of them should swim out, and escape. But the centurion, willing to save Paul, kept them from their purpose; and commanded that they which could swim should cast themselves first into the sea, and get to land:

			Acts 27:42-43

			And the rest, some on boards, and some on broken pieces of the ship. And so it came to pass, that they escaped all safe to land.

			Acts 27:44

			And when they were escaped, then they knew that the island was called Melita.

			Acts 28:1

			“Melita” is Malta. Malta is a prophetic island.

			“Before Caesar” is the climax of your ministry.

			As we escape the wickedness of our lives, we will reach the climax of our calling in ministry.

			Therefore, let Your will be done Lord so that I will land on my Malta, Lord. We are arriving on the island of Malta on our way to the climax of our ministry.

			And the barbarous people shewed us no little kindness: for they kindled a fire, and received us every one, because of the present rain, and because of the cold. And when Paul had gathered a bundle of sticks, and laid them on the fire, there came a viper out of the heat, and fastened on his hand.

			And he shook off the beast into the fire, and felt no harm. Howbeit they looked when he should have swollen, or fallen down dead suddenly: but after they had looked a great while, and saw no harm come to him, they changed their minds, and said that he was a god.

			Acts 28:2-3, 5-6

			Bind any demon that wants to attack you on your way to your destiny!

			As if a man did flee from a lion, and a bear met him; or went into the house, and leaned his hand on the wall, and a serpent bit him.

			Amos 5:19

			Bind whatever demon attacks your life, bind it now!

			In that day the Lord with his sore and great and strong sword shall punish leviathan the piercing serpent, even leviathan that crooked serpent; and he shall slay the dragon that is in the sea.

			Isaiah 27:1

			Every demon that has attacked you is going to regret it!

			Satan, you will be surprised and you will suffer for assignments against me!

			Whatever represents a demonic serpent - be it:

			1. Serpent of opposition

			2. Serpent of fornication

			3. Serpent of immortality

			4. Serpent of drunkenness

			5. Serpent of laziness

			6. Serpent of procrastination

			We bind it in the name of Jesus!

			The end has come for anything that has fastened itself or tries to fasten itself to me. Every fastened cobra, back mamba, I behead that devil. I bind its power in the name of Jesus. Every demon of opposition! Every demon of alcoholism! Every demon of prostitution! Every demon of procrastination! We bind you! Every spirit of poverty! Every spirit of bankruptcy! Every spirit of stupid financial decisions! I bind you! Every demonic power to cause death, cancer, diabetes, we bind you.

			I declare judgement and war against any snake that is trying to fasten itself on me. I disconnect from you now. I reject you! I lift up a curse against you. I declare I am escaping now. I detach myself from you. I cut off the serpent that is trying to shorten my life and my destiny. Every contamination! Every encroachment! Every defilement! I cut you off! I renounce every shameful covenant. I detach myself from it now. Today marks the end of that ancestral and generational curse. Whatever power that makes me poor and in need all the time, I cut it off now.

			13. 	Pray to fulfil your ministry.

			And when we came to Rome, the centurion delivered the prisoners to the captain of the guard: but Paul was suffered to dwell by himself with a soldier that kept him.

			Acts 28:16

			People like Paul obeyed God and took Christianity to Rome. Rome was the secular centre of the world.

			We are praying to go to our Rome - the strategic place of your calling.

			Then said Jesus to them again, Peace be unto you: as my Father hath sent me, even so send I you.

			John 20:21

			Prayer for Our Ministries

			(Ministries to Pray for in My Life)

			1. 	Pray for your prophetic or strategic calling or ministry.

			The dreams, the visions and the guidance, Lord let it come to pass.

			2. 	Pray for your teaching ministry, that you will fulfil it.

			For when for the time ye ought to be teachers, ye have need that one teach you again which be the first principles of the oracles of God; and are become such as have need of milk, and not of strong meat.

			Hebrews 5:12

			3.	Pray for your Pastoral Ministry.

			4.	Pray for your Apostolic Ministry.

			5.	Pray for your Evangelistic Ministry to the ends of the 	world.

			6.	Pray for your Helps Ministry.

			7.	Pray for your Giving Ministry.

			Pray to become one of the best givers in the church.

			Or he that exhorteth, on exhortation: he that giveth, let him do it with simplicity; he that ruleth, with diligence; he that sheweth mercy, with cheerfulness.

			Romans 12:8

			The Prophetic Blessing

			Every holy hand lifted, thanking God!

			I call you a prophet

			I call you a teacher

			I call you a pastor

			I call you an apostle

			I call you an evangelist

			I call you a helper

			I call you a giver because you are a giver

			Thank You for delivering us from tempestuous winds

			Thank You for delivering us from the Euroclydon

			Thank You, Lord

			Thank You for delivering me from all demonic snakes that tried to fasten themselves to my ministry, my marriage, my children, my job, my finances and my health

			Thank You Lord

			Now we thank You for Rome, the climax of my ministry, the epicentre of Christianity

			Thank You father

			May angels visit me in my dwelling place.
