CHAPTER 41




Prayer for the Spirit of Prophecy; Prayer for a Blessing & Prayer about The Ministry




		 			And I fell at his feet to worship him. And he said unto me, See thou do it not: I am thy fellowservant, and of thy brethren that have the testimony of Jesus: worship God: for the testimony of Jesus is the spirit of prophecy.

			Revelation 19:10

		 			Prophecy is also a spirit. For the testimony of Jesus is the Spirit of prophecy.

			Pray for the Spirit of prophecy.

			1. The Spirit of prophecy is going to be the Spirit of visions and dreams.

			Therefore, as you pray now for the Spirit of prophecy, you are praying for the Spirit of visions and dreams. The greatest enemy of prayer is the spirit of slumber. Therefore, keep praying.

			Father, open our eyes and give us the Spirit of prophecy. Please let our visions and dreams be real and not something that are coming out of tiredness.

			And it came to pass, as he was alone praying, his disciples were with him: and he asked them, saying, Whom say the people that I am? They answering said, John the Baptist; but some say, Elias; and others say, that one of the old prophets is risen again. He said unto them, But whom say ye that I am? Peter answering said, The Christ of God. And he straitly charged them, and commanded them to tell no man that thing;

			Luke 9:18-21

			You cannot tell traitors not to tell anyone certain things. They will say it.

			When Jesus came into the coasts of Caesarea Philippi, he asked his disciples, saying, Whom do men say that I the Son of man am? And they said, Some say that thou art John the Baptist: some, Elias; and others, Jeremias, or one of the prophets. He saith unto them, But whom say ye that I am? And Simon Peter answered and said, Thou art the Christ, the Son of the living God. And Jesus answered and said unto him, Blessed art thou, Simon Barjona: for flesh and blood hath not revealed it unto thee, but my Father which is in heaven.

			Matthew 16:13-17

			From these two scriptures (in Luke and Matthew), Jesus was praying for revelation and the revelation came to Peter. When we come to God in prayer, the Holy Spirit also comes with us and God begins to reveal things to us through the Holy Spirit. The Father Himself started revealing things to Peter.

			What must I know and what must be revealed to me? Lord, I ask for the Spirit of revelation.

			How the Spirit of Prophecy Works

			One of the ways through which the Spirit of prophecy works is through a burden.

			1.	 The burden of Nahum is the burden of Nineveh

			The burden of Nineveh. The book of the vision of Nahum the Elkoshite.

			Nahum 1:1

			2. 	The burden of Habakkuk

			The burden which Habakkuk the prophet did see.

			Habakkuk 1:1

			Sometimes when something is weighing you down, it means it is a burden, a worry.

			3. 	The burden of Babylon

			The burden of Babylon, which Isaiah the son of Amoz did see.

			Isaiah 13:1

			4. 	The burden of Isaiah when King Ahaz died

			In the year that king Ahaz died was this burden.

			Isaiah 14:28

			5. 	The burden of Damascus

			The burden of Damascus. Behold, Damascus is taken away from being a city, and it shall be a ruinous heap.

			Isaiah 17:1

			6. 	The burden of Egypt

			The burden of Egypt. Behold, the Lord rideth upon a swift cloud, and shall come into Egypt: and the idols of Egypt shall be moved at his presence, and the heart of Egypt shall melt in the midst of it.

			Isaiah 19:1

			7. 	The burden of Dumah

			The burden of Dumah. He calleth to me out of Seir, Watchman, what of the night? Watchman, what of the night?

			Isaiah 21:11

			8. 	A burden means a spiritual desire

			Even though many of our desires may not be good, we must note that there are prophetic desires.

			Examples of Spiritual Desires

			1. I want to be missionary.

			2. I want to start a church.

			3. I want to witness.

			4. I want to support the Healing Jesus Campaign financially.

			These are all spiritual desires. If we don’t respect these things, we will not be used by God. Therefore, we must respect these spiritual desires.

			Prayer for the Spirit of Prophecy to Know the Will of God

			But ye have an unction from the Holy One, and ye know all things.

			But the anointing which ye have received of him abideth in you, and ye need not that any man teach you: but as the same anointing teacheth you of all things, and is truth, and is no lie, and even as it hath taught you, ye shall abide in him.

			1 John 2:20, 27

			What is the Most Important Thing in this World?

			The most important thing is the will of God. Your relevance comes by doing the will of God.

			And the world passeth away, and the lust thereof: but he that doeth the will of God abideth for ever.

			1 John 2:17

			And the world passes away and disappears, and with it the forbidden cravings (the passionate desires, the lust) of it; but he who does the will of God and carries out His purposes in his life abides (remains) forever.

			1 John 2:17 (AMPC)

			When you do the will of God, you remain relevant, you remain important, you remain useful, you stay around and you stay forever.

			Father, make me relevant in the years ahead of my life. May my importance not be lost in the name of Jesus. May I do your will Lord.

			Our school books will not be important in just a few years. The Bible will remain very important for the rest of our lives.

			Prayer of Thanksgiving

			Giving thanks is the Spirit of prophecy.

			After these things Jesus went over the sea of Galilee, which is the sea of Tiberias. And a great multitude followed him, because they saw his miracles which he did on them that were diseased. And Jesus went up into a mountain, and there he sat with his disciples. And the passover, a feast of the Jews, was nigh. When Jesus then lifted up his eyes, and saw a great company come unto him, he saith unto Philip, Whence shall we buy bread, that these may eat? And this he said to prove him: for he himself knew what he would do. Philip answered him, Two hundred pennyworth of bread is not sufficient for them, that every one of them may take a little. One of his disciples, Andrew, Simon Peter’s brother, saith unto him, there is a lad here, which hath five barley loaves, and two small fishes: but what are they among so many?And Jesus said, Make the men sit down. Now there was much grass in the place. So the men sat down, in number about five thousand. And Jesus took the loaves; and WHEN HE HAD GIVEN THANKS, he distributed to the disciples, and the disciples to them that were set down; and likewise of the fishes as much as they would.

			John 6:1-11

			Howbeit there came other boats from Tiberias nigh unto the place where they did eat bread, after that the Lord had given thanks:

			John 6:23

			You must eat bread after you have given thanks.

			Whatever isn’t enough in your life, give thanks for it and God will glorify His name. Anything that is small in our lives, God through His supernatural power, will change it!

			How Our Prayers are Answered

			Prayer is an amazing thing. Prayer changes things. Prayer secures angelic intervention and angelic activity on your behalf.

			Thinkest thou that I cannot now pray to my Father, and he shall presently give me more than TWELVE LEGIONS OF ANGELS?

			Matthew 26:53

			A legion is made up of 6828 angels. Therefore, “12 legions” means 12 x 6826 = 81,912 angels.

			Our prayers secure:

			i. Angelic intervention

			ii. Angelic activity

			iii. Angelic participation

			iv. Angelic involvement on our behalf

			Peter therefore, was kept in prison: but prayer was made without ceasing of the church unto God for him. And when Herod would have brought him forth, the same night Peter was sleeping between two soldiers, bound with two chains: and the keepers before the door kept the prison. And, behold, the angel of the Lord came upon him, and a light shined in the prison: and he smote Peter on the side, and raised him up, saying, Arise up quickly. And his chains fell off from his hands. And the angel said unto him, Gird thyself, and bind on thy sandals. And so he did. And he saith unto him, Cast thy garment about thee, and follow me. And he went out, and followed him; and wist not that it was true which was done by the angel; but thought he saw a vision. When they were past the first and the second ward, they came unto the iron gate that leadeth unto the city; which opened to them of his own accord: and they went out, and passed on through one street; and forthwith the angel departed from him. And when Peter was come to himself, he said, Now I know of a surety, that the Lord hath sent his angel, and hath delivered me out of the hand of Herod, and from all the expectation of the people of the Jews.

			Acts 12:5-11

			“Prayer was made without ceasing of the church unto God for him [for Peter].” Pray without ceasing.

			1. 	Prayer secures angelic intervention and releases what is bound into our lives.

			No prayer, no angels!

			Pray specifically for your beloved who has been held up somewhere to be released. If you are not married and you are praying this prophetic prayer, believe that it shall come to pass.

			God sent His angel to release Peter from the hands of Herod.

			And when Peter was come to himself, he said, Now I know of a surety, that the Lord hath sent his angel, and hath delivered me out of the hand of Herod, and from all the expectation of the people of the Jews.

			Acts 12:11

			2. 	Prayer brings supernatural interventions.

			When you do not pray, everything becomes natural and everything natural is bad.

			Pray that anything that is tied up in your life will be released.

			Pray that the chains which have held up anything good for you would be released.

			Whatever belongs to me and has been tied up in the spiritual realm, Father, release it in the name of Jesus.

			The “Peter” of Your Life is Untied

			The “Peter” of my life is untied! The chains are broken! The limitations are removed! The stagnation in ministry is removed! I shall move forward in ministry! Financial stagnation is removed! I come out of every prison of want! I come out of every prison of lack! I come out of every spiritual prison!

			3.	Angelic interventions contribute to the binding of the devil.

			Any demonic harassment is ending now as you pray.

			And I heard a great voice out of the temple saying to the seven angels, Go your ways, and pour out the vials of the wrath of God upon the earth. And the first went, and poured out his vial upon the earth; and there fell a noisome and grievous sore upon the men which had the mark of the beast, and upon them which worshipped his image. And the second angel poured out his vial upon the sea; and it became as the blood of a dead man: and every living soul died in the sea. And the third angel poured out his vial upon the rivers and fountains of waters; and they became blood. And I heard the angel of the waters say, Thou art righteous, O Lord, which art, and wast, and shalt be, because thou hast judged thus. For they have shed the blood of saints and prophets, and thou hast given them blood to drink; for they are worthy.

			Revelation 16:1-6

			4. 	Fair judgements are administered by angels.

			One of the reasons why we must leave vengeance to God is because we would not give people blood to drink as a form of revenge.

			An angel is being released to judge anyone who has been unfair to you.

			Father send angels to judge whatever is against us and needs judgement in our lives!

			Prayers for Blessings

			1. 	May God create certain circumstances that will separate us from certain people in our lives.

			The company we keep, affects us. Show me who you are moving with and I will tell you the kind of blessing you will have.

			There are three people who must be out of our lives. These people are the “third voice” in our lives:

			1.	The ungodly

			2.	Sinners

			3.	The Scornful or Mockers

			Blessed is the man that walketh not in the counsel of the ungodly, nor standeth in the way of sinners, nor sitteth in the seat of the scornful.

			Psalm 1:1

			Whoever represents the ungodly must be out of our lives! Whoever represents a sinner must be out of our lives! Whoever represents a mocker must be out of our lives!

			Pray because a blessing is coming into your life by the removal of the ungodly, sinners and mockers.

			We lift up our prayers and we say save us from the pathway of the ungodly, sinners and the scornful. Deliver us from the seat of the scornful or the mockers! They that mock my calling, my ministry, Father save me from them! They mock my calling, they mock my ministry, they mock my preaching, they mock my teachings, they mock my counsel, save me Lord and bless me so that I will see them no more. Bless me Lord so that I will not see them anymore. Let a divine separation take place Lord. Divinely cut them out of my life in the name of Jesus.

			2. 	Pray that you will fall in love with God and delight in His presence day and night.

			But his delight is in the law of the Lord; and in his law doth he meditate day and night.

			Psalm 1:2

			Many of us are not delighting in the Lord. When you love someone, you meditate about the person day and night.

			Pray for it and you will soon be a God-lover. That means you will love God.

			Father give me a love for Your Word. Give me a love for You, Lord.

			3. 	Pray for God to make you like a tree planted by a river.

			When you are planted by a river, you are very healthy and strong.

			And he shall be like a tree planted by the rivers of water, that bringeth forth his fruit in his season; his leaf also shall not wither; and whatsoever he doeth shall prosper.

			Psalm 1:3

			Father we lift our hands to You and call on You to make us a tree planted by the river. If I am planted at a wrong place, may I be replanted at the right place. If I am planted at the desert, may I be replanted at another place by the river.

			4. 	Pray to bring forth fruit in your season.

			And he shall be like a tree planted by the rivers of water, that bringeth forth his fruit in his season; his leaf also shall not wither; and whatsoever he doeth shall prosper.

			Psalm 1:3

			You will be in ministry when you are supposed to be ministry! You will marry when you are supposed to marry! You will prosper when you are supposed to prosper! You will have children when you are supposed to have children! Anything out of God’s time is being corrected now.

			5. 	Pray for “whatsoever he does shall prosper”.

			And he shall be like a tree planted by the rivers of water, that bringeth forth his fruit in his season; his leaf also shall not wither; and whatsoever he doeth shall prosper.

			Psalm 1:3

			Father, let whatsoever I do prosper! Let my ministry prosper! Let me prosper in marriage! Let me prosper in my profession! Father help us to prosper!

			6. 	Pray against ungodliness in you.

			The ungodly are not so: but are like the chaff which the wind driveth away.

			Psalm 1:4

			Pray that God shall have mercy on whatever is ungodly in us.

			Whatever is ungodly in us, please have mercy so that we will not be blown away like a chaff.

			7.	Pray against anything that brings withering in your life.

			Something that withers is cursed. Perhaps your father or your mother have sinned and you have borne their iniquities.

			Our fathers have sinned, and are not; and we have borne their iniquities.

			Lamentations 5:7

			Whatever mistake that has come into my life and causes a withering in my life, please show me mercy. Have mercy Lord. There are a lot of unexplained evil and wickedness in your life. Things just don’t work out easily - poverty, difficulties, marital challenges, chronic singleness, stagnation in life, starting something and not finishing, please exempt us! Please exclude us, show us mercy Lord. Our fathers have sinned. Our mothers have sinned. Please don’t let us bear their iniquities!

			Prayer for Our Ministries

			1.	Pray to fulfil your personal Apostolic Ministry.

			Pray for your apostolic ministry.

			And are built upon the foundation of the apostles and prophets, Jesus Christ himself being the chief corner stone;

			Ephesians 2:20

			2. 	Pray for the completion of your Prophetic Ministry.

			Pray for the completion of your prophetic ministry.

			Don’t despise yourself. You have the Holy Spirit and everything should not be all natural. Elijah was depressed and was lying down. But when an angel touched him, everything changed and he was able to continue his ministry and later on raise up Elisha in ministry (1 Kings 19).

			Our prophetic calling is coming to pass!

			3. 	Pray for your Evangelistic Ministry.

			The evangelistic ministry is the nicest part of all your ministry.

			Your evangelistic ministry is being fulfilled as you pray.

			4. 	Pray for your Pastoral Ministry.

			The pastoral ministry is the loving part of your ministry.

			So when they had dined, Jesus saith to Simon Peter, Simon, son of Jonas, lovest thou me more than these? He saith unto him, Yea, Lord; thou knowest that I love thee. He saith unto him, Feed my lambs. He saith to him again the second time, Simon, son of Jonas, lovest thou me? He saith unto him, Yea, Lord; thou knowest that I love thee. He saith unto him, Feed my sheep. He saith unto him the third time, Simon, son of Jonas, lovest thou me? Peter was grieved because he said unto him the third time, LOVEST THOU ME? And he said unto him, Lord, thou knowest all things; thou knowest that I love thee. Jesus saith unto him, Feed my sheep.

			John 21: 15-17

			Lift your hands and intercede concerning your pastoral ministry. Pray for your shepherding anointing, your pastoral anointing.

			5. 	Pray for your Teaching Ministry.

			Pray about your teaching ministry.

			6. 	Pray for your Helps Ministry.

			This is the big area of all the ministries:

			i. Music

			ii. Administration

			iii. Personal assistance

			iv. Governmental

			Pray that you will finish your helps ministry.

			7. 	Pray for your Giving Ministry.

			Father, help me to be a giver in your house with simplicity and cheerfulness.

			Or he that exhorteth, on exhortation: he that giveth, let him do it with simplicity; he that ruleth, with diligence; he that sheweth mercy, with cheerfulness.

			Romans 12:8

			Forgive me Lord for not giving what I should have given.

			8. 	Pray again for your father’s iniquities.

			Our fathers have sinned, and are not; and we have borne their iniquities.

			Lamentations 5:7

			Thank you for saving us from our father’s iniquities.

			9. 	Pray to finish your ministry.

			There are so many things that can prevent us from finishing our calling. There are so many things that can prevent us from finishing our ministry.

			Lift up your hand and pray. You will finish what you have started. You are not going to die before you finish! You will finish every aspect of your ministry! Lift your hands and thank Him. Anything holding you back has given up because of angelic intervention!

			Prayer to Bind the Devil

			i. 	Pray against the Black Mamba.

			Bind the black mamba!

			Bind anything that is being sent to loosen anything in your life, to destroy your ministry. We reject and bind it in the name of Jesus.

			Every venom, every poison, every accusation, every spirit of intimidation, every spirit of death, every spirit of sickness, every bad news from a far country, I reject it and I bind it in the name of Jesus.

			ii. 	Prayer against [Spiritual] Dogs following us.

			A dog is a personal demon. It is a familiar spirit. It is a domestic spirit assigned to follow you.

			I bind any spiritual dog in the name of Jesus!

			Lift your hands and be grateful.





CHAPTER 42




Prayer from Psalm 22




		 			My God, my God, why hast thou forsaken me? why art thou so far from helping me, and from the words of my roaring?

			O my God, I cry in the day time, but thou hearest not; and in the night season, and am not silent.

			But thou art holy, O thou that inhabitest the praises of Israel.

			Our fathers trusted in thee: they trusted, and thou didst deliver them.

			They cried unto thee, and were delivered: they trusted in thee, and were not confounded.

			But I am a worm, and no man; a reproach of men, and despised of the people.

			All they that see me laugh me to scorn: they shoot out the lip, they shake the head, saying,

			He trusted on the Lord that he would deliver him: let him deliver him, seeing he delighted in him.

			But thou art he that took me out of the womb: thou didst make me hope when I was upon my mother’s breasts.

			I was cast upon thee from the womb: thou art my God from my mother’s belly.

			Be not far from me; for trouble is near; for there is none to help.

			Many bulls have compassed me: strong bulls of Bashan have beset me round.

			Psalm 22:1-12



		 			1. 	God inhabits the praises of His people, therefore, thank Him.

			My God, my God, why hast thou forsaken me? why art thou so far from helping me, and from the words of my roaring? O my God, I cry in the day time, but thou hearest not; and in the night season, and am not silent. But thou art holy, O thou that inhabitest the praises of Israel.

			Psalm 22:1-3

			2. 	Pray for deliverance.

			They cried unto thee, AND WERE DELIVERED: they trusted in thee, and were not confounded.

			Psalm 22: 5

			3. 	Pray that God would deliver you from whatever is mocking you and holding you down.

			But I am a worm, and no man; a reproach of men, and despised of the people.

			All they that see me laugh me to scorn: they shoot out the lip, they shake the head, saying,

			He trusted on the Lord that he would deliver him: let him deliver him, seeing he delighted in him.

			Psalm 22:6-8

			A worm is just a weak insect but all your mockers and all those who laugh you to scorn shall be disappointed. God will deliver you from them.

			Lord, deliver us from those that have meetings about us. Lord, deliver us from those that conspire against us!

			4. 	Pray against spiritual animals in your life – e.g. spiritual bulls.

			MANY BULLS HAVE COMPASSED ME: strong bulls of Bashan have beset me round.

			Psalm 22:12

			5.	Bind any spirit that has made itself strong in your life or home, bind it.

			Begin to bind any strongman in your life. Curse whatever represents a strong bull in your life!

			Many bulls have compassed me: STRONG BULLS OF BASHAN have beset me round.

			Psalm 22:12

			Bulls speak of strength. Bulls are known for their brute strength.

			Examples of strong things to overcome:

			 				Sexual perversions

				Fornication

				Alcoholism

				Boyfriends and Girlfriends6.	Bind the power of bulls in ministry.





			Whatever you curse begins to wither.

			We terminate the activities of bull devils, bulls of poverty!

			Every overwhelming power, we curse you and we say, die!

			7.	Pray against spiritual lions.

			They gaped upon me with their mouths, AS A RAVENING AND A ROARING LION.

			Psalm 22:13

			The lions speak of fear. Bind any fear that has crippled you 	and made you weak.

			I curse every lion that brings a lot fear, backbiting, criticism 	and blasphemers!

			I curse whatever has opened its mouth against me that threatens my ministry, future, health and finances!

			I confront you!

			I bind your powers!

			Whatever represents a roaring lion, a ravening lion, we bind you.

			Let the judgement of God be upon you.

			8. 	Pray against spiritual dogs.

			For dogs have compassed me: the assembly of the wicked have inclosed me: they pierced my hands and my feet.

			Psalm 22:16

			There are spiritual dogs. Dogs are spirits on assignment. They are personally assigned to you. They know you; they know your ways, they can smell you.

			I bind the dog spirit of laziness, wretchedness, dogs of pornography and personal quarrels.

			Today we decree and declare and terminate their assignments.

			I block every channel by which the devil has gotten access into my life, my home, my marriage, my finances in the name of Jesus. I demand immediate termination.

			Deliver my soul from the sword; my darling from the power of the dog.

			Save me from the lion’s mouth: for thou hast heard me from the horns of the unicorns.

			Psalm 22:20-21

			Your “darling” is the part of your life you hold dear.

			9. 	Pray against the spiritual unicorns.

			Pray an bind the unicorns.

			Save me from the lion’s mouth: for thou hast heard me from the horns of the unicorns.

			Psalm 22:21

			10.	Pray for great congregations.

			Pray for all churches everywhere to have great congregations, great preaching and great worship.

			MY PRAISE SHALL BE OF THEE IN THE GREAT CONGREGATION: I will pay my vows before them that fear him.

			Psalm 22:25

			11.	Pray to pay your vows.

			Is there any vow you made to God that you didn’t pay? Your tithes? Your offerings? Make a vow to give yourself to the Lord. Let’s give ourselves to the Lord.

			My praise shall be of thee in the great congregation: I WILL PAY MY VOWS before them that fear him.

			Psalm 22:25

			12. 	Pray for the spirit of meekness, humility and lowliness of mind.

			The meek shall eat and be satisfied: they shall praise the Lord that seek him: your heart shall live for ever.

			Psalm 22:26

			The meek will he guide in judgment: and the meek will he teach his way.

			Psalm 25:9

			If no one teaches you anything, if no one guides you, if no one tells you to do anything then you are probably not meek any more.

			The meek shall be satisfied. Humility brings food. Humility gives jobs. The meek shall be satisfied with a husband.

			Pride is a spirit. A haughty spirit goes before a fall. Strong talking is a sign of pride.

			Deliver me from the spirit of pride.

			13. 	Pray for multinational congregations across the United Denominations1.

			All the ends of the world shall remember and turn unto the Lord: and all the kindreds of the nations shall worship before thee.

			Psalm 22:27

			Let’s pray for the ends of the world.

			Pray for 190 nations in the world.

			Pray for churches in all these nations.

			Pray for crusades to be in many more nations in the world.

			The whole world must know about Jesus.

			14. 	Pray for His Kingdom to come.

			For the kingdom is the Lord’s: and he is the governor among the nations.

			Psalm 22:28

			Thy kingdom come, Lord.

			1 United Denominations is a church founded by the author, Bishop Dag Heward-Mills, with over three thousand churches worldwide

			15. 	Pray for preservation.

			All they that be fat upon earth shall eat and worship: all they that go down to the dust shall bow before him: and none can keep alive his own soul.

			Psalm 22:29

			“None can keep alive his own soul.” This means nobody can make themselves stay alive. Staying alive comes from God.

			Lord, keep our souls alive for another 15 years to serve you!

			No evil will be diagnosed in my belly!

			No evil will be diagnosed in my chest!

			No evil will be diagnosed in my head!

			I am declared cancer free in the name of Jesus!

			16. Pray for your children to serve the Lord. Pray for your future generation to serve God.

			Our children will also serve him. Future generations will hear about the wonders of the Lord. His righteous acts will be told to those not yet born. They will hear about everything he has done.

			Psalm 22:30-31 (NLT)

			Please note that it is not automatic that as we are serving the Lord, our seed or children will serve the Lord.

			Pray that your seed shall serve God!

			Our future generations shall serve God!

			We are one of the seeds and therefore, we too shall continue serve God!

			Our children’s children shall serve God!

			Father, thank You that our children shall serve you.

			Our future generations shall serve You.

			The Prophetic Blessings

			Lift your hands and call for supernatural supplies

			Pray for supplies from heaven

			In this economic holocaust, God is the One we have to look up to

			A holocaust is a very terrible thing

			Therefore, believe in the Lord thy God and believe in His Prophets for your financial breakthrough

			Believe and thank Him for the Word that has been spoken over your supplies

			SAY AMEN TO THE PROPHECY OVER YOUR FINANCES IN THE MIDST OF FAMINE

			RETURN, RESTORE, RECOVER

			JESUS, IT IS HAPPENING

			SAY “JESUS I TRUST YOU, I BELIEVE IN YOU”

			LORD, WE SAY AMEN FOR EVERYTHING WE HAVE PRAYED FOR AND PRAYED AGAINST

			SAY AMEN! SAY AMEN!

			God will surely visit you

			Joseph said “God will surely visit.”

			And Joseph took an oath of the children of Israel, saying, GOD WILL SURELY VISIT YOU, and ye shall carry up my bones from hence.

			Genesis 50:25

			After this season, when people see you, they will see Jesus.

			Thank God for a major change in your life.

			It is time to ask God to forgive us our sins.

			Father, forgive me of my sins.

			Thank Him for mercy for the next fifteen years.

			GOD WILL SURELY VISIT YOU!

CHAPTER 43





Prayer from Daniel Chapter 9 & Warfare Prayer from Psalm 110




		 			In the first year of Darius the son of Ahasuerus, of the seed of the Medes, which was made king over the realm of the Chaldeans;

			In the first year of his reign I Daniel understood by books the number of the years, whereof the word of the Lord came to Jeremiah the prophet, that he would accomplish seventy years in the desolations of Jerusalem.

			And I set my face unto the Lord God, to seek by prayer and supplications, with fasting, and sackcloth, and ashes:

			And I prayed unto the Lord my God, and made my confession, and said, O Lord, the great and dreadful God, keeping the covenant and mercy to them that love him, and to them that keep his commandments;

			We have sinned, and have committed iniquity, and have done wickedly, and have rebelled, even by departing from thy precepts and from thy judgments:

			Neither have we hearkened unto thy servants the prophets, which spake in thy name to our kings, our princes, and our fathers, and to all the people of the land.

			O Lord, righteousness belongeth unto thee, but unto us confusion of faces, as at this day; to the men of Judah, and to the inhabitants of Jerusalem, and unto all Israel, that are near, and that are far off, through all the countries whither thou hast driven them, because of their trespass that they have trespassed against thee.

			O Lord, to us belongeth confusion of face, to our kings, to our princes, and to our fathers, because we have sinned against thee.

			To the Lord our God belong mercies and forgivenesses, though we have rebelled against him;

			Neither have we obeyed the voice of the Lord our God, to walk in his laws, which he set before us by his servants the prophets.

			Yea, all Israel have transgressed thy law, even by departing, that they might not obey thy voice; therefore, the curse is poured upon us, and the oath that is written in the law of Moses the servant of God, because we have sinned against him.

			And he hath confirmed his words, which he spake against us, and against our judges that judged us, by bringing upon us a great evil: for under the whole heaven hath not been done as hath been done upon Jerusalem.

			As it is written in the law of Moses, all this evil is come upon us: yet made we not our prayer before the Lordour God, that we might turn from our iniquities, and understand thy truth.

			Therefore, hath the Lord watched upon the evil, and brought it upon us: for the Lord our

			God is righteous in all his works which he doeth: for we obeyed not his voice.

			And now, O Lord our God, that hast brought thy people forth out of the land of Egypt with a mighty hand, and hast gotten thee renown, as at this day; we have sinned, we have done wickedly.

			O Lord, according to all thy righteousness, I beseech thee, let thine anger and thy fury be turned away from thy city Jerusalem, thy holy mountain: because for our sins, and for the iniquities of our fathers, Jerusalem and thy people are become a reproach to all that are about us.

			Now therefore, O our God, hear the prayer of thy servant, and his supplications, and cause thy face to shine upon thy sanctuary that is desolate, for the Lord’s sake.

			O my God, incline thine ear, and hear; open thine eyes, and behold our desolations, and the city which is called by thy name: for we do not present our supplications before thee for our righteousnesses, but for thy great mercies.

			O Lord, hear; O Lord, forgive; O Lord, hearken and do; defer not, for thine own sake, O my God: for thy city and thy people are called by thy name.

			And whiles I was speaking, and praying, and confessing my sin and the sin of my people Israel, and presenting my supplication before the Lord my God for the holy mountain of my God;

			Yea, whiles I was speaking in prayer, even the man Gabriel, whom I had seen in the vision at the beginning, being caused to fly swiftly, touched me about the time of the evening oblation.

			And he informed me, and talked with me, and said, O Daniel, I am now come forth to give thee skill and understanding.

			Daniel 9:1-22



		 			Prayer from Daniel Chapter 9

			1.	Pray and confess your sins.

			And I set my face unto the Lord God, to seek by prayer and supplications, with fasting, and sackcloth, and ashes:

			And I prayed unto the Lord my God, and made my confession, and said, O Lord, the great and dreadful God, keeping the covenant and mercy to them that love him, and to them that keep his commandments;

			Daniel 9: 3-4

			When we come to God, we cannot be pretentious. We have to come to God and confess our sins like Daniel did. We must not be too righteous. Rather we must confess our sins.

			Lift your hands and ask God for mercy.

			One of the sins we have committed is that we have not hearkened to His servants, the Prophets.

			Neither have we hearkened unto thy servants the prophets, which spake in thy name to our kings, our princes, and our fathers, and to all the people of the land.

			Daniel 9:6

			2. 	We confess whatever represents confusion in our lives right now.

			O Lord, righteousness belongeth unto thee, but unto us confusion of faces, as at this day; to the men of Judah, and to the inhabitants of Jerusalem, and unto all Israel, that are near, and that are far off, through all the countries whither thou hast driven them, because of their trespass that they have trespassed against thee.

			O Lord, to us belongeth CONFUSION OF FACE, to our kings, to our princes, and to our fathers, BECAUSE WE HAVE SINNED AGAINST THEE.

			Daniel 9:7-8

			Confusion of faces come when we have not hearkened. Confusion comes when do not listen to the servants, the Prophets.

			What is Confusion?

			Confusion is a situation in which people are uncertain about what to do or are unable to understand something clearly.

			Confusion is the feeling you have when you do not understand what is happening. Confusion is a state in which many things are happening in an uncontrolled or disorderly fashion.

			We must not be afraid to make a U-turn like the prodigal son. This makes us great.

			Father, please take away confusion of faces. Be merciful Lord. Please take away the uncertainty, Lord. I have not listened Lord and therefore, take away the bewilderment.

			Whatever shame or confusion in our lives that have come about for not hearkening to the Prophet is being taken away:

			Confusion in our lives

			Confusion in our marriages

			Confusion in our profession

			Shame in our business

			Shame in our finances

			To the Lord our God belong mercies and forgivenesses, though we have rebelled against him;

			Daniel 9:9

			3. 	Pray for mercies and “forgivenesses”.

			“Forgivenesses” means a lot of forgiveness. Time is mercy. Therefore, now that we are alive, God is being merciful.

			Yea, all Israel have transgressed thy law, even by departing, that they might not obey thy voice; therefore, the curse is poured upon us, and the oath that is written in the law of Moses the servant of God, because we have sinned against him.

			Daniel 9:11

			A curse is poured out for not obeying God. A great evil comes when we do not obey God.

			What has God told you to do personally that you have not done?

			As a church, what has He told us to do that we have not done?

			Lord, whatever you have told ME to do that I have not done, please forgive me.

			Lord, whatever I promised that I will do that I have not done, please forgive me.

			4. 	Pray concerning your church’s work for the Lord.

			Pray that the Lord’s churches would be established on top of the mountains.

			5. 	Pray against curses for not planting churches.

			Curses are unexplainable, repetitive evil.

			And Noah builded an altar unto the Lord; and took of every clean beast, and of every clean fowl, and offered burnt offerings on the altar. And the Lord smelled a sweet savour; and the Lord said in his heart, I will not again curse the ground any more for man’s sake; for the imagination of man’s heart is evil from his youth; neither will I again smite any more every thing living, as I have done.

			Genesis 8:20-21

			No more curse Lord!

			No more stagnation

			No more backwardness

			No more limitations

			Let the curse break, Lord.

			I break the curse of barrenness

			No more curses, no more curses!

			O Lord, according to all thy righteousness, I beseech thee, let thine anger and thy fury be turned away from thy city Jerusalem, thy holy mountain: because for our sins, and for the iniquities of our fathers, Jerusalem and thy people are become a reproach to all that are about us.

			Daniel 9:16

			Father, turn away Your anger and fury from us.

			Turn anger and fury away from us and cause Your face to shine on Your sanctuary.

			6.	Pray for power.

			You see His power and His glory when you pray.

			Yea, whiles I was speaking in prayer, even the man Gabriel, whom I had seen in the vision at the beginning, being caused to fly swiftly, touched me about the time of the evening oblation.

			And he informed me, and talked with me, and said, O Daniel, I am now come forth to give thee skill and understanding.

			Daniel 9:20-22

			While Daniel was praying, power started to come. An angel is a special agent from God.

			To see thy power and thy glory, so as I have seen thee in the sanctuary.

			Psalm 63:2

			Why Do We Want to Pray for Power?

			Thy people shall be willing in the day of thy power, in the beauties of holiness from the womb of the morning: thou hast the dew of thy youth.

			Psalm 110:3

			 				People are willing when they see power.

				Demons are willing to leave when they see power.

				Wickedness is ready to leave when it sees power.

				Power belongs to God.God hath spoken once; twice have I heard this; that POWER BELONGETH UNTO GOD.

Psalm 62:11

O God, thou art my God; early will I seek thee: my soul thirsteth for thee, my flesh longeth for thee in a dry and thirsty land, where no water is;

Psalm 63:1





			Warfare Prayers from Psalm 110

			1.	Pray that your enemies will become your footstool.

			Whatever threatens and attacks you is an enemy.

			The Lord said unto my Lord, Sit thou at my right hand, until I make thine enemies thy footstool.

			Psalm 110:1

			God is reducing your enemies until they become your footstool.

			2. 	God is establishing us to rule in the midst of our enemies.

			The Lord shall send the rod of thy strength out of Zion: rule thou in the midst of thine enemies.

			Psalm 110:2

			3. 	Bind any personal devils.

			They are called “familiar” spirits. Sometimes it is a sexual thing, depression, a divorce-prone woman or man, a quarrel, family curses, unwillingness to forgive, poverty.

			Thy people shall be willing in the day of thy power, in the beauties of holiness from the womb of the morning: thou hast the dew of thy youth.

			Psalm 110:3

			Whatever is a personal devil, bind it now!

			Cast it out of your life!

			Bind personal and familiar spirits!

			Rebuke any messenger of satan!

			I bind notorious familiar spirits!

			By the blood of Jesus, your assignments have ended!

			I declare your work has ended in my life, in my home, on my children’s life!

CHAPTER 45




Prayer Changes Things




		 			In those days was Hezekiah sick unto death. And Isaiah the prophet the son of Amoz came unto him, and said unto him, Thus saith the Lord, Set thine house in order: for thou shalt die, and not live.

			Then Hezekiah turned his face toward the wall, and prayed unto the Lord,

			And said, Remember now, O Lord, I beseech thee, how I have walked before thee in truth and with a perfect heart, and have done that which is good in thy sight. And Hezekiah wept sore.

			Then came the word of the Lord to Isaiah, saying,

			Go, and say to Hezekiah, Thus saith the Lord, the God of David thy father, I have heard thy prayer, I have seen thy tears: behold, I will add unto thy days fifteen years.

			Isaiah 38:1-5

		 			1. 	Pray and confess your sins.

			Neither let us commit fornication, as some of them committed, and fell in one day three and twenty thousand.

			Neither let us tempt Christ, as some of them also tempted, and were destroyed of serpents.

			Neither murmur ye, as some of them also murmured, and were destroyed of the destroyer.

			1 Corinthians 10:8-10

			Father forgive us, for lusting after things, for lusting after desires.

			We cast aside the spirit of complaining, murmuring and disloyalty

			We cast aside evil relationships, evil friendships.

			We change our garments, Lord.

			In those days was Hezekiah sick unto death. And Isaiah the prophet the son of Amoz came unto him, and said unto him, Thus saith the Lord, Set thine house in order: for thou shalt die, and not live.

			Then Hezekiah turned his face toward the wall, and prayed unto the Lord,

			And said, Remember now, O Lord, I beseech thee, how I have walked before thee in truth and with a perfect heart, and have done that which is good in thy sight. And Hezekiah wept sore.

			i.	Prayer changes destinies. Prayer changed what had been predicted and prophesied about King Hezekiah. Hezekiah’s death was determined, prophesied, and predicted. Prayer changes things are predicted, determined, prophesied against you.

			ii.	We must be able to talk to God like we talk to our friends. Our prayers must be like how we talk to somebody.

			iii.	Prayer can be non-verbal. We must weep as Hezekiah wept. The non-verbal prayer of King Hezekiah as he wept, moved God.

			Prayer changes things. Whatever negative prediction is over our lives is changing now.

			Prayer changes destinies. Pray and commit your destiny to God.

			As you pray, that negative thing that has been said about you is changing now.

			2. 	Pray that you shall be transformed into a good soldier.

			Pray that we will endure hardness and become good soldiers like Jesus Christ.

			Thou therefore, endure hardness, as a good soldier of Jesus Christ.

			2 Timothy 2:3

			Father take away the softness that makes me unable to follow You.

			Father take away the softness that makes me unable to be a hard Christian.

			Please take away any spirit of softness.

			The Lord is a Man of War

			The Lord is a man of war: the Lord is his name.

			Exodus 15:3

			Every negative characteristic that is not allowing you to become a good soldier is being removed now in the name of Jesus! Your countenance is being changed into a man of war, a good soldier, enduring hardness for God!

			3. 	Pray that you shall be transformed into an athlete.

			And if anyone enters competitive games, he is not crowned unless he competes lawfully (fairly, according to the rules laid down).

			2 Timothy 2:5 (AMPC)

			And athletes cannot win the prize unless they follow the rules.

			2 Timothy 2:5 (NLT)

			What do Athletes Do?

			Athletes practice all the time. If your pulse rate is between 40 to 50, then you are not an athlete. Athletes are disciplined, they cannot do comfortable things, otherwise they cannot win. Athletes have muscles. Athletes sweat.

			Prayer Changes Things!

			Father make me a man of speed, a man of stamina, a man of strength to run marathons in Your kingdom in the name of Jesus! Father, make me an athlete for Your kingdom!

			And being in an agony he prayed more earnestly: and HIS SWEAT was as it were great drops of blood falling down to the ground.

			Every net that has been laid for you!

			Every pit that has been dug for you!

			Every trap that has been set for you!

			4.	The next transformation is to change into a farmer.

			[It is] the hard-working farmer [who labors to produce] who must be the first partaker of the fruits.

			2 Timothy 2:6 (AMPC)

			In those days was Hezekiah sick unto death. And the prophet Isaiah the son of Amoz came to him, and said unto him, Thus saith the Lord, Set thine house in order; for thou shalt die, and not live.

			It may be the Lord thy God will hear all the words of Rabshakeh, whom the king of Assyria his master hath sent to reproach the living God; and will reprove the words which the Lord thy God hath heard: wherefore lift up thy prayer for the remnant that are left.

			Because every time there is prayer, there are angels

			190 nations

			2,000 Apostles

			We pray for labourers, we pray for shepherds.

			 				Father give us:



			Jewish pastors

			Egyptian pastors

			Moroccan pastors

			Japanese pastors.

			Please bring the gospel to every corner of the world.

			Let Your wind blow.

			9. 	Pray for the Spirit of power and love.

			We need the power of God!

			We need the love of God!

			And we need a right thinking, well-balanced mind to be able to go to all nations.

			The Prophetic Blessings

			Prayer changes things

			Thank God that prayer has changed everything

			Prayer has done it

			Whatever we lack, His power has changed it

			Prayer has changed who we are

			Thank You Lord.

			Amen.





CHAPTER 46




Prayer for God to Make You a Watchman; Prayer for the Holy Spirit & Prayer to Bind Demons




		 			The burden of Dumah. He calleth to me out of Seir, Watchman, what of the night? Watchman, what of the night?

			The watchman said, The morning cometh, and also the night: if ye will enquire, enquire ye: return, come.

			Isaiah 21:11-12



		 			Prayer to Become a Watchman

			1. 	Pray that the Lord will make you a true watchman.

			The burden of Dumah. He calleth to me out of Seir, Watchman, what of the night? Watchman, what of the night?

			The watchman said, The morning cometh, and also the night: if ye will enquire, enquire ye: return, come.

			Isaiah 21:11-12

			2. 	Pray concerning your ministry as a watchman.

			Pray for your watchman ministry and your prophetic ministry; that your eyes and ears may be opened.

			Son of man, I have made thee a watchman unto the house of Israel: therefore, hear the word at my mouth, and give them warning from me.

			Ezekiel 3:17

			Son of man, speak to the children of thy people, and say unto them, When I bring the sword upon a land, if the people of the land take a man of their coasts, and set him for their watchman:

			If when he seeth the sword come upon the land, he blow the trumpet, and warn the people;

			Then whosoever heareth the sound of the trumpet, and taketh not warning; if the sword come, and take him away, his blood shall be upon his own head.

			He heard the sound of the trumpet, and took not warning; his blood shall be upon him. But he that taketh warning shall deliver his soul.

			But if the watchman see the sword come, and blow not the trumpet, and the people be not warned; if the sword come, and take any person from among them, he is taken away in his iniquity; but his blood will I require at the watchman’s hand.

			Ezekiel 33:2-6

			A watchman is to do two things:

			i.	To be Awake (to see and hear)

			ii.	To Warn (yourself and others)

			Who is blind, but my servant? or deaf, as my messenger that I sent? who is blind as he that is perfect, and blind as the Lord’s servant?

			Seeing many things, but thou observest not; opening the ears, but he heareth not.

			Isaiah 42:19-20

			A Christian must not be blind. You must be very observant.

			Any unusual thing that you notice means there’s something unusual happening.

			Your eyes and ears are opening now!

			The Holy Spirit is speaking and showing you things. You are prophetic!

			Thus saith the Lord God; It shall also come to pass, that at the same time shall things come into thy mind, and thou shalt think an evil thought:

			Ezekiel 38:10

			But ye have an unction from the Holy One, and ye know all things.

			1 John 2:20

			Respect thoughts. They are not neutral things. They are spirits.

			This wisdom have I seen also under the sun, and it seemed great unto me:

			There was a little city, and few men within it; and there came a great king against it, and besieged it, and built great bulwarks against it:

			Now there was found in it a poor wise man, and he by his wisdom delivered the city; yet no man remembered that same poor man.

			Then said I, Wisdom is better than strength: nevertheless the poor man’s wisdom is despised, and his words are not heard.

			Ecclesiastes 9:13-16

			Prayer for the Holy Spirit

			1.	Pray for godly wisdom concerning your finances, relationships and ministry.

			Wisdom can make you escape.

			If the iron be blunt, and he do not whet the edge, then must he put to more strength: but wisdom is profitable to direct.

			Ecclesiastes 10:10

			Wisdom is very important. Behave wisely according to godly wisdom!

			Then said I, Wisdom is better than strength: nevertheless the poor man’s wisdom is despised, and his words are not heard.

			Ecclesiastes 9:16

			And the spirit of the Lord shall rest upon him, the spirit of wisdom and understanding, the spirit of counsel and might, the spirit of knowledge and of the fear of the Lord;

			And shall make him of quick understanding in the fear of the Lord: and he shall not judge after the sight of his eyes, neither reprove after the hearing of his ears:

			Isaiah 11:2-3

			We need good advice for our lives. You are the “head of state” of your life. You don’t know everything about everything. You need counsel.

			2. 	Pray for quick understanding. You will understand things quickly.

			Almost everything to do with the Holy Spirit has to do with how you think. Your whole life is based on how you think.

			Receive a softened heart that understands quickly.

			Be ye not as the horse, or as the mule, which have no understanding: whose mouth must be held in with bit and bridle, lest they come near unto thee.

			Psalm 32: 9

			Every spirit of a horse or mule that causes you not to understand is going out in Jesus’ name!

			Pray for good sense, forward thinking and quick understanding. Pray for the Holy Spirit!

			But he that prophesieth speaketh unto men to edification, and exhortation, and comfort.

			1 Corinthians 14:3

			3. 	Pray for the Holy Spirit.

			He giveth power to the faint; and to them that have no might he increaseth strength. Even the youths shall faint and be weary, and the young men shall utterly fall: But they that wait upon the Lord shall renew their strength; they shall mount up with wings as eagles; they shall run, and not be weary; and they shall walk, and not faint.

			Isaiah 40:29-31

			Seven Things that Happen When You Wait on God

			i.	When you wait on God, your strength is renewed. Your strength and power are increased as you wait on God.

			ii.	When you wait on God it increases your altitude (your level), you keep going up.

			iii.	When you wait on God, it makes you sensitive to the Holy Spirit.

			iv.	When you wait on God it increases your speed (in life and ministry).

			v.	When you wait on God it increases your stamina.

			vi.	When you wait on God it increases your walk and fellowship with God and godly people.

			vii.	When you wait on God it increases your freshness.

			Thank God for the seven things happening to you as you wait on Him.

			4.	Pray for the Spirit of humility. Pray for the Spirit of lowliness. It is the master key out of every siege.

			Humility is invisible. Usually if you think you’re humble, you’re actually not. Humility is a spirit.

			Be clothed with humility!

			Pride goeth before destruction, and an haughty spirit before a fall.

			Proverbs 16:18

			Things don’t just easily work out. There are a lot of unexplained evil and wickedness in life. Examples are:

			 				Poverty

				Difficulties

				Marital challenges

				Chronic singleness

				Stagnation in life

				Starting something and not finishing it



			Please exempt us

			Please exclude us

			Show us mercy Lord

			Our fathers have sinned

			Our mothers have sinned

			Please don’t let us bear their iniquities

			May God deliver you from any puffed up spirit in you. Anything that makes you feel and think of yourself bigger or higher than you are!

			Pray to be very meek! The anointing of Moses is upon your life!

			5.	Pray for the Spirit of divine guidance and direction!

			Pray for the Spirit of counsel!

			And the Lord said unto Moses, Stretch out thine hand toward heaven, that there may be darkness over the land of Egypt, even darkness which may be felt.

			And Moses stretched forth his hand toward heaven; and there was a thick darkness in all the land of Egypt three days:

			They saw not one another, neither rose any from his place for three days: but all the children of Israel had light in their dwellings.

			Exodus 10:21-23

			People don’t benefit from the Passover Lamb (Christ and salvation) because after taking the blood, they still stay in Egypt (the world).

			You will have light in your life by the Spirit of counsel.

			Cry aloud, spare not, lift up thy voice like a trumpet, and shew my people their transgression, and the house of Jacob their sins…

			And THE LORD SHALL GUIDE THEE CONTINUALLY, and satisfy thy soul in drought, and make fat thy bones: and thou shalt be like a watered garden, and like a spring of water, whose waters fail not.

			Isaiah 58:1, 11

			God will guide you continually (before, during and after the drought and crises).

			The meek will he guide in judgment: and the meek will he teach his way.

			Psalm 25:9

			God doesn’t guide everybody. He guides the meek. If you’re not humble, you can’t be guided. Lack of humility makes you unguidable.

			Pray for the Holy Spirit! The Spirit of continual guidance!

			Prayer to Bind Demons

			1. 	Take up a lamentation against the devil in your life!

			Son of man, take up a lamentation upon the king of Tyrus, and say unto him, Thus saith the Lord God; Thou sealest up the sum, full of wisdom, and perfect in beauty.

			Ezekiel 28:12

			Thou brakest the heads of leviathan in pieces, and gavest him to be meat to the people inhabiting the wilderness.

			Thou didst cleave the fountain and the flood: thou driedst up mighty rivers.

			The day is thine, the night also is thine: thou hast prepared the light and the sun.

			Thou hast set all the borders of the earth: thou hast made summer and winter.

			Remember this, that the enemy hath reproached, O Lord, and that the foolish people have blasphemed thy name.

			O deliver not the soul of thy turtledove unto the multitude of the wicked: forget not the congregation of thy poor for ever.

			Have respect unto the covenant: for the dark places of the earth are full of the habitations of cruelty.

			Psalm 74:14 - 20

			2.	Everything that harasses you, stops and finishes now.

			Bind every messenger of satan sent to your life now!

			Whatever is a messenger and has come to stay with you can no longer stay with you in Jesus’ name!

			Bind whatever represents stress and buffeting in your life. It’s ending now!

			Every treatment you don’t like in your life is from the devil

			Bind every evil meeting and conspiracy against your life!

			Bind every strike of evil against you!

			Pray for a counterstrike to end the conflict and battle against your top enemy!

			Bind every spirit of demotion and humiliation!

			O God, why hast thou cast us off for ever? why doth thine anger smoke against the sheep of thy pasture?

			Remember thy congregation, which thou hast purchased of old; the rod of thine inheritance, which thou hast redeemed; this mount Zion, wherein thou hast dwelt.

			Lift up thy feet unto the perpetual desolations; even all that the enemy hath done wickedly in the sanctuary…

			They said in their hearts, Let us destroy them together: they have burned up all the synagogues of God in the land.

			Psalm 74:1-3, 8

			3.	Whatever the enemy has done wickedly in your life and in the church is neutralized!

			Every spirit of destruction spoiling your life, love, church, relationships is neutralized!

			Bind every destruction and wickedness!

			Break the head of leviathan (serpents, vipers, marine spirits, etc.)

			Bind the spirit of death.

			Bind every spirit that is dwelling with you physically! Cast it out!

			Ask for the head of every serpent in your life!

			Every authority of evil over your life is destroyed now!

			Break the head of every serpent in your life!

			Take up a funeral lamentation against your enemy!

			Your enemy is finished and destroyed!

			Declare the funeral of your enemies!

			Whatever plan that is working against you is not working again in Jesus’ name!

			Communion Prayers

			The Bread

			Father, we present this holy bread before You

			We say now this is the body of our Saviour Jesus Christ

			Thank You, Lord, for Your body which was broken for us

			By His stripes we are healed

			We receive healing and long life as we receive this holy communion today - the body of Jesus

			As this body enters you

			Whatever could not be in Jesus cannot be in you

			Whatever disease could not be in Jesus cannot be in you

			A virus that could not be in Jesus cannot be in you

			Every virus dies

			Every bacteria dies

			Every sickness ends

			Every pestilence ends

			Every bad news comes to a conclusion in Jesus’ name

			You are set free

			You are healed, covered, and helped

			By the body of our Saviour Jesus Christ. (Eat It)

			The Blood

			By one man sin entered

			By one man sin has been removed - my mistake, my father’s mistake, my mother’s mistakes

			May their sin, their iniquities, and their transgressions

			Which are passed on to my generation and my children’s generation, be neutralized by this cup, the cup of blessing

			Let the curse that has operated in every family, be neutralized by this blood

			Let the sins that I have come into the world to perform

			And my iniquities and transgressions

			Be washed today and neutralized, and their power be nullified

			By the blood of the Lamb that taketh away the sins of the world

			May I be restored to God and recover from the effects of my sins, transgressions, and iniquities

			May my sins not be recounted to me anymore

			May my mistakes not be mentioned again

			Any demonic record that are accessed by devils and used against me, are neutralized and wiped away by this powerful blood

			The blood that washes away and makes me white as wool

			I receive this cup of blessing and the blood of Jesus. (Drink It)

			The Prophetic Blessing

			May the Passover eradicate what was supposed to stop in your house and stay to bring sadness and destruction

			It moves on out of your house NOW

			In the name of Jesus Christ!

			What visited you cannot stay, it must go!

			Whatever evil came to you cannot stay on

			You are cleansed, forgiven, and restored

			May the visitation of darkness end and be replaced by the visitation of God

			Receive healing, receive blessing, receive restoration, and health from above

			These are the days of Elijah

			Days of supernatural involvement with the affairs of our life.

			Amen.
